  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将3D引力嵌入拓扑弦理论是重要的理论目标。此前工作仅建立了两类理论在运动方程层面的联系，本文旨在经典作用量层面显式完成维度约化，为未来将3D引力欧几里得路径积分嵌入拓扑弦理论铺平道路。

Method: 作者提出了一种新的改写方法：将非局域的6D Kodaira-Spencer作用量在特定复结构形变子扇区内重写为局域的6D全纯Chern-Simons作用量，规范群为SL(2,ℂ)。在此框架下显式执行维度约化，获得3D手性引力的作用量，并用两个独立复共轭拷贝恢复完整的3D Einstein作用量。

Result: 成功在经典作用量层面证明了6D Kodaira-Spencer引力的维度约化包含3D手性引力子扇区，且两个独立复共轭拷贝的组合给出完整3D Einstein-Hilbert作用量，将之前仅在运动方程层面的对应推广到了作用量层面。

Conclusion: 该工作为3D引力嵌入拓扑弦理论提供了关键一步，建立了从6D扭曲全息理论到3D引力经典作用量的显式对应关系，有望将3D引力的欧几里得路径积分纳入拓扑弦理论的框架。

Abstract: In view of embedding 3D gravity into topological string theory, we show that the dimensional reduction of 6D Kodaira-Spencer gravity on $\text{AdS}_3\times S^3$, i.e. the low-energy description of twisted holography, contains a subsector that coincides with the action of chiral 3D gravity on $\text{AdS}_3$. Furthermore, we show that the full 3D Einstein action is obtained as the reduction of two independent complex-conjugate copies of 6D Kodaira-Spencer gravity. We perform the dimensional reduction explicitly at the level of the classical actions, extending our previous work that related the equations of motion of the two theories. Our reduction procedure is based on a novel rewriting of the non-local 6D Kodaira-Spencer action as a local 6D holomorphic Chern-Simons action for the gauge group $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{C})$, valid within a subsector of complex structure deformations that we identify. Our results provide a necessary step toward embedding the Euclidean path integral of 3D gravity into topological string theory.

</details>


### [97] [The EEC-Hedron: Positivity Bounds on Energy Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19322)
*Bianka Meçaj,Ian Moult,Hidde Stoffels,Matthew T. Walters,Yuan Xin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一般幺正量子场论中能量正定性所施加的约束，证明能量-能量关联函数的分波展开系数必须位于一个有界参数区域内（称为"EEC-hedron"），并利用这些正定性约束对3d Ising和O(2)共形场论中的OPE系数推出了新的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子场论中能量正定性这一基本物理要求所带来的数学约束，以及这些约束如何转化为对共形场论中OPE系数等关键参数的限制。

Method: 通过研究一般幺正量子场论中能量-能量关联函数的分波展开，利用能量正定性条件限制展开系数的允许范围，构造"EEC-hedron"有界参数区域；对CFT情形，将这些正定性约束映射为对OPE系数的边界条件，并在3d Ising和O(2)模型中进行具体应用。

Result: 证明能量-能量关联函数的分波展开系数约束在一个有界区域内（EEC-hedron），并在3d Ising和O(2) CFTs中导出了新的OPE系数上界/边界。

Conclusion: 能量正定性为量子场论提供了新的非平凡约束，可作为研究CFT中OPE系数的有力工具，能够在具体理论（如3d Ising和O(2)模型）中获得此前未知的定量边界。

Abstract: We study the constraints placed by energy positivity on general unitary quantum field theories. We show that the coefficients in the partial wave expansion of energy-energy correlators all must lie within a bounded region of parameter space, which we call the ``EEC-hedron''. For the particular case of conformal field theories, these positivity constraints on energy correlators lead to bounds on the allowed values of coefficients in the operator product expansion. We demonstrate this approach in the 3d Ising and $O(2)$ CFTs, deriving new bounds on OPE coefficients in both theories.

</details>


### [98] [Supergroup Gauged Linear Sigma Models and their Physical Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19775)
*Arif Er,Zhangcheng Liu,Meng-Chwan Tan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了具有U(1|1)^N超规范群的二维N=(2,2)超对称规范线性sigma模型（可能含超势），并利用其超对称态空间研究数学应用，建立了超格拉斯曼流形上的Calabi-Yau/NLSM与超规范Landau-Ginzburg orbifold之间的对应关系，推广了Clader、Zhao和Fan-Jarvis-Ruan的Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg对应，同时发现了超格拉斯曼流形上的Calabi-Yau超向量丛的Atiyah型翻转变换，以及超格拉斯曼流形上二次型Calabi-Yau完全交的conifold转变，推广了Kuznetsov-Perry的齐次射影对偶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管具有非酉（nonunitary）性质的U(1|1)^N超规范群在物理上不满足传统酉性要求，但研究者仍希望通过研究其超对称态空间，探索其在纯数学中的应用，特别是推广已有的Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg对应及相关的代数几何结果到超格拉斯曼流形与超群框架下。

Method: 构造2d N=(2,2)超对称规范线性sigma模型（GLSM），其规范群为超群U(1|1)^N，可能包含超势。通过分析模型的超对称态空间（supersymmetric states），建立以下对应关系：(1) 超格拉斯曼流形上Calabi-Yau完全交的非线性sigma模型与超规范Landau-Ginzburg orbifold之间的对应；(2) 超格拉斯曼流形乘积空间上Calabi-Yau超曲面的NLSM与混合NLSM/超规范Landau-Ginzburg orbifold之间的对应；(3) 超格拉斯曼流形上Calabi-Yau超向量丛的Atiyah型拓扑转变；(4) 超格拉斯曼流形上二次型Calabi-Yau完全交的conifold型拓扑转变。

Result: 实现了四类数学结果的超格拉斯曼/超群推广：1) 推广Clader和Zhao的超格拉斯曼完全交Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg对应；2) 推广Fan-Jarvis-Ruan的乘积空间中超曲面的混合Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg对应；3) 推广Atiyah型翻转变换的Calabi-Yau超向量丛birational等价；4) 推广Kuznetsov-Perry的Calabi-Yau二次型的齐次射影对偶。所有这些物理结果在退化到普通格拉斯曼流形时均可还原为已知的数学结论。

Conclusion: 尽管超gauged模型是非酉的，其物理结构仍可产生深刻的数学对应关系。本文建立了超格拉斯曼流形与超群框架下的Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg对应、混合对应、Atiyah翻转变换以及conifold转变/齐次射影对偶，为超几何代数几何与超对称量子场论之间的交叉提供了新的桥梁。

Abstract: We construct 2d $\mathcal{N}=(2,2)$ gauged linear sigma models with $\mathrm{U}(1|1)^N$ supergauge group possibly with superpotential. Despite being nonunitary, one can still study their space of supersymmetric states and explore their applications to mathematics. In particular, we find a relation between a nonlinear sigma model on a Calabi-Yau complete intersection of hypersurfaces in a super-Grassmannian and a supergauged Landau-Ginzburg orbifold, which can reduce to a regular Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg correspondence for complete intersections. This defines a super-Grassmannian/supergroup generalization of the correspondence proved by Clader [1] and Zhao [2]. Similarly, we find a relation between a nonlinear sigma model on a Calabi-Yau hypersurface in a product of super-Grassmannians and a hybrid NLSM/supergauged Landau-Ginzburg orbifold, which can reduce to a regular hybrid Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg correspondence for hypersurfaces in product space. This defines a super-Grassmannian/supergroup generalization of the correspondence proved by Fan-Jarvis-Ruan [3]. We also find that Calabi-Yau supervector bundles over a super-Grassmannian can undergo a physically related mild topology change which is reducible to a regular Atiyah-type flop transition. This defines a super-Grassmannian generalization of a birational equivalence of Calabi-Yau vector bundles in mathematics. Similarly, we find that a Calabi-Yau complete intersection of quadrics in a super-Grassmannian can also undergo a physically related topology change which is reducible to a regular conifold transition. This defines a super-Grassmannian generalization of a homological projective duality for Calabi-Yau quadrics by Kuznetsov-Perry [4] in mathematics.

</details>


### [99] [Inflationary Bispectra and IR Physics from Quantum Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19327)
*Matthias Thomas Nowinski,Ivo Sachs*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体作为量子模拟器，将早期宇宙粒子产生的类比扩展到相互作用理论，计算了3点关联函数的领先相互作用项，并应用于膨胀和周期性振荡的模型宇宙，发现周期性驱动系统产生随时间线性增长的共振关联子，易于实验检测，可直接用于研究德西特空间中无质量标量场的晚时红外发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体已被成功用作早期宇宙粒子产生的量子模拟器，但此前的工作仅局限于自由理论。为了更真实地模拟宇宙中的相互作用过程，需要将这一类比扩展到相互作用理论，从而研究3点关联函数等更高阶的量子关联，并探索其在宇宙学相关现象（如红外发散）研究中的应用。

Method: 作者首先计算了通用实验装置中预期的领先相互作用项，这些项决定了3点关联函数。然后将这些结果应用于两类模型宇宙：一是宇宙学动机驱动的膨胀型尺度因子模型，二是周期性振荡型模型。对周期性驱动系统，通过共振条件分析得到了随时间线性增长的相关函数。

Result: 膨胀型尺度因子由于实验上可实现的尺度因子受约束，不一定能产生易于观测的签名；而周期性驱动的系统会产生共振的、随时间线性增长的3点关联子，非常适合实验检测。该方法可作为研究德西特空间中无质量标量场晚时红外发散问题的直接实验手段。

Conclusion: 周期性驱动的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体提供了一个可行且敏感的实验平台，用于研究相互作用理论中的宇宙学粒子产生和量子关联，特别是为实验探索德西特空间中红外发散这一理论难题提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Bose-Einstein condensates have been used successfully as quantum simulators for early-universe particle production. Here we extend this analogy to the interacting theory. To this end, we compute the leading interaction terms expected in a general experimental setup that determine the 3-point correlations. We apply them to cosmologically motivated expanding model universes, but also periodically oscillating ones. While expanding scale factors do not necessarily lead to easily observable signatures due to experimental constraints on the realizable scale factor, periodically driven ones produce resonant correlators that grow linearly over time and lend themselves well to experimental detection. One direct application of this analysis is the experimental study of late-time infrared divergencies often discussed in the context of massless scalars in de Sitter space.

</details>


### [100] [Deep neural networks as lattice gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19331)
*Ro Jefferson,Shradha Ramakrishnan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将NN/QFT对偶进行修改，引入网络的逐层排列对称性，得到(0+1)维格点规范理论。在此框架下计算了树级神经元传播子，并发展了1/N微扰展开下的费曼图机制，给出了O(1)阶所有传播子修正的递推表达式（代表网络系综中的统计涨落），并初步分析了按1/N逐阶贡献的神经元散射振幅，为研究深度网络中的高阶关联和信息传播提供了场论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的NN/QFT对偶没有考虑神经网络中层与层之间的置换对称性，这限制了对网络系综统计性质的完整刻画。作者希望通过引入这种对称性，建立一个更自然的场论框架来研究深度网络中的信息传播、相关性以及统计涨落。

Method: 将NN/QFT对偶修改为(0+1)维格点规范理论：每层N个神经元作为N分量格点，权重矩阵作为连接link上的规范场。计算树级神经元-神经元传播子以描述层方差的演化，发展1/N微扰展开中的费曼图机制，获得O(1)阶精确传播子所有修正的递推表达式（含无穷多个来自前层的loop图），并进行神经元散射振幅的初步分析。

Result: 得到了O(1)阶所有传播子修正的递推表达式，代表网络系综中的统计涨落，包括无穷多个介导前层相互作用的loop图；给出了神经元散射振幅的逐阶(1/N)分析，为研究高阶点关联和信息传播提供了场论工具。

Conclusion: 该工作为深度网络与量子场论的交叉提供了新方向，建立的格点规范理论框架为研究网络统计性质、信息传播以及更高阶相关性奠定了理论基础，并指出了若干未来研究方向的展望。

Abstract: We modify the NN/QFT duality [1] to incorporate the layerwise permutation symmetry of the network, resulting in a $(0\!+\!1)$-dimensional lattice gauge theory, in which each layer of $N$ neurons acts as an $N$-component lattice site, and the weight matrices play the role of gauge fields living on the links. In this framework, we compute the tree-level neuron-neuron propagator which describes the evolution of layer variance in the network, and develop the Feynman diagram machinery to compute interactions in the perturbative expansion in $1/N$. In particular, we obtain a recursive expression for all corrections to the exact propagator at $O(1)$, representing statistical fluctuations in the ensemble of networks, including infinitely-many loop diagrams mediating the interactions from previous layers. We also present a preliminary analysis of neuron scattering amplitudes that contribute order-by-order in $1/N$, which provides a field-theoretic framework for studying higher-point correlations, and by extension information propagation, in deep networks. We remark on some interesting directions for future work at the intersection of neural networks and quantum field theory.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum chaos and late-time equipartition of symmetry-resolved Krylov complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19346)
*Jayashish Das,Suman Das,Juan F. Pedraza,Le-Chen Qu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限维混沌量子多体系统中对称性分辨的Krylov复杂度。当哈密顿量和初始算符都守恒一个电荷时，算符动力学分解为独立的对称性扇区。饱和后，未分辨的Krylov复杂度在扇区上满足可加性；若无额外Liouvillian简并，某扇区的晚期贡献由d_q(d_q-1)控制，导致维度加权的均分，渐近于d_q^2/∑d_q'^2的大扇区标度。该晚期规则不同于文献中讨论的早期加权平均，而是由可及算符空间的维度控制。作者通过实部和复部SYK模型、混沌玻色自旋模型及混合场Ising链的数值研究验证了该解析预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov复杂度是量化量子混沌算符增长的重要诊断工具，但此前的研究通常忽略了守恒对称性对Krylov复杂度饱和行为的影响。作者希望阐明对称性分辨如何改变Krylov复杂度的饱和值，从而正确解读其作为混沌算符增长的诊断指标。

Method: 作者从理论上推导了对称性分辨下Krylov复杂度的饱和行为，证明其可加性结构，并给出由扇区希尔伯特空间维度d_q控制的晚期标度律。通过数值模拟对实部和复部SYK模型、混沌玻色自旋模型以及混合场Ising链进行验证。

Result: 理论预测显示，晚期Krylov复杂度的扇区贡献由d_q(d_q-1)控制，维度加权均分趋近于d_q^2/∑d_q'^2的标度。该规律与早期加权平均不同，由可及算符空间维度决定。数值结果与解析预测一致。

Conclusion: 解析精确对称性对解释Krylov复杂度饱和值至关重要，若忽视对称性，将无法正确使用Krylov复杂度作为混沌算符增长的诊断工具。对称性分辨的Krylov复杂度揭示了更精细的量子混沌结构。

Abstract: We study symmetry-resolved Krylov complexity in finite-dimensional chaotic quantum many-body systems. When both the Hamiltonian and the initial operator commute with a conserved charge, the operator dynamics decomposes into independent symmetry sectors, each with its own Krylov chain. We show that, after saturation, the unresolved Krylov complexity is additive over symmetry sectors. In the absence of additional Liouvillian degeneracies, the late-time contribution of a sector with Hilbert-space dimension $d_q$ is controlled by $d_q(d_q-1)$, leading to a dimension-weighted equipartition that approaches the simple large-sector scaling $d_q^2/\sum_{q'}d_{q'}^2$. This late-time rule differs from the early-time weighted-average discussed in the literature and is governed instead by the dimensions of the accessible operator spaces. We support the analytic prediction with numerical studies of the real and complex SYK models, a chaotic bosonic spin model, and the mixed-field Ising chain. Our results show that resolving exact symmetries is essential for interpreting the saturation value of Krylov complexity as a diagnostic of chaotic operator growth.

</details>


### [102] [Quantum effects of charged massive scalar fields on charged black hole space-times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19359)
*Cormac Breen,George Montagnon,Peter Taylor,Elizabeth Winstanley*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞背景下大质量带电标量场的重整化期望值（标量凝聚、电荷流和应力-能量张量），覆盖Boulware、Unruh和Hartle-Hawking三种量子态，并利用高效数值方法探索了包括近极端电荷和接近超辐射极限的大范围参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞背景下，计算量子场论中的重整化期望值（如应力-能量张量）对于理解黑洞热力学、霍金辐射及半经典引力具有重要意义。此前的研究受限于数值方法的效率，难以覆盖大参数空间，尤其是近极端黑洞和接近超辐射极限的标量场质量区域。

Method: 采用两项关键技术：1) 对Hartle-Hawking态使用高效且精确的模态求和（mode sum）处方计算重整化期望值；2) 对Boulware和Unruh态采用态减法（state subtraction）方法。通过选取足够大的标量场质量以保证不存在经典超辐射，从而简化物理图景。

Result: 成功在Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞背景下计算了三种量子态下的标量凝聚、电荷流和应力-能量张量的重整化期望值，并高效探索了大参数空间，包括近极端黑洞电荷和接近超辐射极限的标量场质量。

Conclusion: 开发的数值方法在效率上足够优越，使得能够探索广大的参数空间，这为理解带电黑洞背景下的量子场论效应（如霍金辐射和半经典反作用）提供了更全面的视角。

Abstract: We compute the renormalized expectation values of the scalar condensate, charge current and stress-energy tensor for a massive charged quantum scalar field on a Reissner-Nordstrom black hole. The mass of the scalar field is sufficiently large that classical superradiance is absent. We present results for the Boulware, Unruh and Hartle-Hawking states. Renormalized expectation values in the Hartle-Hawking state are computed using an efficient and accurate mode sum prescription, and for the other two states we employ state subtraction. The efficiency of our numerical method enables us to explore a large part of the parameter space, including near-extremal black hole charges and scalar field masses close to the superradiant limit.

</details>


### [103] [Higher Chern--Simons Theory in $2n+2$ Dimensions for Balanced 2-term $L_\infty$-Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19539)
*Danhua Song,Yibo Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种与平衡2-term L∞-代数相关联的2n+2维半严格高阶Chern-Simons（HCS）规范理论，并建立了更高阶的Chern-Weil定理及扩展的Cartan同伦公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高阶规范理论的研究主要基于L∞-代数框架，但如何系统地构造高阶Chern-Simons形式、推广Chern-Weil定理以及统一更高阶的transgression结构，仍缺乏系统的半严格（semistrict）框架。

Method: 从同伦Maurer-Cartan理论出发，引入2-term L∞-代数规范理论并展示四维HCS构造；将不变双线性配对推广到适当次数的不变多重线性形式，定义闭且规范不变的(2n+3)维高阶Pontryagin-Chern形式；通过transgression得到(2n+2)维HCS形式；建立更高阶Chern-Weil定理；应用扩展的Cartan同伦公式作为共同起源。

Result: 成功构造了半严格2n+2维HCS规范理论，证明了高阶Chern-Weil定理生成更高阶transgression形式，并证明HCS理论是高阶transgression规范理论的一个特例；同时证明扩展的Cartan同伦公式统一了高阶Chern-Weil定理与三角形方程。

Conclusion: 该工作为半严格高阶规范理论提供了统一的构造框架，揭示了高阶Chern-Weil定理、HCS理论与Cartan同伦公式之间的深层联系，为研究更高维拓扑场论提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: We construct a semistrict higher Chern--Simons (HCS) gauge theory in $2n+2$ dimensions associated with balanced 2-term $L_\infty$-algebras. Starting from the homotopy Maurer--Cartan theory, we first introduce 2-term $L_\infty$-algebra gauge theory, and show that there is a four-dimensional HCS construction. Then we extend invariant bilinear pairings to invariant multilinear forms of the appropriate degree, and define a $(2n+3)$-dimensional higher Pontryagin--Chern form, which is closed and invariant under infinitesimal gauge transformations. Its transgression yields an explicit $(2n+2)$-dimensional HCS form. We further establish a higher Chern--Weil theorem that generates higher transgression forms, and prove that the HCS theory is a distinguished instance of the higher transgression gauge theory. Finally, we apply the extended Cartan homotopy formula in this semistrict setting, and show that it is a common origin of both the higher Chern--Weil theorem and the associated triangle equation.

</details>


### [104] [Krylov complexity and the growth of the black hole interior in 3D gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19373)
*Arpan Bhattacharyya,Sounak Pal,Juan F. Pedraza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维引力中黑洞内部生长的边界重构。通过带涂抹端点的Wilson线将codimension-one表面（体积）与边界热场双态中的涂抹算符关联函数联系起来，发现算子Krylov复杂度能够复现黑洞内部晚期线性增长，而态的Krylov spread复杂度则不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子引力和全息原理中，黑洞内部几何演化如何被边界理论编码是一个核心问题。本文旨在将复杂度-体积猜想推广到AdS_3，并探索Krylov复杂度（一种量子复杂性度量）能否捕捉黑洞内部的几何增长。

Method: 采用Chern-Simons形式化描述三维引力，将带涂抹端点的Wilson线视为边界上涂抹算符关联函数的体表示，从而将Wilson线与codimension-two观测量（纠缠熵）的传统联系推广到codimension-one观测量（体积）。随后从这些关联函数中提取Lanczos数据，计算算子Krylov复杂度，并与态Krylov spread复杂度进行比较。

Result: 算子Krylov复杂度成功复现了BTZ黑洞内部特征性的晚期线性增长，验证了算子增长与体几何之间的关联可推广到AdS_3。相反，从半经典引力配分函数得到的态Krylov spread复杂度在分析的可行范围内不表现出体体积的线性增长。

Conclusion: 结果表明算子Krylov复杂度在编码黑洞内部增长方面具有独特作用，超越了二维引力的框架，同时揭示了算子Krylov复杂度与态Krylov复杂度之间的本质区别。

Abstract: We investigate the growth of the black hole interior in three-dimensional gravity from the boundary theory. For the two-sided BTZ black hole, we propose a boundary reconstruction of the time dependence of a codimension-one surface in terms of correlation functions of smeared operators in the thermofield double state, reproducing the characteristic late-time linear growth predicted by the complexity-volume proposal. Using the Chern--Simons formulation of three-dimensional gravity, these nonlocal correlators are represented by bulk Wilson lines with smeared endpoints, extending the familiar connection between Wilson lines and codimension-two observables underlying holographic entanglement entropy to codimension-one observables. We then ask whether the same geometric growth is captured by Krylov complexity. For the smeared operators, we extract the Lanczos data from their correlation functions and find that operator Krylov complexity reproduces the late-time linear growth of the black hole interior, extending previous connections between operator growth and bulk geometry to AdS$_3$. By contrast, the Krylov spread complexity of the thermofield double state, obtained from the semiclassical gravitational partition function, does not exhibit the linear growth of the bulk volume within the regime accessible to our analysis. Our results therefore point to a distinguished role for operator Krylov complexity in encoding black hole interior growth beyond two-dimensional gravity, while highlighting a qualitative distinction between operator and state notions of Krylov complexity.

</details>


### [105] [Stochastic Schwinger Effect: de Sitter and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19378)
*Lucas Vicente García-Consuegra,Azadeh Maleknejad*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于Schwinger-Keldysh (in-in) 形式体系，发展了de Sitter时空中Schwinger效应的随机化表述，将规范场视为经典随机系综，对无质量带电物质积分后推导出影响泛函和粒子产生核至规范耦合的首个非平凡阶，并利用共形不变性将结果推广到一般空间平坦的FLRW时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙中非平稳规范背景下的粒子产生是一个重要而复杂的问题，传统静态Schwinger效应对非平稳规范场不适用，需要建立统一的随机形式体系来刻画膨胀、再加热及非热BSM扇区中由随机规范场产生的物质。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Keldysh (in-in) 形式体系，将规范场视为预设的经典随机系综并积分掉无质量带电物质，在规范耦合的首个非平凡阶推导影响泛函和粒子产生核；利用共形不变性将结果从de Sitter推广到一般空间平坦FLRW时空，无需依赖渐进出口态；确立红外安全性与经典性条件，并进一步扩展到无质量共形耦合标量及弱耦合非阿贝尔规范扇区。

Result: 建立了de Sitter时空中随机Schwinger效应的完整随机形式体系，得到了与常规静态Schwinger效应的关系；确立了描述的经典性条件；框架成功扩展至共形标量和非阿贝尔规范扇区，涵盖标准模型与隐藏扇区例子，并在热修正可忽略的范围内适用。

Conclusion: 该框架为膨胀、再加热及非热BSM扇区中随机规范场的物质产生提供了通用描述方法，将弯曲时空中的量子场论与早期宇宙及高能唯象学联系起来。

Abstract: We develop a stochastic formulation of the Schwinger effect in de Sitter spacetime using the Schwinger--Keldysh (in-in) formalism, tailored to particle production by non-stationary gauge backgrounds in the early Universe. Treating the gauge field as a prescribed classical stochastic ensemble, we integrate out massless charged matter and derive the corresponding influence functional and particle-production kernel to leading non-trivial order in the gauge coupling. Conformal invariance allows the result to be extended directly from de Sitter to generic spatially flat FLRW spacetimes, without relying on asymptotic out states. We establish the infrared safety and classicality conditions of the stochastic description and clarify its relation to the conventional static Schwinger effect. We further extend the framework to massless conformally coupled scalars and to weakly coupled non-Abelian gauge sectors, including Standard Model and hidden-sector examples, in regimes where thermal corrections are negligible. Our results provide a general framework for matter creation by stochastic gauge fields in inflation, preheating, and non-thermal BSM sectors, connecting quantum field theory in curved spacetime with early-Universe and high-energy phenomenology.

</details>


### [106] [Lorentzian Kähler-Dirac fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19400)
*Simon Catterall,Jay Hubisz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了洛伦兹号差时空中Kähler费米子的表述问题，发现该理论作为洛伦兹不变的微分形式（或反对称张量场）理论时是非幺正的。通过引入修改的内积（插入算子J），可以恢复幺正性并保证所有态具有正模，但J与洛伦兹变换性质不相容。在平坦时空中，幺正化后的表述等价于4个Dirac费米子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kähler费米子通常在有欧几里得号差的时空中被研究，但其在洛伦兹号差（物理上更相关）的时空中存在幺正性问题。作者旨在揭示这一困难，并探索恢复幺正性的可能性。

Method: 作者在Minkowski时空中分析Kähler场（微分形式/张量场），证明其标准洛伦兹不变表述是非幺正的。通过构造一个与场的某些模式反对易的算子J，修改希尔伯特空间中的内积，使所有态获得正模。作者给出了J的显式局域形式，并检验了其与哈密顿量及洛伦兹变换性质的相容性。

Result: 证明了洛伦兹号差下Kähler费米子理论（作为张量场理论）的非幺正性；提出了插入算子J的修正内积方案恢复幺正性；J与哈密顿量对易但与洛伦兹变换性质不相容；在平坦空间中，幺正化的理论等价于4个Dirac费米子。

Conclusion: Kähler费米子在洛伦兹号差时空中的传统表述是非幺正的，虽然可通过修改内积恢复幺正性，但代价是破坏洛伦兹协变性。这一结果表明Kähler费米子在洛伦兹号差下的物理意义需要重新审视，其与4个Dirac费米子的等价性仅能在平坦空间中建立。

Abstract: We examine the formulation of Kähler fermions on spacetimes with Lorentz signature. In practice we focus on Minkowski spacetime since most of the difficulties that are encountered are visible even when the spacetime is flat. We show that the theory, when interpreted as a Lorentz invariant theory of forms or antisymmetric tensor fields, is non-unitary. We show that unitarity can be restored provided one adopts a modified inner product on the Hilbert space. This modified inner product requires the insertion of an operator J that anti-commutes with certain modes of the Kähler field in such a way as to guarantee all states have positive norm. We give an explicit (and local) form for J and show that while it commutes with the Hamiltonian, it is incompatible with the Lorentz transformation properties of the tensor fields. In flat space, the unitarized formulation is equivalent to 4 Dirac fermions.

</details>


### [107] [BCFW recursion for light-like Wilson loop correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19418)
*James Drummond,Matthew Rochford,Rowan Wright*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将全纯链环的思想应用于N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中多个光锥回路算符的关联函数，将平面散射振幅积分或单Wilson环的BCFW递推关系扩展到多回路算符关联函数，发现了一个将单环递推馈入多环递推的新项，并进一步用全纯链环推导了颜色精确Wilson环关联函数的递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将BCFW递推关系从平面散射振幅/单Wilson环推广到多个光锥回路算符的关联函数，从而能够系统计算多回路算符的关联函数，拓展可计算量子的范围。

Method: 运用全纯链环(holomorphic linking)思想，构建多光锥Wilson环关联函数的BCFW递推关系；发现新的馈入项将单环递推结果连接到多环递推；结合Q-bar方程进行验证；并进一步推导颜色精确(非平面)Wilson环关联函数的递推关系。

Result: 成功将BCFW递推扩展到多回路算符关联函数，识别出连接单环与多环递推的新项，并通过若干实例验证了递推关系；同时利用全纯链环导出了颜色精确Wilson环关联函数的递推公式。

Conclusion: 全纯链环方法为多Wilson环关联函数提供了系统的递推计算框架，可与平面散射振幅的递推结构统一，并可推广至非平面情形。

Abstract: We apply the idea of holomorphic linking (arXiv:1101.1329) to correlators of multiple light-like loop operators in $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory. This allows us to extend the BCFW recursion relations for planar scattering amplitude integrands or single Wilson loops in the planar theory to correlators of multiple loop operators. We discover a novel term which feeds the recursion for single loops into the relation for multiple loop operators. We illustrate the recursion relations with several examples and highlight its applicability in combination with the $\bar{Q}$-equation for multiple loop operators. Finally, we again employ holomorphic linking to derive a recursion relation for colour-exact Wilson loop correlators.

</details>


### [108] [Holographic Bit Threads from String-Diagrammatic Quantum Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19428)
*Yi-Yu Lin,Song Cheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在范畴量子力学的框架下，将Coecke的量子信息流（QIF）概念应用于全息张量网络，证明QIF轨迹满足比特线的无散度和密度约束条件，从而为比特线的非唯一性提供了过程中心的新解释，并展示了QIF能探测超越纠缠熵的更精细纠缠结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特线作为全息纠缠熵最小面积公式的凸对偶，其物理图像长期与Bell对解释相关联，但其详细轨迹的物理意义特别是非唯一性一直不清楚。作者试图通过量子信息流理论为比特线轨迹提供更深刻的物理理解，解决其非唯一性问题。

Method: 将范畴量子力学中Coecke的量子信息流（QIF）概念引入全息设置，在协议-网络几何框架下分析离散全息体（张量网络）中的QIF轨迹，证明其满足比特线的无散度和密度边界条件；利用ZX弦图分析稳定子型全息张量网络，探索QIF轨迹对精细纠缠结构的探测能力。

Result: QIF轨迹在张量网络中的行为满足比特线的核心约束条件；比特线的非唯一性可以从过程中心视角自然解释——同一蒸馏任务可由不同协议实现，产生不同QIF轨迹；在稳定子型张量网络中，QIF轨迹能够揭示超越纠缠熵所能刻画的更精细纠缠结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为比特线的物理图像提供了基于量子信息流的过程中心诠释，不仅解释了比特线轨迹的非唯一性来源，还展示了量子信息流作为比纠缠熵更精细的纠缠结构探测工具在全息框架中的潜力，沟通了范畴量子力学与全息原理两个领域。

Abstract: Bit threads, arising as the convex dual of the minimal-surface formula for holographic entanglement entropy, are line-like structures with nontrivial bulk trajectories. Their physical picture has long been associated with Bell-pair interpretations, yet the meaning of their detailed trajectories, particularly their nonuniqueness, remains unclear. We propose to apply Coecke's notion of quantum information flow (QIF) in protocol-network geometry, developed within categorical quantum mechanics, to a holographic setup, and show that its trajectories within the discrete holographic bulk---the tensor network---obey the divergence-free and density-bound conditions of bit threads. From this process-centered perspective, bit-thread nonuniqueness becomes natural: for a given resource state, different protocols realizing the same distillation task can give rise to different QIF trajectories. We further analyze stabilizer-type holographic tensor networks using ZX string diagrams and show that QIF trajectories can probe finer entanglement structure beyond the level captured by entanglement entropy.

</details>


### [109] [Tropical and Stringy Integrals for In-In Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19720)
*Song He,Xiang Li,Yong-Xiang Su,Fan Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为共形耦合标量场宇宙学in-in关联函数中的固定图贡献引入了热带积分和弦性积分。全时表示可分解为图相关的顶点空间拉普拉斯积分（每顶点一个实变量）和初等边空间拉普拉斯积分（每内边一个实变量）的乘积。顶点空间指数为与位点和相对边时间相关的绝对值之和，作为分段线性函数是in-in区域多胞体的支撑函数；每个绝对值也是正洛朗二项式的热带极限。非热带化保留这些二项式定义了有限α'的顶点空间弦性积分，使得多胞体及其弦性积分可直接从物理时间积分读出。α'→0极限下该积分变为in-in区域多胞体的归一化对偶体积，边空间因子独立变形为beta积分乘积并恢复初等传播子归一化。文章还推导了场论有理形式、增广图室、精确有限α'平行边约化、边能量与部分能量极点的因式分解公式以及子孙塔，并给出了树图关联子作为其线图“图立方体”的环境闵可夫斯基和与弦性积分实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立宇宙学in-in关联函数与热带几何、弦性积分（stringy integrals）及正几何结构之间的严格联系，为固定图贡献的关联函数提供一种新的几何与组合学视角，从而统一并简化对关联函数极结构、因子化性质及多胞体实现的理解。

Method: 将全时表示分解为顶点空间与边空间两个拉普拉斯积分；利用绝对值之和定义分段线性支撑函数（in-in区域多胞体）；以正洛朗二项式的热带极限识别每个绝对值项；通过非热带化保留二项式构造有限α'弦性积分；利用增广图室推导场论有理形式；通过并行边约化和极结构因式分解处理传播子归一化与极点分析；最后以线图图立方体给出关联子的环境闵可夫斯基和实现。

Result: 建立了in-in关联函数固定图贡献与in-in区域多胞体及其弦性积分的显式对应关系；α'→0极限将弦性积分化为多胞体对偶体积；推导了场论有理形式（来自增广图室）、精确有限α'平行边约化公式、边能量/部分能量极点因子化公式及子孙塔；给出了树图关联子作为线图图立方体的环境实现，并记录了相关的对数临界方程与仿射因子排列的通用参考度数。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了宇宙学关联函数与热带/弦性几何之间的深层联系，提供了一种统一的组合几何框架来理解固定图关联函数的结构，其有限α'变形、多胞体实现与因子化性质为未来进一步探索正几何方法与宇宙学振幅理论的交叉提供了基础。

Abstract: We introduce tropical and stringy integrals for fixed-graph contributions to cosmological in-in correlators of conformally coupled scalars. The full-time representation factorizes into a graph-dependent vertex-space Laplace integral, with one real variable for each graph vertex, and an elementary edge-space Laplace integral, with one real variable for each internal edge. The vertex-space exponent is a sum of absolute values associated with sites and relative edge times; as a piecewise-linear function, it is the support function of the in-in zonotope. Each absolute value is also the tropical limit of a positive Laurent binomial. Retaining these binomials before tropicalization defines a finite-$α'$ vertex-space stringy integral, so both the polytope and its stringy integral are read directly from the physical time integral. In the $α' \to 0$ limit this integral becomes the normalized dual volume of the in-in zonotope, while the edge-space factor deforms independently into a product of beta integrals and restores the elementary propagator normalization. We derive the field-theory rational form from augmented-graph chambers, as well as exact finite-$α'$ parallel-edge reduction, factorization formulas for edge-energy and partial-energy poles, and even descendant towers. As an alternative geometric realization of fixed-graph correlators, we find an ambient Minkowski-sum and stringy-integral realization of the graph correlahedron for a tree graph as the so-called graph cubeahedron of its line graph. For completeness, we also record the logarithmic critical equations and generic reference degrees of the associated affine divisor arrangement.

</details>


### [110] [A Stationary Composition Law for Schwinger-Keldysh Effective Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19795)
*Denis Comelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文建立了开放量子系统与闭合系统在Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量层面的变分组合法则。开放系统的有效作用量Γ_O不是闭合系统Γ_C与环境诱导贡献Γ_IF的简单相加，而是通过一个关于中间场Ψ的驻点条件满足Γ_O=Stat_Ψ{Γ_C[Ψ]+Γ_IF[Φ-Ψ]}的组合关系，适用于局域与非局域的非平衡量子场论有效作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放系统的连通生成泛函W_O与闭合系统W_C之差W_IF（环境诱导贡献）具有直接的加法关系，但对应的Legendre变换后，有效作用量并不满足类似的加法关系。作者旨在找到有效作用量层面的正确组合法则，以更深入地理解开放系统（如量子耗散系统）与其闭合对应物之间的结构关系。

Method: 通过Legendre变换分析，发现有效作用量Γ_O、Γ_C和Γ_IF之间满足一种驻点组合法则（stationary composition law），即Γ_O[Φ]等于关于Ψ对Γ_C[Ψ]+Γ_IF[Φ-Ψ]取驻点值。该法则是Legendre变换下加法关系转变为变分组合的自然结果。作者通过二次理论（线性实现）和一般含时无关有效作用量（非线性实现）两种情形加以例证。

Result: 证明了开放系统有效作用量由闭合系统有效作用量与环境诱导有效作用量通过驻点条件组合而成，该法则同时适用于局域和非局域Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量。在二次理论中展示其线性实现，在一般含时无关情形中展示其非线性实现。

Conclusion: 开放系统与闭合系统的有效作用量之间存在优雅的变分组合结构，弥补了生成泛函层面的简单加法关系在Legendre变换后失效的问题，为非平衡量子场论中开放量子系统的有效作用量构造提供了系统的框架。

Abstract: Let $W_{\cal O}[J]$ and $W_{\cal C}[J]$ denote the connected generating functionals of an open system and its closed system counterpart, and define the environment-induced contribution $W_{\rm IF}[J]$ by the difference: $ W_{\rm IF}[J]\equiv W_{\cal O}[J]-W_{\cal C}[J].$ The corresponding Legendre transforms, $Γ_{\cal O}$, $Γ_{\cal C}$, and $Γ_{\rm IF}$, do not obey an analogous additive relation but satisfy a stationary composition law given by:
  \[
  Γ_{\cal O}[Φ]
  =
  \operatorname*{Stat}_Ψ
  \left\{
  Γ_{\cal C}[Ψ]
  +
  Γ_{\rm IF}[Φ-Ψ]
  \right\} \] where ${\rm Stat}$ denotes evaluation at a solution of the stationarity condition with respect to $Ψ$. This variational composition applies to both local and nonlocal Schwinger-Keldysh effective actions in nonequilibrium quantum field theory. We illustrate its linear and nonlinear realizations through quadratic theories and general time independent effective actions, respectively.

</details>


### [111] [Holographic Dual of Synge's Identity in AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19845)
*Bingbing Chen,Deyou Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在任意维度下识别了有限AdS测地线的Synge恒等式对应的CFT对偶，从精确的有限体-边界对出发推导出Weyl框架标量两点函数和有限纠缠熵的端点尺度方程，并揭示了平面框架下与膨胀Ward恒等式、二维情况下与纠缠RG方程的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Synge恒等式在AdS/CFT对应中描述了有限AdS测地线端点之间的几何关系，但其在CFT侧的精确对偶形式尚未被系统识别。本工作旨在建立这一几何恒等式的边界对偶表述，并揭示其与Ward恒等式、纠缠熵尺度演化方程之间的深层联系。

Method: 从精确的有限体-边界对（finite bulk-boundary pairs）出发，将Synge方程转化为端点尺度方程。分别对Weyl框架下的标量两点函数和不相交球体间的有限纠缠熵进行分析，在平面框架中推导共同尺度关联子方程即膨胀Ward恒等式；在纠缠扇区则由横向双曲面积主导相对尺度方程，并验证二维情形退化为已知的纠缠RG方程。

Result: 证明了Synge恒等式在CFT侧对应两类端点尺度方程：一是标量两点函数在Weyl框架下的共同尺度方程（在平面框架中化为膨胀Ward恒等式）；二是有限纠缠熵的相对尺度方程，由横向双曲面积控制，并在二维极限下精确还原为先前发现的纠缠RG方程。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了AdS中Synge恒等式与CFT尺度演化的严格对应关系，统一了Ward恒等式、纠缠熵因式与有限AdS几何之间的联系，为有限尺度背景下AdS/CFT对偶的精确表述提供了新的结构性见解。

Abstract: We identify CFT counterparts of Synge's identity for finite AdS geodesics in arbitrary dimensions. Starting from exact finite bulk--boundary pairs, the Synge equation induces endpoint scale equations for a Weyl-frame scalar two-point function and for the finite entanglement entropy between disjoint balls. In the planar frame, the common-scale correlator equation is the dilatation Ward identity. In the entanglement sector, the corresponding relative-scale equation is governed by a transverse hyperbolic area and reduces in two dimensions to the previously identified entanglement RG equation.

</details>


### [112] [One more Sine-Gordon soliton in AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19859)
*D. V. Diakonov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在AdS时空中变形的sine-Gordon理论中发现了另一种单孤子解，该解在平直空间中没有对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期工作（arXiv:2604.00160）中，作者已在AdS时空中变形的sine-Gordon理论里找到孤子解，这些解在无穷大半径极限下退化为平直空间中的单孤子解。本工作的动机是探索AdS时空中是否存在平直空间没有对应物的新型孤子解。

Method: 本文在前期工作基础上继续研究AdS时空中变形的sine-Gordon理论，通过构造和分析寻找另一种单孤子解，该解具有纯粹的AdS时空特性，在平直空间极限下没有对应解。

Result: 找到了一种在平直空间中无对应物的单孤子解，这种解是AdS时空特有的非线性现象。

Conclusion: AdS时空中的变形sine-Gordon理论不仅包含可退化到平直空间的孤子解，还拥有平直空间中没有的独特孤子解，体现了AdS时空曲率对非线性场论的丰富影响。

Abstract: In previous work arXiv:2604.00160, we found soliton solutions in a deformation of the sine-Gordon theory in AdS spacetime that, in the infinite-radius limit, reduce to single-soliton solutions in flat space. In this paper, we find another single soliton solution that has no analog in flat space.

</details>


### [113] [Massive spin-2 supermultiplet and supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19896)
*Yu. M. Zinoviev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了无质量N=1超引力与一个包含(2,3/2,3/2,1)自由度的有质量超多重态的相互作用，作为双引力（bigravity）的候选超对称扩展。通过使用规范不变描述和基于unfolded方程的新方法，并限制最小导数相互作用（玻色子两阶导数、费米子一阶导数），整个构造被完全唯一地确定，消除了场重定义带来的歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双引力理论（bigravity）是一种重要的修改引力理论，但其超对称扩展尚未被充分研究。本文旨在构建无质量N=1超引力与大规模自旋多重态的相互作用，作为双引力的超对称版本，同时解决规范不变描述中场重定义引起的歧义问题。

Method: 采用规范不变描述来处理有质量的自旋-2、自旋-3/2和自旋-1场；利用基于unfolded方程的新近提出的方法来固定场重定义带来的歧义；将相互作用限制在最小导数数目（玻色子顶点二阶导数、费米子顶点一阶导数），从而完全确定整个构造。

Result: 结果表明，在上述约束条件下（unfolded方程方法+最小导数数目限制），整个超引力与有质量超多重态的相互作用构造被完全唯一地固定，不存在剩余的自由度或歧义。

Conclusion: 本文展示了一种系统化且唯一的方法来构造N=1超引力的有质量超多重态扩展，为超对称双引力理论提供了一个明确的候选框架。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the interaction of massless $N=1$ supergravity with a massive (2,3/2,3/2,1) supermultiplet, as a possible candidate for a supersymmetric extension to bigravity. A gauge invariant description for massive spin-2, spin-3/2 and spin-1 fields is used. As a result of this, ambiguities arise related to possible field redefinitions, which are fixed by using a recently proposed method based on unfolded equations, and by restricting ourselves to interactions with the minimum number of derivatives (two for bosonic and one for fermionic vertices). It appears that this completely fixes the entire construction.

</details>


### [114] [A Note on a High-Energy Limit of Gravitational Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19941)
*Poul H. Damgaard,Ludovic Plante*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究后闵可夫斯基展开中二进制散射问题动量冲量的全阶公式，证明其本质上是渐近展开，级数发散但具有Borel可和性，且完整积分形式的函数是有限的，并确定了展开系数的超越性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后闵可夫斯基展开是广义相对论中围绕平直时空按牛顿常数G展开的方法，但该展开的适用范围有限。当前研究致力于通过全阶求和来扩展其适用范围，特别是高能极限下二进制散射问题的动量冲量公式。理解该展开的收敛性质（是渐近展开还是收敛级数）对能否可靠地应用该方法至强耦合区域至关重要。

Method: 作者证明该动量冲量公式本质上是一个渐近展开（发散级数），并通过Borel可和性分析来研究其求和性质。同时利用积分形式的完整函数表达式来验证有限性，并进一步分析展开系数的超越性（transcendentality）性质。

Result: 证明该级数确实是渐近展开而非收敛级数；尽管级数发散，但给出了Borel可和性的证据；完整函数在积分形式下是有限的；作为副产品，确定了展开式系数的高次超越性（transcendentality）结构。

Conclusion: 该全阶动量冲量公式具有明确的渐近特性，Borel可和性保证了对发散级数进行有意义求和的可能性，为此类求和方法的可靠应用提供了理论基础。系数超越性性质的确定有助于理解展开的结构并可能指导进一步的改进。

Abstract: The post-Minkowskian expansion of general relativity expands the metric in Newton's constant G around flat space-time, but much effort currently focuses on ways to extend its range of applicability by summing the perturbative series to all orders. A regime of particular interest is a specific high-energy limit where a formula derived recently provides the momentum kick of binary scattering problem to all orders, and even suggests an approach towards strong coupling. We show that this is an asymptotic expansion. Although the series diverges we give evidence that it is Borel summable, and the full function in integral form is finite. As a by-product we determine the transcendentality properties of the coefficients in the expanded expression.

</details>


### [115] [Hilbert space relation from dS to AdS through Reflection Positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19948)
*Yu-ki Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了de Sitter (dS) 背景下自由标量场单粒子扇区中，unitity与反射正定性之间的逻辑独立性。结合Witten的dS配对与Bousso-Maloney-Strominger的径向伴随，将不变Hilbert积与赤道反射复合：主序列中该形式恒为零且OS商无态；互补序列中反射核在单位球上非零，由Neeb-Ólafsson定理知该核正半定当且仅当下权重满足标量共形unitarity界，所得Hilbert空间赋予AdS等距的正能量表示，其值域恰为Klebanov-Witten替代量子化窗口，从而在表示论视角建立AdS与dS的新联系，无需半径的解析延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明dS时空量子引力中unitarity与反射正定性（Osterwalder-Schrader条件）这两个关键物理条件之间的逻辑关系，并探索在不依赖传统半径解析延拓（Wick旋转）的情况下建立AdS与dS之间的表示论联系。

Method: 以固定dS背景上的自由标量场为对象，限制于单粒子扇区。将不变Hilbert积与赤道反射复合（受Witten的dS配对和BMS径向伴随启发），分别分析主序列与互补序列两种情况；利用Neeb与Ólafsson关于反射核正半定性的定理，建立与标量共形unitarity界及AdS等距正能量表示的联系。

Result: 主序列中反射形式恒为零，Osterwalder-Schrader商空间不含任何态；互补序列中反射核非零且正半定当且仅当下权重满足标量共形unitarity界。所得Hilbert空间实现AdS等距群的正能量表示，其值域与Klebanov-Witten替代量子化窗口精确吻合。

Conclusion: dS的unitarity与反射正定性确实在逻辑上独立；通过表示论框架，互补序列的反射Hilbert空间自然衔接AdS正能量表示，为AdS/dS对偶提供了一种不经由半径解析延拓的新途径，并深化了对替代量子化窗口的理解。

Abstract: De Sitter unitarity and reflection positivity are logically independent. We study their relation for a free scalar field on a fixed de Sitter background, restricting attention to the one-particle sector. Motivated by Witten's de Sitter pairing and the radial adjoint of Bousso, Maloney and Strominger, we compose the invariant Hilbert product with equatorial reflection. In the standard principal-series realization, this form vanishes identically and its Osterwalder-Schrader quotient contains no states. For the complementary series, the reflected product becomes a nonzero reflected kernel on the unit ball. A result of Neeb and Ólafsson shows that this kernel is positive semidefinite precisely when the lower weight satisfies the scalar conformal unitarity bound. The resulting Hilbert space with the reflected inner product furnishes a positive-energy representation of AdS isometries according to Neeb and Ólafsson. We identify that the range coincides with the Klebanov-Witten alternative-quantization window from physics point of view. This correspondence suggests a novel relation from the representation viewpoint between AdS and dS without the conventional analytic continuation of radius.

</details>


### [116] [Singularity-Rank Signatures in Generalized Dirac Oscillators near a BTZ Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20071)
*João V. R. Alencar,Allan R. P. Moreira,João B. R. Silva,Abdullah Guvendi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非极值BTZ黑洞近视界区域中奇异广义Dirac-振荡耦合。通过协变构造将两种奇异轮廓分别与固有加速度及其径向梯度相关联，得到1/ρ和1/ρ²两种近视界行为。p=1情形为Fuchsian系统（正则奇点），可通过自旋子变换约化为Whittaker方程；p=2为第一类非正则情形（Poincaré秩1），具有本质因子e^{±β₂/ρ}ρ^{-1/2}。有限半径响应满足精确的Riccati方程，其大阻尼展开在γ^{-3}阶首次对相互作用的径向梯度敏感，且即使两种耦合在ρ₀处匹配，响应仍保留极点阶数的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究BTZ黑洞近视界区域中奇异广义Dirac-振荡耦合的数学结构，理解不同奇异势（1/ρ和1/ρ²）下Dirac方程解的解析性质，并为后续匹配完整BTZ外部区域提供局域旋量数据。

Method: 采用有效协变构造方法（适配平稳视界congruence），将两种奇异轮廓分别映射到固有加速度及其径向梯度。对p=1情形使用常数自旋子变换将矩阵-Coulomb系统精确约化为Whittaker方程，同时通过消元获得等价的合流Heun表示；对p=2情形分析局部分支及系数的阶乘级数展开。有限半径响应Z_p定义为两个独立解的比值，满足精确Riccati方程，并研究其大阻尼（γ）展开。

Result: p=1系统为正则奇点（Fuchsian），可精确约化为Whittaker方程，且等价于带表观有限奇点的合流Heun表示；p=2系统为第一非正则情形，局部分支含本质因子e^{±β₂/ρ}ρ^{-1/2}，系数展开具有典型的阶乘晚期阶增长。响应函数Z_p满足Riccati方程，大阻尼展开在γ^{-3}阶首次感知势的径向梯度信息；即使两耦合在ρ₀处相等，响应仍能区分不同的极点阶数。

Conclusion: 奇异Dirac-振荡耦合的极点阶数p（1或2）对系统解析结构有本质影响：p=1给出Fuchsian的Whittaker/Heun描述，p=2引入非正则奇点和阶乘发散。有限半径响应的Riccati结构及其对径向导数的敏感性为后续匹配完整BTZ外部解提供了关键的局域旋量数据。

Abstract: We study singular generalized Dirac-oscillator couplings in the near-horizon region of a nonextremal BTZ black hole. An effective covariant construction adapted to the stationary horizon congruence relates the two singular profiles to the proper acceleration and its radial gradient, whose leading near-horizon behaviors are $1/ρ$ and $1/ρ^2$. This motivates the family $\mathcal{L}_p(ρ)=λ_0+β_p/ρ^p$, with $p=1,2$. The $p=1$ system has a regular singular point and is Fuchsian, whereas $p=2$ is the first irregular case; more generally, a leading $ρ^{-p}$ interaction with $p>1$ has Poincaré rank $p-1$. For $p=1$, a constant spinor transformation reduces the matrix-Coulomb system exactly to the Whittaker equation, while elimination in the original basis gives an equivalent confluent-Heun representation with an apparent finite singularity. For $p=2$, the local branches contain the essential factors $e^{\pmβ_2/ρ}ρ^{-1/2}$, and their coefficient expansions exhibit generic factorial late-order growth. The finite-radius response $\mathcal{Z}_p(Ω;ρ_0)=ψ_{2,p}(ρ_0)/ψ_{1,p}(ρ_0)$ obeys an exact Riccati equation whose large-damping expansion first becomes sensitive to the radial gradient of the interaction at order $γ^{-3}$. Even when the two couplings are matched at $ρ_0$, this response retains information about their different pole orders. The response functions provide local spinorial data for subsequent matching to the complete BTZ exterior.

</details>


### [117] [Maximal Torus Topological Entanglement Entropy in WZW Theories at Bounded Ground-State Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20333)
*Ce Shen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究非扭WZW模张量范畴中真空通量态的普适熵贡献Γ_{T^2}=2log D的最大化问题，证明当简单对象数r(g,k)≤R时，最大熵Γ_max(R)满足渐近关系~[7ζ(3)/(4π^2)]q_R^2（q_R=log2R），且Sp(2n)_n序列达到该渐近系数。该系数源于类型C根积的Fourier展开丢失偶模，熵-谱不等式证明经典族的优性，秩-级对偶与固定秩估计控制不平衡和例外序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子场论和拓扑量子计算中，张量范畴的普适熵贡献是重要的物理量。作者试图在有限简单对象数约束下，系统性地最大化真空通量态在环面上的普适熵贡献，揭示其渐近增长规律及最优实现序列。

Method: 将最大化普适熵问题转化为在r(g,k)≤R约束下优化2log D。利用根积的Fourier展开分析，发现类型C根积在等秩等级下丢失偶模，给出系数2π^{-2}∑_{m odd}m^{-3}=7ζ(3)/(4π^2)。通过熵-谱不等式证明经典族的最优性，并结合秩-级对偶和固定秩估计处理非平衡及例外序列。

Result: 建立了最大普适熵的渐近公式Γ_max(R)~[7ζ(3)/(4π^2)]q_R^2（q_R→∞），渐近系数由7ζ(3)/(4π^2)≈0.2130给出，并由Sp(2n)_n序列实现。该结果对任意R值都成立，且在所有简单李代数与正整数级范围内最优。

Conclusion: 真空通量态的普适熵在有限简单对象数约束下以q_R^2速率渐近增长，Sp(2n)_n是渐近最优序列。这一发现揭示了WZW模张量范畴中熵与谱结构之间的深刻联系，为拓扑序的分类和优化提供了新视角。

Abstract: For an untwisted Wess--Zumino--Witten modular tensor category $\cC(\mathfrak g,k)$, let $r(\mathfrak g,k)$ be the number of simple objects and $\cD(\mathfrak g,k)$ its total quantum dimension. The vacuum-flux state on a torus divided into two cylinders has positive universal entropy contribution $Γ_{T^2}=2\log\cD$. We maximize this quantity over all simple Lie algebras and positive integral levels subject to $r(\mathfrak g,k)\leq R$. If $q_R=\log_2R$, then $Γ_{\max}(R)\sim[7ζ(3)/(4π^2)]q_R^2$ as $R\to\infty$.
  The sequence $Sp(2n)_n$ attains the asymptotic coefficient. At equal rank
  and level, the Fourier expansion of the type-$C$ root product loses its even modes, leaving $2π^{-2}\sum_{m\,\mathrm{odd}}m^{-3}=7ζ(3)/(4π^2)$. An entropy--spectral inequality proves optimality among the classical families, while rank--level duality and fixed-rank estimates control unbalanced and exceptional sequences.

</details>
