### [118] [Non-Inertial Response of Correlations: From Scalar Bell Observables to an Extended Correlation Tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19242)
*Timur F. Kamalov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了贝尔可观测量在光子与费米子单重态两个扇区中的符号反转现象：光子扇区具有正的前置因子，而费米子扇区具有负的前置因子。这种符号差异源于完整的二维粒子张量在不同角扇区中的投影，可通过相关张量而非不同的贝尔可观测量定义来区分两类物体。文章还提出了运动依赖扩展张量及相位同步实验方案，并通过相位构造恢复丘奇森界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝尔可观测量通常被视为一个标量关联，这一表述掩盖了其作为完整两粒子张量投影的本质。作者希望揭示光子与费米子单重态在贝尔可观测量中根本的符号差异，并利用这一差异作为实验上识别两类量子物体的新判据。

Method: 将贝尔可观测量构造为完整两粒子张量（关联块）的投影，区分两个不同的角扇区。通过将相位方向映射到线性偏振器的物理轴，推导出双角度依赖关系。对光子，平均两个投影振幅并跨越完整的非惯性相位区间；对费米子，利用负交换和乐（holonomy）的完整相位-动量扇区。同时引入运动依赖扩展张量及频率依赖的非惯性磁化率，并用相位构造导出二元联合概率。

Result: 核心结果：光子扇区的角余弦定律具有正前置因子，费米子单重态扇区具有负前置因子。在重合校准设置下，光子贝尔可观测量为正，费米子为负。光子斯托克斯相关张量具有正的线性偏振分量和负的圆偏振分量，而费米子单重态由各向同性的负相关张量描述。相位构造可恢复丘奇森界，光子与费米子扇区给出符号相反的CHSH最优组合。

Conclusion: 光子与费米子单重态通过其相关张量的符号结构即可被区分，无需修改贝尔可观测量的定义。提议的相位同步实验（结合机械与等效光学调制）能够将校准基旋转与相关结构的残余响应分离，为两类物体的实验识别提供了可操作的方案。

Abstract: A standard Bell observable is a scalar correlation associated with a selected pair of local measurement directions. We formulate it as a projection of the correlation block of a complete two-particle tensor and distinguish two fundamentally different angular sectors. The central result is a reversal of the sign multiplying the angular cosine law: the photon sector has a positive prefactor, whereas the fermionic singlet sector has a negative prefactor. For coincident calibrated settings, the Bell observable is positive for photons and negative for fermions. This sign difference can be used for experimental identification of the two types of objects. For photons, this result follows from averaging two projection amplitudes over the complete non-inertial phase interval; the fermionic sign follows from the negative exchange holonomy of the complete phase--momentum sector. Mapping the phase directions to the physical axes of linear polarizers produces the corresponding double-angle dependence. Accordingly, the two cases are distinguished by their correlation tensors rather than by different definitions of the Bell observable. The photon Stokes correlation tensor has positive linear-polarization components and a negative circular-polarization component, whereas the fermionic singlet is described by an isotropic negative correlation tensor. A motion-dependent extended tensor and its generally frequency-dependent non-inertial susceptibility are introduced. A phase-synchronous experiment with mechanical and equivalent optical modulation is proposed to separate calibrated basis rotations from a residual response of the correlation structure. The same phase construction yields binary joint probabilities and recovers the Tsirelson bound, with opposite signed optimal CHSH combinations for the photon and fermionic sectors.

</details>


### [119] [A Zoology of Quantum Turing Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20151)
*Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子Turing图灵斑图形态学，发现同一Lindblad方程可支撑条纹、斑点、孔洞、迷宫及缺陷等多种形态。在可控的稳定条纹态中，形态学阈值与高斯见证（Gaussian witness）丢失在参数上可分离：可见的Turing条纹可在高斯见证裕量过零后依然存在，且见证阈值按N^{-1}衰减，而条纹向列阈值在固定条件下趋向非零值，两者比值随N增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放量子系统中Turing斑图形态学的全貌（zoology），并定量厘清经典可见形态与量子非经典性（Gaussian witness）之间的分离关系，为在量子平台上观测和表征Turing斑图提供理论依据。

Method: 采用完全正定的Lindblad主方程描述耗散量子格点系统，构建Turing失稳条件以生成多种斑图形态。通过定义两类Gaussian witness（基于k*模的精确壳上判定）与形态学判据（条纹向列序），系统比较它们在不同系统尺寸N下的阈值行为及其参数依赖。

Result: 发现形态学阈值远低于Gaussian witness阈值，可见条纹可在量子非经典性见证消失后仍持续存在；见证阈值按N^{-1}衰减至零，而形态学向列阈值在固定格子尺寸、时间窗和形态判据下趋向非零定值，因此两者阈值比随N发散。

Conclusion: Turing斑图的经典形态学与量子关联丢失是不同扇区，可通过实空间成像与动量分辨协方差测量分别探测，这一参数分离为耗散量子斑图系统中区分经典与量子效应提供了可操作的实验方案。

Abstract: We explore quantum Turing pattern zoology, where the same Lindblad equation supports a morphology atlas of stripes, spots, holes, labyrinths, and defects. The stable stripe species provides a quantitatively controlled case in which morphology and Gaussian witness loss separate parametrically. In particular, visible Turing stripes can remain after two Gaussian witness margins associated with the same $k_*$ mode cross zero in a completely positive Lindblad lattice. The witness thresholds on the exact shell fall as $\mathcal{N}^{-1}$. The stripe nematic threshold tends to a nonzero value at fixed lattice size, time window, and morphology criterion. The ratio of the morphology threshold to either witness threshold therefore grows with $\mathcal{N}$. Imaging and momentum-resolved covariance measurements probe these sectors separately.

</details>


### [120] [The HALO Engine: $\mathcal{O}(1)$-Step Compilation and Localized String Rupture for Lattice Gauge Theories on Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19243)
*Abhiroop Gohar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了硬件感知晶格优化（HALO）编译器，通过不可变的O(1)电路深度执行全局时间演化步，解决了量子晶格规范理论（LGT）数字模拟中电路深度随晶格尺寸线性增长的瓶颈。作者利用该框架在16量子比特超导transmon处理器上模拟了L=15介子弦的Schwinger模型量子链路模型（QLM）截断动力学，结合零噪声外推（ZNE）抑制退相干，精确识别了t≈0.790格点单位的拓扑跃迁（破裂概率18.3±2.2%），并绘制了介观晶格动力学相图，确定禁闭相边界g_c=1.0。此外，该框架被扩展到更高维度，提供了可扩展的恒定深度2D单元胞蓝图，为容错二维量子色动力学（QCD）模拟铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准数字量子模拟依赖Trotter化方案，电路深度随晶格尺寸线性增长，这与NISQ硬件的相干性极限相冲突。实时模拟晶格规范理论（尤其是QCD等强相互作用理论）需要突破这一深度缩放瓶颈，以在近期量子硬件上实现介观尺度的非微扰动力学模拟。

Method: 提出HALO编译器架构，实现每个Trotter步的O(1)电路深度全局时间演化。采用复合多量子比特测量链接（gauge link）表示支持非平凡的电场动力学，并在16量子比特超导transmon处理器上初始化并执行L=15介子弦的拉伸状态。将O(1)编译与零噪声外推（ZNE）结合以抑制硬件退相干，同时将HALO引擎的数学原理扩展到二维单元胞设计方案。

Result: 成功在16量子比特处理器上模拟非微扰动力学，在t≈0.790格点单位处识别到拓扑跃迁，破裂概率为18.3±2.2%。通过对ZNE抑制退相干后提取精确的局域对产生动力学交叉，实验绘制了介观晶格的动力学相图，准确确定有效禁闭相边界g_c=1.0。此外，提出了消除磁学plaquette路由开销的恒定深度2D单元胞蓝图。

Conclusion: HALO编译器通过恒定深度编译成功将量子规范理论的数字模拟推进到介观尺度，在NISQ硬件上实现了非微扰动力学的精确提取。该框架的可扩展数学原理为更高维度（尤其是二维QCD）的容错模拟提供了直接的算法路径，展示了克服深度缩放瓶颈的可行方案。

Abstract: Simulating the real-time dynamics of lattice gauge theories (LGTs) represents a challenge for near-term quantum computing. Standard digital simulations rely on Trotterization schemes where circuit depth scales proportionally with lattice size, inevitably colliding with the coherence limits of noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware. To deal with this depth-scaling bottleneck, we introduce the Hardware-Aware Lattice Optimization (HALO) compiler, an architecture that executes global time-evolution steps in an immutable $\mathcal{O}(1)$ circuit depth per Trotter step. Leveraging this framework, we elevate the digital simulation of the Quantum Link Model (QLM) truncation of the Schwinger model to the mesoscopic scale, utilizing a composite multi-qubit gauge link representation to support non-trivial electric field dynamics. We initialize and execute the non-perturbative dynamics of a heavily stretched $L=15$ meson string on a 16-qubit superconducting transmon processor. By coupling the $\mathcal{O}(1)$ compilation with Zero-Noise Extrapolation (ZNE), we suppress physical hardware decoherence to extract the precise dynamical crossover of localized pair creation, identifying the topological transition at $t \approx 0.790$ lattice units with an $18.3 \pm 2.2\%$ rupture probability. Furthermore, we empirically map the dynamical phase diagram of the mesoscopic lattice, pinpointing the effective confinement phase boundary at precisely $g_c = 1.0$. Finally, we extend the mathematical principles of the HALO engine to higher dimensions, presenting a scalable, constant-depth 2D unit-cell blueprint that eliminates the routing overhead of magnetic plaquettes, paving a direct algorithmic pathway toward the fault-tolerant simulation of two-dimensional Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD).

</details>


### [121] [Dissipation-enhanced scrambling in the SYK model coupled to a lossy cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19310)
*Pietro Pelliconi,Bastien Lapierre,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了存在耗散（以Lindblad主方程建模，玻色子泄漏率为κ）的Yukawa-SYK模型，通过大N理论和数值方法求解了该模型在稳态下晚时的费米子弛豫率和Lyapunov指数，发现：Lyapunov指数在任意κ下均为正，且对p>2可随κ增大而增大；p=2（QED情形）介于混沌（p>2）和可积（p=1）之间；玻色子-费米子比存在临界值γ_c≈2/p²划分不同动力学区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来SYK模型在量子模拟器中的实现方案被广泛提出，理解耗散（尤其是玻色子泄漏）对SYK模型动力学性质的影响，对于在实际量子模拟装置中实现和观测SYK混沌行为至关重要。

Method: 采用Lindblad主方程描述耗散，对Yukawa-SYK模型（N个Majorana费米子与R=γN个玻色子线性耦合于独立SYK p-体相互作用）进行大N理论求解；在无穷温度稳态下初始化系统，同时通过数值方法和对大p极限的解析方法求解晚时费米子弛豫率与Lyapunov指数。

Result: 发现了丰富的动力学相图：Lyapunov指数在所有κ值下均为正，表明耗散不会完全破坏混沌；对p>2，Lyapunov指数甚至随κ增大而增长；p=2的QED情形展现出介于完全混沌（p>2）与可积（p=1）之间的特殊行为；识别出临界玻色子-费米子比例γ_c≈2/p²，该值将系统分割为不同动力学区域。

Conclusion: 耗散玻色子泄漏虽然改变SYK模型的动力学特性，但不会完全消除其混沌行为；系统在不同参数区域展现出丰富的动力学景观，为量子模拟器中实现和调控SYK模型混沌提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study the Yukawa-Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model, a disordered model of $N$ Majorana fermions and $R=γN$ bosons in which the bosons are linearly coupled to independent realizations of SYK $p$-body interactions, in the presence of dissipation, modeled by a Lindblad master equation. Motivated by recent proposals for implementing SYK models in quantum simulators, we focus on bosonic leakage at rate $κ$. Initializing the system in the steady state, we analyze the late-time fermionic relaxation rate and the Lyapunov exponent, solving the large-$N$ theory both numerically and for $p$ large, finding a rich landscape of dynamical behaviors. Most notably, the Lyapunov exponent remains positive for every value of $κ$ and, for $p>2$, can even grow as $κ$ increases. The QED case $p=2$, which lies between the fully chaotic regime $p>2$ and the integrable case $p=1$, exhibits special features. We also identify a critical value of the boson-to-fermion ratio $γ_c \approx 2/p^2$ separating distinct dynamical regimes.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum-mechanical wave functions in singular potentials: linear and nonlinear states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20282)
*Hidetsugu Sakaguchi,Boris A. Malomed*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文总结了在三维偶极玻色子气体中，通过排斥接触相互作用抑制由吸引奇异反平方势引起的量子坍缩，从而创建缺失基态的理论结果；同时讨论了在r→∞处具有奇异性的排斥势所产生的1D/2D反常束缚态，并展望了此类奇异势下线性与非线性束缚态的研究前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维线性薛定谔方程中的吸引奇异反平方势会引发临界量子坍缩，导致基态缺失。本文旨在展示如何利用排斥接触相互作用（杨振宁-格罗斯-皮塔耶夫斯基理论框架）来抑制这种坍缩并恢复基态，同时探索在无穷远奇异性势下产生的反常束缚态谱。

Method: 采用平均场近似，将排斥接触相互作用表示为Gross-Pitaevskii方程中的三次非线性项。分析偶极玻色子气体（带电偶极矩的玻色子）被中心电荷吸引时的量子行为，同时研究了1D和2D线性薛定谔与GP方程中在r→∞处呈现奇异性的排斥势产生的束缚态。

Result: 排斥接触相互作用能够抑制奇异反平方势引发的量子坍缩，使原本缺失的基态得以创建；此外，在r→∞处增长快于负谐振子势的排斥势，能够产生完整的非直观的可归一化（局域化）束缚态谱。文中还简要考虑了带角动量的激发态。

Conclusion: 文章为奇异势（在r→0或r→∞处奇异）下线性与非线性束缚态的进一步研究提供了方向与展望，展示了此类系统中丰富的量子现象。

Abstract: It is known that the attractive singular inverse-square potential gives rise to the critical quantum collapse in the framework of the three-dimensional (3D) linear Schroedinger equation. This article summarizes theoretical results which demonstrate suppression of the collapse, caused by this singular potential, and the creation of the otherwise missing ground state (GS) in a 3D gas of bosonic particles, carrying an electric dipole moment, which are pulled to the central electric charge, with repulsive contact interactions between the particles. In the mean-field approximation, the repulsive interactions are represented by the cubic term in the respective Gross-Pitaevskii (GP) equation. In addition to the GS, excited states with angular momentum are briefly considered too. Another topic considered in the article is 1D and 2D bound states in the linear Schroedinger and GP equations with the repulsive potential, which demonstrates a singularity at r --> infinity. A very recent result is that such a potential, growing faster than the negative harmonic-oscillator potential, produces a full spectrum of counter-intuitive normalizable (localized) bound states. The article puts forward perspectives for further studies of linear and nonlinear bound states existing under the action of the potentials with the singularity at r --> 0 or r --> infinity.

</details>


### [123] [Proof of the hiding conjecture for Gaussian boson sampling with an arbitrary number of squeezed input modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19314)
*Laura Shou,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Victor Galitski,Sarah H. Miller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完成了高斯玻色采样（GBS）中隐藏猜想（hiding conjecture）的证明，适用于任意数量的压缩模式K。对于任意K和N=o(√K)，证明了对称乘积MK^{-1/2}U_{NK}U_{NK}^T在总变差距离上接近N×N对称复高斯矩阵G，以及GG^T/√K（G为N×K的iid标准复高斯矩阵）。同时还发现Aaronson和Arkhipov的基于密度的实例生成方法在K=cM且c<1/2时失效，因此改用近似实例生成来实现经典硬度归约中的隐藏过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯玻色采样被视为展示量子优势的关键采样任务，其经典硬度论证依赖于隐藏猜想（hiding conjecture）的成立。此前该猜想的证明仅适用于特定情况，本文旨在完成对任意数量压缩模式K的完整证明，从而完善GBS经典不可模拟性的理论依据。

Method: 通过数学分析证明对称乘积MK^{-1/2}U_{NK}U_{NK}^T与N×N对称复高斯矩阵G在总变差距离上的收敛性，涵盖任意K值；同时对比分析Aaronson-Aarkhipov的密度型实例生成方法（Lemma 5.8）与近似实例生成方法在GBS隐藏过程中的适用性条件。

Result: 核心结果表明，对于任意K和N=o(√K)，隐藏性质成立。但发现密度型实例生成方法在K=cM且c<1/2时失败，需要采用近似实例生成方法来实现经典硬度归约中的隐藏程序。

Conclusion: 完成了GBS任意压缩模式数下隐藏猜想的完整证明，同时识别了密度型实例生成方法的适用范围限制，并通过近似实例生成方案解决了该问题，为GBS经典不可模拟性的论证提供了完整且严谨的数学基础。

Abstract: Gaussian boson sampling (GBS) is a sampling task proposed to demonstrate quantum advantage. We consider Gaussian boson sampling on $M$ optical modes, with $K$ equally squeezed input modes and $N$ observed photon counts. We complete the proof of the hiding conjecture for Gaussian boson sampling with an arbitrary number of squeezers $K$, which is a part of the argument for classical hardness of GBS. In particular, we show that for any $K$ and $N=o(\sqrt{K})$, the symmetric product $MK^{-1/2}U_{NK}U_{NK}^T$, for $U_{NK}$ the top left $N\times K$ submatrix of an $M\times M$ Haar random unitary $U$, is close in total variation distance to both an $N\times N$ symmetric complex Gaussian matrix $\mathbf G$ with independent entries, and the symmetric product $GG^T/\sqrt{K}$ for $G$ an $N\times K$ matrix of iid standard complex Gaussians. We show however that the density-based instance generating method of [Aaronson and Arkhipov, Theory Comput. 9, 143 (2013), Lemma 5.8] used to efficiently implement a hiding procedure fails for Gaussian boson sampling with $K=cM$ if $c<1/2$. Instead we use approximate instance generating to implement the hiding for the usual classical hardness reduction.

</details>


### [124] [Phase information beyond entanglement sudden death in coherence-to-entanglement conversion under post-gate noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19247)
*Asad Ali,Hashir Kuniyil,M. T Rahim,Saif Al-kuwari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究后门噪声对CNOT协议产生的相位编码X态中纠缠和相位信息的不同退化行为，发现存在一个纠缠零但相位敏感的精确状态区域，并给出了纠缠死亡时的残余量子Fisher信息及其解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解噪声如何分别影响量子信息处理中的纠缠资源与相位估计能力，特别是两者在噪声下的非同步退化特性，为量子计量和量子信息处理提供参考基准。

Method: 对CNOT门产生的相位编码X态进行理论分析，利用并发度（negativity）刻画纠缠，用量子Fisher信息（QFI）刻画相位灵敏度；引入相干性参数fκ和种群惩罚g两个坐标描述状态空间，分析四类标准噪声通道的轨迹，并比较双量子比特测量与单量子比特探针的精度。

Result: 纠缠度是相干性减种群惩罚的阈值化差值，当fκ≤g时纠缠消失；QFI是平滑比值4z²/(a+b)，在任意非零相干性下为正。因此存在纠缠为零但相位敏感的状态区域。纠缠死亡点的残余QFI为Fφ*=4g*²/(1-2g*)，全局和独立局部去极化通道在最大输入相干性下共享Fφ*=1/6。非对称种群转移增加第三个坐标，改变纠缠但不改变QFI。

Conclusion: 纠缠和相位信息在噪声下不同步退化，纠缠死亡后相位灵敏度依然存在。结果作为相位信息保留的参考基准，而非宣称计量优势。

Abstract: An ideal CNOT maps the phase of a coherent qubit onto the coherence between $\ket{00}$ and $\ket{11}$ of a two-qubit state, producing an output that carries both entanglement and estimable phase information. We ask how post-gate noise degrades these two quantities, and find that they are not lost together. For the phase-encoded X states generated by the protocol, the negativity is a thresholded difference of the surviving coherence $z=fκ$ and a population penalty $g$, vanishing once $fκ\le g$, while the phase quantum Fisher information (QFI) is the smooth ratio $F_φ=4z^2/(a+b)$, which stays positive for any nonzero coherence. As a result there is an exact region of state space in which the output is separable but still phase-sensitive. We characterize this region, give the residual QFI $F_φ^\star=4g_\star^2/(1-2g_\star)$ at entanglement death, and show that channels reaching death at the same coordinate share this residual, with global and independent local depolarization forming one such class and $F_φ^\star=1/6$ at maximal input coherence. Four standard channels appear as trajectories through this common geometry, and asymmetric population transfer adds a third coordinate that changes the entanglement but leaves the QFI unchanged, which marks where the two-coordinate description applies. We identify a measurement that attains the bound and compare with a direct single-qubit probe, which is more precise under matched exposure; the results are therefore reference benchmarks for phase-information retention, not a claim of metrological advantage.

</details>


### [125] [Thermal Throttling of Quantum State Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19315)
*Twesh Upadhyaya,T. C. Mooney,Yifan Hong,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子比特晶格中近似量子态转移在热噪声存在下的基本限制，推导了实现近似态转移所需的算符交换子范数增长的紧下界，并针对一维幂律系统给出了与温度标度相关的代数运行时间界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态转移是量子信息处理中的关键步骤，但在实际系统中，中间辅助位点的热噪声如何阻碍态转移至今不清楚。理解这一基本限制对于实际量子信息处理系统的设计至关重要。

Method: 作者推导了实现近似量子态转移所需的交换子范数增长的紧下界。然后将结果特化到具有热辅助态的一维幂律系统，通过分析温度随系统尺寸的标度关系，将原有对数界改进为代数界。

Result: 研究表明，态转移的运行时间对温度随系统尺寸的标度方式非常敏感。具体而言，通过改进从对数界限到代数界限，揭示了热噪声对近似态转移效率的根本性限制。

Conclusion: 该工作将先前关于精确态转移的结果推广到了性质不同且更贴近实际应用的近似态转移情形，为理解热噪声影响下量子态转移的基本极限提供了严格的理论框架。

Abstract: Quantum state transfer on qubit lattices is a crucial step in a panoply of quantum information processing tasks. Understanding its fundamental limits in the presence of practical imperfections remains a pressing open question. In particular, it is unclear how thermal noise in the intermediate ancilla sites impedes transfer. In this work, we derive tight lower bounds on the necessary growth of commutator norms to achieve approximate state transfer. Specializing to 1D power-law systems with thermal ancilla states, we demonstrate that state transfer runtimes depend sensitively on the scaling of temperature with system size, improving logarithmic bounds to algebraic ones. Our work extends previous results on exact state transfer to the qualitatively different and more practically relevant regime of approximate state transfer.

</details>


### [126] [Demons on a Budget: Adaptive Measurement Placement at the Entanglement Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19248)
*Rohan Pandey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子监控电路中的测量诱导相变，指出测量位置的选择过程（而非仅测量率）是关键的调控参数。固定测量预算时，确定性连续扫描策略将半割熵削减3.4倍，并消除了体积律-面积律相变（表现为p*∝1/L、熵饱和于不依赖L的上限）。扫描的效应来自空间排序而非信息量；学习类策略（交叉熵、PPO）无法复现该策略，因其参数化测量位置而非解决简并分数的排序问题。监控动力学的相图是放置过程而非仅测量速率的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对测量诱导相变的研究将测量率p作为控制参数，测量位置随机放置。作者质疑该范式，提出测量预算固定时，测量位置的放置过程本身是否同样（或更）重要，从而揭示监控电路相图的更完整图景。

Method: 在砖墙随机Clifford电路中固定测量预算，对比随机放置、确定性连续扫描、等覆盖非结构化放置、贪心策略以及交叉熵和PPO训练的学习策略。通过半割熵、三体互信息（TMI）交叉、稳态熵缩放关系等多角度分析；利用stabilizer动力学中每个结果确定性或公平掷硬币的特性，精确计算记录的Shannon熵与记录代价。

Result: （1）确定性连续扫描比随机放置将半割熵削减3.4倍，优于等覆盖非结构化放置和全状态访问贪心策略；（2）扫描效应由空间排序单独承载（测k个最近最少测位置：随机破平局4.14±0.06 bits，位置有序破平局1.29±0.04 bits）；（3）扫描消除而非移动相变：p*∝1/L，稳态熵饱和于约0.46/p的L无关上限，64≤L≤512数据塌缩到S=p⁻¹f(pL)；（4）扫描在熵-记录代价前沿占优，且每次测量代价约1 bit，与随机放置相同；（5）学习策略无法发现扫描，因其参数化测量位置而非简并分数解决顺序。

Conclusion: 测量诱导相变的相图并非仅由测量率决定，而是测量放置过程的本质属性。空间排序（而非信息）主导了纠缠抑制效果，且该效应超出当前基于策略参数化的学习方法所能捕捉的范围。这挑战了该领域以测量率为唯一控制参数的既有框架。

Abstract: Monitored quantum circuits exhibit a measurement-induced phase transition between volume-law and area-law entanglement as a function of the measurement rate $p$. Prior work places measurements at random locations and treats the rate as the control parameter. We instead fix the measurement budget and vary the placement process, comparing random placement against hand-designed and learned policies in brickwork random Clifford circuits at matched budget. First, placement geometry matters more than placement information. A deterministic contiguous sweep cuts the half-cut entropy by a factor of 3.4 relative to random placement, while equal-coverage unstructured placement and a greedy policy with full state access do far worse. The effect is carried by spatial order alone: measuring the $k$ least recently measured sites gives $4.14 \pm 0.06$ bits with random tie-breaking and $1.29 \pm 0.04$ bits with position-ordered tie-breaking. Second, the sweep eliminates the transition rather than shifting it. Tripartite mutual information crossings recede as $p^* \propto 1/L$, the steady-state entropy saturates at an $L$-independent ceiling near $0.46/p$, and data for $64 \le L \le 512$ collapse onto the form $S = p^{-1} f(pL)$ predicted by a ballistic regrowth argument. Third, in stabilizer dynamics every outcome is deterministic or a fair coin flip, so the record's Shannon entropy is exactly countable; the sweep dominates the entropy-versus-record-cost frontier while paying the same roughly one bit per measurement as random placement. Policies trained by cross-entropy and proximal policy optimization do not find the sweep: score-based policies parameterize which sites to measure, not the order in which degenerate scores are resolved, and the effect lives in that order. The phase diagram of monitored dynamics is a property of the placement process, not only of the measurement rate.

</details>


### [127] [Simulating Black Hole Thermality and Interior Scrambling on a Superconducting Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19318)
*Ryan Smith,Ewan Forbes,Iason Sofos Andrew Hallam,Jiannis Pachos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM超导量子硬件上实现了一个手性自旋链黑洞模拟器，在统一的微观框架内同时探测了半经典视界物理与相互作用的量子混沌（scrambling）现象。研究者测量了外域、视界及过倾内域的色散关系，通过视界内部局域激发的密度响应校准了Hawking温度的动态估计器，并区分了自由费米子极限下的非指数算符展宽与强相互作用手性区间的Lyapunov型OTOC衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单一微观框架下统一研究黑洞视界几何、Hawking热性以及相互作用的量子混沌，是量子引力与量子信息交叉领域的核心目标。此前相关工作往往分别处理半经典视界物理与量子混沌，缺乏可在同一可编程平台上同时验证两者的实验方案。

Method: 采用手性自旋链作为黑洞模拟器，部署于IBM超导量子处理器。设计观测特定Floquet电路（源自同一手性母模型及其平均场和坐标等价XY描述）以降低电路深度并保留各探针相关物理。通过色散关系测量探测有效光锥结构演化；通过视界内部局域激发在外部位点的密度响应标定表面引力与Hawking温度；通过连续调节相互作用强度，对比自由费米子非指数展宽与强相互作用手性区间的Lyapunov型OTOC衰变。

Result: 实验成功复现了外域、视界和过倾内域间有效光锥结构的预期演化；观测到密度响应峰值到达时间与表面引力之间符合预期的反比关系，建立了校准的Hawking温度动态估计器；在相互作用可调的情况下，清晰区分了自由费米子极限的非指数算符展宽与强相互作用手性区间的Lyapunov型OTOC指数衰变。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了超导量子硬件可作为统一的、可编程的黑洞模拟平台，同时研究视界几何、Hawking热性与相互作用量子混沌，为未来在量子器件上进行更复杂的量子引力相关模拟奠定了基础。

Abstract: We implement a chiral spin-chain black hole simulator on IBM superconducting quantum hardware and probe, within a common microscopic framework, both semiclassical horizon physics and interacting quantum scrambling. We first measure the dispersion relation across the exterior, horizon and over-tilted interior regimes, reproducing the predicted evolution of the effective light-cone structure. To probe Hawking thermality, we prepare a localised excitation inside the horizon and monitor its density response at an exterior site, observing the predicted inverse relation between the peak arrival time and the surface gravity, thereby establishing a calibrated dynamical estimator of the Hawking temperature. Beyond the semiclassical regime, we continuously tune the interactions and distinguish non-exponential operator spreading in the free-fermion limit from Lyapunov-like OTOC decay in the strongly interacting chiral regime. These measurements use observable-specific Floquet circuits derived from the same parent chiral model, including its mean-field and coordinate-equivalent XY descriptions, to reduce circuit depth while preserving the physics relevant to each probe. Our results provide a unified programmable platform for studying horizon geometry, Hawking thermality and interacting scrambling on quantum hardware.

</details>


### [128] [Adaptive Error Budget Allocation for Fault-Tolerant Quantum Resource Estimation: A Metaheuristic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19249)
*Asif Akhtab Ronggon,Tasnuva Farheen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练的优化框架，直接在Azure Quantum Resource Estimator (AQRE)上进行无导数搜索，实现针对未见过的量子电路的自适应误差预算分配。在433个电路（2-91 qubits，31个家族）上，模拟退火和量子粒子群优化两种元启发式算法平均降低超过33%的时空成本，且两种方法结果差异在1.34%以内，验证了收益的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统级资源估算是容错量子计算工具链的关键环节，其效率取决于全局误差容限在逻辑操作、T态蒸馏和旋转合成之间的分配方式。传统的均匀分配策略忽略电路特定结构，导致非活动或次要子系统的过度预配，造成空间-时间估计膨胀。先前工作使用基于离线生成数据集的监督学习模型来改进，但引入了额外数据生成成本并限制了部署灵活性。因此需要一个免训练、实例特异性的误差预算分配优化框架。

Method: 提出一个训练无关的优化框架，直接在Azure Quantum Resource Estimator (AQRE)上执行无导数（derivative-free）搜索，以针对此前未见过的电路进行实例特定的误差预算分配。为验证对优化器选择的鲁棒性，框架使用两种结构不同的元启发式算法：模拟退火和量子粒子群优化。

Result: 在MQT Bench套件的433个电路（2至91个qubit，31个电路家族）上评估：两种方法平均降低超过33%的时空成本，且结果差异在1.34个百分点以内，表明收益在不同元启发式搜索策略下均稳定。分析发现优化收益主要由误差剖面的不对称性驱动而非电路规模，且Gini系数（优化分配的度量）可作为预期改进的可解释诊断指标。

Conclusion: 自适应误差预算分配可定位为FTQC资源估算流水线中的系统软件优化层，该框架免训练、支持未见电路、对不同优化器鲁棒，并提供了可解释的性能改进诊断指标。

Abstract: System-level resource estimation is a key component of fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) toolchains. Its efficiency depends on how global error tolerance is allocated across logical operations, T-state distillation, and rotation synthesis to minimize physical resource overhead. The commonly used uniform-allocation strategy ignores circuit-specific structure and can overprovision inactive or less critical subsystems, leading to inflated space-time estimates. Prior work aims to address this limitation using supervised models trained on offline-generated datasets. However, this approach incurs additional data-generation costs and limits deployment flexibility. To overcome these drawbacks, we propose a training-free optimization framework that performs derivative-free search directly on the Azure Quantum Resource Estimator (AQRE), enabling instance-specific error budget allocation for previously unseen circuits without requiring offline training data. To evaluate robustness to optimizer choice, we instantiate the framework with two structurally distinct metaheuristics, simulated annealing and quantum particle swarm optimization. We evaluate our framework across 433 circuits spanning 2 to 91 qubits from 31 families in the MQT Bench suite. Across the benchmark suite, both methods reduce space-time cost by more than 33\% on average and agree within 1.34\% points, indicating that the gains are stable across different metaheuristic search strategies. Our analysis further finds that the optimization benefit is driven primarily by error-profile asymmetry rather than circuit scale, and the metric, Gini coefficient of optimized allocation, provides an interpretable diagnostic of expected improvement. Together, these results position adaptive error budget allocation as a system-software optimization layer for FTQC resource estimation pipeline.

</details>


### [129] [Statistical Mechanics of Non-Abelian Learnability Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19325)
*Ruochen Ma,Romain Vasseur*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了1+1维具有SU(2)对称性的监控量子动力学的有效理论，通过复制的环模型描述，并预言"自旋锐化"转变与纠缠转变重合为同一个转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔荷（如SU(2)自旋）守恒的监控量子系统中，观察者如何学习总荷这一问题尚未解决。与阿贝尔情形中不同位点上的荷测量可交换不同，SU(2)对称测量是非对易的融合测量，构成真正的量子推断问题。

Method: 提出一个由复制配对场和携带SU(2)荷的扩散（z=2）背景扇区组成的有效复制环模型；通过大环逸度展开进行受控分析。

Result: 理论预言自旋锐化转变和纠缠转变重合为单一转变：配对场有序时产生体积极律纠缠并将背景扇区隐藏于测量之外，总自旋的学习时间标度为t~L³；配对场无序时，背景扇区单独给出对数纠缠和扩散学习时间标度t~L²。

Conclusion: 该理论首次统一描述SU(2)对称监控动力学中的纠缠与可学习性转变，揭示非阿贝尔荷带来的非对易测量本质上是量子推断问题，并给出了两个不同相中总自旋学习时间的不同标度行为。

Abstract: Monitored many-body quantum systems can undergo sharp learnability transitions characterized by how much information can be learned by the observer. When the dynamics conserves a non-Abelian charge, such as an $SU(2)$ spin, understanding how the observer learns the total charge remains an outstanding problem. Unlike the Abelian case, where charge measurements on distinct sites commute, the $SU(2)$-symmetric readouts are noncommuting fusion measurements, making learning a genuinely quantum inference problem. In this work, we propose a theory of $1+1d$ monitored quantum dynamics with $SU(2)$ symmetry, and show that it can be described by an effective replicated loop model comprised of a replica-pairing field and a diffusive ($z=2$) background sector that carries the $SU(2)$ charge and remains gapless throughout the phase diagram. Our theory predicts that the "spin-sharpening'' and entanglement transitions coincide as a single transition. Ordering of the pairing field produces volume-law entanglement and hides the background sector from measurements, leading to a learning time of $t\sim L^{3}$ for the total spin. When the pairing field disorders, the background sector alone gives logarithmic entanglement and a diffusive learning time $t\sim L^{2}$. Our analysis is controlled by a large-loop-fugacity expansion.

</details>


### [130] [Rethinking Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19370)
*Steven Rayan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以四种相互关联的方式解读量子电路：从门的操作组合出发，发展为可计算的图表示（ZX-calculus），进一步引入量子纠错保护过程，最终将量子电路几何化（通过双曲量子码和超导电路实验）。文章从比特和量子比特的基础开始，涵盖Deutsch和Grover算法，无需量子信息先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过四种递进的视角（操作性、图式性、保护性、几何性）统一理解量子电路，并将数学理论（ZX-calculus、量子纠错码）与物理实验（超导电路）相结合，为量子电路赋予几何层面的解读，同时确保内容对无量子信息背景的读者开放。

Method: 文章采用教学式递进结构：(1) 从比特/量子比特基础出发，以Deutsch和Grover算法作为量子电路的入门实例；(2) 将量子电路图式化，引入紧致闭合弦图和ZX-calculus；(3) 通过Knill--Laflamme条件、同调表面码等概念介绍量子纠错，并将纠错视为图操作；(4) 探讨双曲量子码的性质及用超导电路模拟负曲率晶格以支持这些码的物理实验。

Result: 文章提供了一套从操作层面到几何层面的量子电路统一解读框架，展示了ZX图在理论纠错和物理设备实验中如何相互印证，将数学抽象（双曲量子码）与实验物理（超导电路）联系起来，实现了对量子电路几何层的完整阐述。

Conclusion: 作者总结认为，这些数学思想和物理实验共同代表了一种将几何层赋予量子电路的方式；文中后期虽包含原创研究且数学性逐渐增强，但通篇不假设读者具有量子信息、量子计算或量子纠错的先验知识，使内容具有自足性。

Abstract: These notes develop four interconnected ways of reading a quantum circuit. A circuit for us begins as an operational composition of gates; then, it becomes a diagram whose local equalities may be used as calculations; next, it becomes a protected process once errors, syndromes, and logical degrees of freedom are separated; and finally, it becomes geometric when its connectivity, topology, and boundary data are treated as physical design parameters. The development begins at the level of bits and qubits before appealing to Deutsch's and Grover's algorithms as basic examples of quantum circuits. With the basics in hand, we interpret quantum circuits diagramatically, leading us to compact closed string diagrams and the ZX-calculus. After that, we consider how to correct quantum circuits by introducing the Knill--Laflamme condition, homological surface codes, and related concepts with a view towards thinking of these as operations on diagrams. The lectures eventually arrive at the properties of hyperbolic quantum codes and the prospect of physical superconducting circuits emulating the negatively-curved lattices needed to support those codes. These mathematical ideas and physical experiments, taken together, represent one way to impart a geometric layer onto quantum circuits. By the very end, we bring the ideas nearly full circle by assessing the extent to which these device physics experiments operationalize the basic ZX diagrams encountered much earlier in the story. While the later material reports on original research, and while the discussion becomes increasingly mathematical as the sections progress, no prior knowledge of quantum information, quantum computing, or quantum error correction is actually assumed.

</details>


### [131] [Quantum Gaussian processes for prediction of channel observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19306)
*Jonas Jäger,Yaroslav Khmelnitskiy,Paolo Braccia,Artur Miroszewski,Diego García-Martín,M. Cerezo,Piotr Czarnik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了量子高斯过程（QGP）回归框架，使其不再局限于酉演化，而是适用于一般量子信道。作者证明了在均匀（Lebesgue）先验下信道输出收敛到QGP并推导出闭式核函数，同时针对核中维度因子导致的指数抑制问题，提出了经验贝叶斯启发式方法（用可学习尺度参数替代维度因子）。在多达64量子比特的数值模拟中验证了方法的有效性，并在真实噪声量子计算机上展示了鲁棒性，还将其应用于带噪声XXZ动力学下态制备的贝叶斯优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的QGP回归仅适用于酉演化，无法处理更一般的量子信道（开放量子系统、噪声动力学等）。此外，Lebesgue先验下的核函数含有维度因子，当子系统尺度随系统大小扩展时会引发指数级抑制，导致无法学习。因此需要扩展QGP框架并解决该可学习性问题。

Method: 1) 将QGP框架推广到非酉动力学：在均匀先验（Lebesgue测度）下证明信道输出收敛到QGP，并推导闭式核函数；2) 分析核中维度因子对观测精度的影响，指出当子系统扩展时产生指数抑制；3) 提出经验贝叶斯启发式，用可学习尺度参数替代维度因子，保留核的状态重叠相关结构；4) 在64量子比特上对局部和全局信道进行数值模拟，并在噪声量子计算机上进行验证；5) 将QGP作为贝叶斯优化代理应用于带噪声XXZ动力学下的态制备。

Result: 1) Lebesgue核在64量子比特局部信道上表现出强归纳偏置，可实现可靠的预测外推；2) 对于全局64量子比特信道，重标定核恢复可学习性，预测随测量预算增加而系统改善；3) 噪声量子计算机实验验证了QGP回归在实际条件下的鲁棒性；4) QGP作为贝叶斯优化代理在带噪声XXZ动力学下的态制备任务中有效。

Conclusion: QGP回归成功从酉演化扩展到一般量子信道，经验贝叶斯重标定有效地解决了维度因子导致的指数抑制问题，使全局信道的学习成为可能。该方法在大系统尺寸和噪声环境下均表现出良好的鲁棒性，并可作为贝叶斯优化的有效代理，为量子信道回归与优化提供了统一的框架。

Abstract: Given a set of input states, we consider the task of predicting the expectation value of a Pauli observable at the output of an unknown quantum evolution, using only a limited number of measurements. Recently, quantum Gaussian process (QGP) regression was introduced for this task across various classes of unitary evolution. Here, we extend the QGP framework beyond unitary dynamics. In particular, we prove convergence of the channel's outputs to a QGP and derive the associated closed-form kernel under a uniform (Lebesgue measure) prior over quantum channels. The kernel's dimensional factor, however, dictates the required observation precision. While manageable when the channel and observable are restricted to small subsystems, exponential suppression precludes learning when the subsystem grows extensively with the system size. Since the Lebesgue prior is overly broad for many applications, we propose an empirical Bayes heuristic that replaces the dimensional factor with a learnable scale parameter while retaining the kernel's state-overlap correlation structure. In numerical simulations of up to 64 qubits, channel QGP regression with the Lebesgue kernel exhibits a strong inductive bias for local channels, enabling faithful extrapolation. For global 64-qubit channels, the rescaled kernel restores learnability, with predictions improving systematically with the shot budget. Results from a noisy quantum computer further demonstrate the robustness of QGP regression under experimental conditions. Beyond regression, we validate QGPs as Bayesian-optimization surrogates for state preparation under noisy XXZ dynamics.

</details>


### [132] [Efficient Classical Simulation of Weakly Interacting Fermion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19448)
*Chu Zhao,Iman Marvian,Yu Tong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多项式时间的经典算法，用于模拟弱相互作用费米子系统的实时动力学。通过分析海森堡绘景中算符随相互作用强度λ的微扰展开收敛性，证明在D维几何局域格点系统上，当λ|t|^{2D+1}=O(1)时（该条件与系统大小无关），可以高效估计局部可观测量期望值；若目标是在归一化Frobenius范数下近似时间演化算符，收敛域可扩展至λ|t|=O(1)（准多项式时间）；当非相互作用部分表现出安德森局域化时，多项式算法可进一步扩展到λ|t|=O(1)（忽略多对数因子）。算法融合了连续时间量子蒙特卡洛、图解量子蒙特卡洛和Majorana传播思想，通过新颖的海森堡绘景算符增长分析实现采样复杂度的严格可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟强关联费米子系统实时动力学是量子多体物理的核心难题。已有的量子蒙特卡洛方法通常面临符号问题和动力学指数增长瓶颈，而经典方法在实时演化领域缺乏严格高效保证。本文旨在利用弱相互作用、几何局域性和安德森局域化等物理特性，为实时费米子动力学提供可严格证明的经典可解性突破，填补弱相互作用区域中缺乏系统尺寸无关的经典算法空白。

Method: 本文的核心方法论在于：(1) 在海森堡绘景中对局部可观测量A(t)进行关于相互作用强度λ的微扰级数展开，并系统分析该级数的收敛性，尤其关注算符增长（operator growth）行为对收敛半径的限制；(2) 结合连续时间量子蒙特卡洛（continuous-time QMC）、图解量子蒙特卡洛（diagrammatic QMC）和Majorana传播（Majorana Propagation）三种技术路线，设计采样算法；(3) 引入新颖的海森堡绘景算符增长分析，确保采样方差与系统尺寸无关，从而严格控制采样复杂度；(4) 利用光锥结构（D维格点局域性导致的有限速度传播）来约束微扰展开中有效贡献的阶数。

Result: 主要结果包括三个层次：(1) 对于局部可观测量期望值估计，在λ|t|^{2D+1}=O(1)条件下实现多项式时间经典算法，该收敛条件独立于系统大小；(2) 对于归一化Frobenius范数意义下逼近时间演化算符，收敛域扩展到λ|t|=O(1)，运行时间为准多项式；(3) 在安德森局域化存在时，多项式时间算法可扩展到λ|t|=O(1)（含多对数因子）。所有算法均具有严格的采样复杂度上界，且采样方差独立于系统尺寸。

Conclusion: 本文证明在弱相互作用、几何局域性和/或安德森局域化的共同作用下，实时费米子动力学存在宽广的经典可解区域。这些结果不仅提供了严格的复杂度保证，也揭示了物理机制（微扰收敛性、光锥限制、局域化）如何决定量子模拟的经典可解性边界，为后续量子优势问题的边界划定和更优算法设计提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We consider the task of simulating the real-time dynamics of weakly interacting fermionic systems. In particular, we focus on computing the expectation value of a local observable $A$ at time $t$. By analyzing the convergence of the perturbative expansion in the interaction strength $λ$ for the Heisenberg-picture observable, we propose a polynomial-time algorithm for estimating this expectation value in the weakly interacting regime $λ|t|^{2D+1}=\mathcal{O}(1)$, when the Hamiltonian is geometrically local on a $D$-dimensional lattice. Importantly, this condition is independent of the system size. If the goal is instead to approximate the time-evolved observable in normalized Frobenius norm, we extend the convergence regime to $λ|t|=\mathcal{O}(1)$ with quasi-polynomial runtime. When the non-interacting part exhibits Anderson localization, our polynomial-time algorithm can be extended up to $λ|t|=\mathcal{O}(1)$, modulo polylogarithmic factors. Our algorithm brings together ideas from continuous-time QMC, diagrammatic QMC, and Majorana Propagation, but with a new Heisenberg-picture operator-growth analysis that makes the sampling complexity rigorously controllable. This leads to provably efficient classical algorithms in regimes where the interaction is weak enough that the sampling variance remains bounded independently of system size. Together, these results identify broad regimes in which weak interactions, locality, and localization can be leveraged to make real-time fermionic dynamics classically tractable.

</details>


### [133] [Is the Quantum-Entangled Universe a Small World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19459)
*Gregory S. Duane*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出粒子间的部分纠缠可能为宇宙提供足够连接以满足"小世界"网络定义。通过在类空超曲面上定义粒子网络，以交换粒子链的交互次数作为连接准则，发现除恒星和行星等最小尺度外，宇宙网络在几乎所有尺度上呈现小世界或随机网络结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索宇宙是否具有"小世界"网络结构，即任何两个粒子之间是否通过少量中间连接即可关联。该问题关乎宇宙基本层面的连通性，且此前缺乏针对宇宙物理网络结构的研究。

Method: 在给定的类空超曲面上定义粒子网络：两个粒子之间存在长程连接当且仅当它们通过一条少于某个小最大交互次数的交换粒子链相连。基于不同空间区域粒子的平均自由程，计算纠缠连接随距离的概率分布，以此分析网络的小世界特征。

Result: 发现除最小尺度（对应恒星和行星）外，宇宙网络在所有尺度上均呈现小世界或随机网络结构。在恒星和行星尺度上，需要添加其他类型的连接才能完善小世界图像。

Conclusion: 部分纠缠足以在绝大多数宇宙尺度上构建小世界网络，但恒星和行星尺度上的连接机制需另行补充。该研究为理解宇宙物理网络结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Partial entanglement may provide enough connections in the universe to satisfy the definition of a small world. To investigate this possibility, we define a network of particles on a given space-like hyper-surface with a long-range link between any two particles that are connected by a chain of exchanged particles involving less than some small maximum number of interactions. Considering the mean free paths of particles in different regions of space and the resulting probability distributions of entanglement connections vs. distance, we find evidence of small-world or random network structure on all but the smallest scales - corresponding to stars and planets - on which other types of connections would need to be added to complete the small world picture.

</details>


### [134] [Glassy dynamics with softened kinetic constraints on a noisy quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19335)
*Marcel Cech,Igor Lesanovsky,Federico Carollo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在超导量子处理器上实现了Floquet-East模型，利用中途测量获取轨迹级观测，发现了玻璃态动力学特有的动态异质性，并通过时空无活性区域的面积/周长标度交叉验证了动态一阶相变的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多体系统的集合平均量通常掩盖了轨迹层面的动力学结构。中途测量能够直接访问轨迹级可观测量的视角，为研究隐藏的动力学现象提供了新途径。

Method: 在超导量子处理器上实现Floquet-East模型，结合中途测量、动力学约束幺正操作和硬件噪声，对时空分辨的中途测量轨迹进行统计分析，研究无活性时空区域的尺寸概率分布。

Result: 识别出动态异质性这一玻璃态动力学标志；无活性区域概率呈现从面积主导到周长主导的标度交叉，该特征是玻璃体的特性，且与动态一阶相变的邻近性相关。

Conclusion: 当前含噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）设备可作为可扩展的试验平台，用于在测量轨迹层面研究关联性多体现象。

Abstract: Mid-circuit measurements provide direct access to trajectory-level observables, revealing dynamical structures in many-body systems that are invisible in ensemble-averaged quantities. We exploit this capability to realize and study an instance of the Floquet-East model on a superconducting quantum processor. Here, the combination of mid-circuit measurements, kinetically constrained unitary operations and hardware noise gives rise to intricate many-body phenomena. Analyzing trajectories obtained from temporally and spatially resolved mid-circuit measurements, we identify dynamical heterogeneity --- a hallmark of glassy dynamics. We quantify this emergent behavior by studying the probability of finding inactive space-time regions of a given size. This quantity displays a crossover from an area- to perimeter-dominated scaling, which is a characteristic property of glasses and is associated with the proximity to a dynamical first-order phase transition. Our results establish current noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices as scalable testbeds for investigating correlated many-body phenomena at the level of measurement trajectories.

</details>


### [135] [Beyond Integrability Preserving Renormalization-Group Protocol in Non-Hermitian Hamiltonians with Time-Dependent Interaction Strengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19519)
*Parameshwar R. Pasnoori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了时间依赖可积模型的范围，涵盖具有时变相互作用强度的非厄米量子模型。利用广义Bethe ansatz框架，证明可积性施加的约束更为宽泛：在RG中流动的相互作用强度遵循RG轨迹，而RG不变的强度可以是常数或具有可积性约束的特定时间依赖，从而建立了非厄米时间依赖系统中可积性保持的强度集合大于RG协议对应的集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，在时间依赖量子系统中，保持可积性的时变相互作用强度与对应静态模型的重整化群（RG）轨迹一致。作者希望将这一结论扩展到非厄米量子模型，并探索非厄米系统中可积性约束是否允许更广泛的时间依赖形式。

Method: 利用最近提出的广义Bethe ansatz框架（Pasnoori, Phys. Rev. B 112, L060409 (2025)），分析非厄米时变系统中可积性对相互作用强度时间依赖形式的约束，并将结果与RG协议的预测进行比较。

Result: 结果表明，在非厄米时间依赖系统中，可积性保持的时间依赖强度集合比RG协议对应的集合更大。具体而言，静态模型中RG流动的强度遵循RG轨迹，而RG不变的强度既可以保持常数，也可以具有由可积性约束的特定时间依赖形式。

Conclusion: 作者建立了非厄米时间依赖系统中可积模型的新分类：除RG协议外，还存在更广泛的受可积性约束的时间依赖形式，这扩展了非Hermitian可积模型的整体图景。

Abstract: It is well established that in time-dependent quantum systems, integrability preserving time-dependent interaction strengths are identical to the renormalization group (RG) trajectories of the corresponding static model when time `$t$' in the driven model is identified with the logarithm of the cutoff `$\logΛ$' of the static model. We refer to this integrability preserving driving as the RG protocol. In this work we extend the class of time-dependent integrable models to include non-Hermitian quantum models with time-dependent interaction strengths. Using the recently formulated generalized Bethe ansatz framework [P. R. Pasnoori, Phys. Rev. B 112, L060409 (2025)], we show that the constraints imposed by integrability are more general: The interaction strengths of the static model that flow in the RG follow the respective RG trajectories in the corresponding time-dependent model as described above. In addition, the interaction strengths of the static model that are RG invariant can either be constant or have a specific time-dependence in the corresponding time-dependent model which is constrained by integrability. Thus we establish that in the context of time-dependent non-Hermitian systems, the set of integrability preserving time-dependent strengths is larger than the set corresponding to the RG protocol.

</details>


### [136] [Multipolar Light-Matter Hamiltonians in Symmetry-Breaking Photonic Vacuums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19336)
*Liu Yang,Jiadu Lin,Qing-Dong Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明当光子真空破坏空间反演或时间反演对称性时，传统的多极哈密顿量需要被定性修正。通过显式应用Power-Zienau-Woolley变换，推导了空间手性真空和时间手性真空两种情况下的多极哈密顿量，并展示了由此产生的对称性依赖的能谱移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多极哈密顿量建立在常规光子真空假设之上，但当光子环境具有手性（破坏空间反演或时间反演对称性）时，标准的处理框架可能失效。作者旨在建立一个统一框架来描述手性量子电动力学中的光-物质相互作用，并识别由此产生的对称性依赖效应。

Method: 显式应用Power-Zienau-Woolley变换推导多极哈密顿量。针对两种理想化手性光子环境——空间手性真空（破坏反演对称性）和时间手性真空（破坏时间反演对称性），分别推导哈密顿量的修正项。使用囚禁的氢原子和带电谐振子作为最小示例来验证理论预测。

Result: 空间手性真空情形下，变换产生一个破坏反演对称性的自能项；时间手性真空情形下，产生附加的类塞曼能量。在两种最小模型中，这些对称性依赖项均导致特征性的光谱移动。

Conclusion: 该工作为手性量子电动力学中的光-物质相互作用提供了通用框架，能够识别并描述腔中原子、分子和量子材料上对称性依赖的效应，拓展了传统多极哈密顿量的适用范围。

Abstract: We show that the conventional multipolar Hamiltonian is qualitatively modified when the photonic vacuum breaks inversion or time-reversal symmetry. By explicitly applying the Power-Zienau-Woolley transformation, we derive the resulting multipolar Hamiltonians for two idealized chiral photonic environments: a spatial-chiral vacuum, which breaks inversion symmetry, and a temporal-chiral vacuum, which breaks time-reversal symmetry. In the spatial-chiral case, the transformation generates an inversion-breaking self-energy, whereas in the temporal-chiral case it produces an additional Zeeman-like energy. Using a trapped hydrogen-like atom and a charged harmonic oscillator in cavities as minimal examples, we show that these symmetry-dependent terms lead to characteristic spectral shifts. Our work provides a general framework for describing light-matter interactions in chiral quantum electrodynamics and identifying the associated symmetry-dependent effects on cavity-embedded atoms, molecules, and quantum materials.

</details>


### [137] [A charge selection rule fixes what a squeezed-light reservoir computer can compute and afford](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19668)
*Daniel Soh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上揭示了光学量子储备池的计算边界：读取重复次数随特征阶数呈超指数增长，其可读取性由探测器而非光学元件决定。通过对参量泵浦相位编码的储备池，证明了序数D读出恰好达到至多D个单位电荷核的集合；弱压缩族下的一次谐波零差读出对衰减记忆泛函具有普适性，代价是随精度多项式增长的射击数；而固定压缩度下任何有限阶都无法覆盖所有扇区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解光学量子储备池的实际计算能力与成本限制，澄清光学元件与探测器各自承担的角色，为量子储备池的硬件设计提供理论指导。

Method: 通过守恒律（成对光子交换保持整数相位电荷）推导读出极限；利用序数D读出、一次谐波零差读出等不同测量方案进行分析；并借助硬件保真的数字孪生（未建造实际器件）在开放RF语料上验证，比较位移编码与电荷代数预测的差异。

Result: 序数D读出恰好达到至多D个单位电荷核的集合；弱压缩族下一次谐波零差读出的射击成本随精度呈多项式增长；固定压缩度时任何有限阶均达不到所有扇区且距离可计算；数字孪生验证中，同光子数下的位移编码控制损失17.7准确率点，符合电荷代数的预测。

Conclusion: 光学量子储备池的计算能力根本上受限于探测器的读取方案，而非光学非线性本身；电荷守恒代数提供了精确刻画可读取边界与成本的统一框架，为实际系统的性能预算提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Reading an optical quantum reservoir costs repetitions growing super-exponentially with feature order: what it can afford is set by its detector, not its optics. For reservoirs encoding data in a parametric pump's phase, one conservation law fixes what is readable and what it costs. Pairwise photon exchange conserves an integer phase charge: across an ensemble of input masks, order-D readout reaches exactly the assemblies of at most D unit-charge kernels; degree-one homodyne readout is universal for fading-memory functionals along weak-squeezing families, at shot cost polynomial in accuracy; and at fixed squeezing no finite degree reaches every sector, at a computable distance. Nonlinearity sits in the optics, not the detector, whose per-shot variance is fixed at every order. In a hardware-faithful digital twin-no device was built-the rule is visible: on an open RF corpus a displacement-encoded control at identical photon number loses 17.7 accuracy points, as the charge algebra predicts.

</details>


### [138] [Stability and squeezing of the three-photon degenerate parametric down-conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19494)
*Vinícius V. Seco,Alencar J. de Faria*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究腔内简并三光子参量下转换过程，该过程由经典泵浦驱动、相干模刺激并受腔自发辐射耗散影响。使用相空间方法和随机微分方程求解，发现系统不具有多稳态性，仅存在单个稳定稳态分支和多个不稳定分支。通过线性化动力学计算正交变量的谱密度，表征三光子下转换的压缩特性，并利用正Wigner函数方法计算谱密度以研究系统的非高斯性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性多光子转换过程在量子光学和量子信息领域具有重要的理论和实验价值，能够产生显著的量子性质。基于近期实验进展以及广义三光子压缩的潜在应用，研究者希望深入理解腔内三光子参量下转换的稳态性质、稳定性条件以及压缩和非高斯量子特性。

Method: 采用相空间方法将量子主方程映射为随机微分方程，分析系统稳态及其稳定性条件；在稳定稳态附近线性化动力学，计算正交变量谱密度以表征压缩特性；针对高阶多光子过程中准概率微分方程含高阶导数、难以映射为标准随机微分方程的困难，采用正Wigner函数方法计算与三时关联函数相关的二维谱密度，以研究非高斯性质。

Result: 发现系统不具备多稳态性，仅存在有限的参数区域支持单个稳定稳态分支，伴随多个不稳定分支；在三光子下转换中识别并表征了正交变量的压缩；利用正Wigner函数方法成功计算了二维谱密度，为研究系统的非高斯量子性质提供了可行框架。

Conclusion: 腔内简并三光子参量下转换系统在特定参数条件下存在单一稳定稳态并表现出压缩特性，正Wigner函数方法为处理高阶多光子过程的非高斯量子性质提供了有效的理论工具，对量子信息和量子计算中的三光子压缩应用具有参考价值。

Abstract: Nonlinear multiphoton conversion processes are theoretically and experimentally challenging systems in quantum optics and related areas. These phenomena bring remarkable quantum properties and can be of fundamental relevance in quantum information and computation applications. Based on recent experimental advances and the possible uses in generalized three-photon squeezing, we investigate degenerate three-photon parametric down-conversion generated inside a cavity fed by a classical pump. In addition, we consider that the process is stimulated by a coherent driving mode and subjected to dissipation by spontaneous emission of the cavity. A treatment using phase-space methods and stochastic differential equations is applied and the stability conditions of its steady states are found. We find that the system does not exhibit multistability, instead it has a limited parameter region leading to a single stable steady branch, alongside multiple unstable branches. Focusing on the stable steady states and linearizing the dynamics around them, we calculate the spectral densities of the quadrature variables. We identify and characterize the squeezing of three-photon down-conversion. Moreover, since the quasi-probability differential equations of the higher-order multiphoton processes have higher-order derivatives, hindering their mapping into stochastic differential equations, we make use of a positive Wigner function approach to calculate two-dimensional spectral densities associated with three-time correlation functions, with the goal of studying non-Gaussian properties of the system.

</details>


### [139] [Geometric phase of open paths and a geodesic-selection rule at a level degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19679)
*Hyeonseok Yang,Changsuk Noh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决开放路径几何相位中测地线闭合规则的不确定性问题。当控制场扫过能级简并点时，路径在Bloch球面上形成端点对跖的开放曲线。作者通过坐标无关的磁单极联络内在地定义开放路径立体角Ω[C]，并证明几何相位与包围立体角的等价性仅在唯一的闭合测地线（密切平面内的大圆，α=0）上成立。作为推论，得到了Berry π不变量和±π/2反射对称值，并证明纯态极限下的有限温度Uhlmann相位自动选择密切平面闭合，将文献中的启发式闭合规则转化为可计算的处方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放路径几何相位的传统测地线闭合规则存在内在歧义：当路径端点对跖时，闭合该路径的测地线有无穷多条，且不同闭合方式包围不同的立体角，导致几何相位不能唯一定义。这一模糊性阻碍了开放路径几何相位的精确计算和理论应用。

Method: 作者采用坐标无关的磁单极联络定义开放路径立体角Ω[C]，使其不依赖坐标选择而内在地确定。然后通过将简并点位移εû闭合路径，推导出闭合立体角Ω(εû)=Ω[C]+2α+O(ε)的封闭解表达式，其中α为û横向部分相对于控制曲线主法线的方位角。该公式表明只有唯一一条闭合测地线——密切平面内的大圆（α=0），由简并点处的曲率提供——才能使几何相位等于包围立体角。

Result: 论文获得了开放路径几何相位的明确解析解：几何相位-立体角恒等式仅在密切平面闭合（α=0）时成立。由此推导出两个重要推论：Berry的π在位移反转下的不变性，以及反射对称下的±π/2取值。此外，有限温度Uhlmann相位的纯态极限自动选择密切平面闭合，验证了理论的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文完全消解了开放路径几何相位的闭合歧义，给出了唯一正确的测地线闭合方案（密切平面大圆），并将文献中长期使用的启发式闭合规则提升为基于微分几何的严格可计算处方。这一结果对量子控制、偏振光学和几何量子计算等领域中开放路径几何相位的精确预测具有重要价值。

Abstract: When the control field of a qubit, a polarization state, or a spin-$\tfrac12$ system is swept through a level degeneracy, its direction traces an open curve on the Bloch sphere whose endpoints are antipodal, and the geodesic rule for the open-path geometric phase becomes ambiguous: infinitely many geodesics close the path, and different closures enclose different solid angles. We resolve this ambiguity in closed form. A coordinate-free monopole connection defines the open-path solid angle $Ω[C]$ intrinsically, and displacing the degeneracy by $ε\uhat$ closes the path with enclosed solid angle $Ω(ε\uhat)=Ω[C]+2α+O(ε)$, where $α$ is the azimuth of the transverse part of $\uhat$ measured from the principal normal of the control curve at the crossing. The identity between geometric phase and enclosed solid angle therefore holds for exactly one closing geodesic---the great circle in the osculating plane ($α=0$)---supplied by the curvature at the degeneracy. Berry's $π$ invariant under reversal of the displacement and the values $\pmπ/2$ under a reflection symmetry follow as corollaries, and the pure-state limit of the finite-temperature Uhlmann phase selects the osculating-plane closure automatically, turning the heuristic closing rules of the open-path literature into a computable prescription.

</details>

*Patryk Michalski,Jerzy Paczos,Navdeep Arya,Magdalena Zych*

TL;DR: 本文分析了引力波背景下，通过移动腔镜模拟的调制边界条件对强耦合原子-腔场相互作用的影响，证明了调制会产生共振增强效应，在原子跃迁概率中留下可测量的印记，为在量子光学系统中探测类广义相对论效应提供了可实现平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一个既能体现量子效应又能体现广义相对论效应的平台——引力波背景中与电磁场相互作用的原子。特别是，平面引力波引起的模式频率时间调制可等效为调制边界条件（如移动腔镜），本文旨在分析这种调制对强原子-腔耦合体系中原子-场相互作用的影响。

Method: 在强原子-腔耦合（Rabi振荡发生）区域中，对由引力波诱导的调制边界条件引起的场模式频率周期变化进行解析分析，研究该调制对原子跃迁概率的影响。

Result: 解析证明该调制会得到共振增强，在原子跃迁概率中产生可测量的印记，从而确立了探测类广义相对论效应的实验可行平台。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了现实且可实验访问的量子光学平台，用于模拟和探测类比广义相对论效应，将量子光学与引力物理在强耦合体系中联系起来。

Abstract: One of the proposed platforms in which both quantum and general relativistic effects can become observable is an atom interacting with the electromagnetic field in a gravitational-wave background. The periodic modulation of field modes induced by variations of the spacetime metric modifies the atomic emission spectrum. Notably, the temporal modulation of the mode-frequency induced by a plane gravitational wave can be simulated through modulated boundary conditions, such as moving cavity mirrors. We analyze the impact of this modulation on atom-field interactions in the strong atom-cavity coupling regime, where Rabi oscillations occur. We show analytically that the modulation is resonantly enhanced, leading to measurable imprints in the atomic transition probability. This establishes a realistic and experimentally accessible platform for probing analogue general relativistic effects in quantum optical systems.

</details>


### [141] [Krylov Tomography and Finite-Uncertainty Certification of Exceptional-Point Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19761)
*Aritra Ghosh,M. Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Krylov层析成像技术，一种考虑制备和测量感知的框架，利用时间分辨数据和带不确定性边界的模型来量化激发对奇异点（EP）动力学的访问程度、检测器可区分的响应，以及区分信号缺失与未检测，并通过红边带光机模型的有限精度模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异点（EP）能产生显著响应，但仅仅定位一个EP并不能揭示：激发能访问其动力学的多少、检测器能区分哪些响应，以及缺失信号究竟是不存在还是未被检测到。这些问题需要一种制备与测量感知的框架来回答。

Method: 提出Krylov层析成像框架，利用时间分辨数据并结合具有明确不确定性限度的模型来回答上述问题，同时给出偏移引起的偏离精确EP行为的边界。作为具体示例，对红边带光力模型进行有限精度模拟，该模型的二阶相干度扇区包含三阶奇异点，用于认证制备敏感地访问两个和三个方向。

Result: 在红边带光力模型中成功认证了制备敏感访问两个和三个EP方向，验证了Krylov层析成像框架在连接EP结构与有限精度测量方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Krylov层析成像弥合了EP结构与有限精度测量之间的鸿沟，为非厄米系统提供了一种通用的分析框架，能够量化EP动力学的可访问性与可观测性。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) can produce striking responses, but locating one does not reveal how much of its dynamics an excitation accesses, which responses a detector distinguishes, or whether a missing signal is absent or undetected. We introduce Krylov tomography, a preparation- and measurement-aware framework that uses time-resolved data and models with stated uncertainty limits to answer these questions, while also bounding offset-induced departures from exact-EP behavior. As an explicit illustration of the general framework, we present finite-precision simulations of a red-sideband optomechanical model, whose second-moment coherence sector contains a third-order EP, certifying preparation-sensitive access to two and three directions. Krylov tomography thus bridges EP structure and finite-precision measurements, providing a general framework for non-Hermitian systems.

</details>

*Jonathan Daniel,Martin Y. Kim,Jesse Hernandez,Emanuel Suarez,Sangwoo Lee,Jinhee Lee,Je-Hyung Kim*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV色心系综磁强计在无屏蔽环境中具有鲁棒矢量测量能力，但部署受限于复杂的校准流程和对外部数据参考的依赖。传统统计机器学习需要海量训练数据，且存在严重的仿真到现实（sim-to-real）不匹配问题，亟需一种数据高效且能适配真实硬件非理想性的方法。

Method: 提出物理引导的混合机器学习框架：将塞曼分裂的物理模型直接嵌入学习管线；采用混合架构，将稀疏物理测量与可扩展的合成数据生成相结合，并纳入真实硬件非理想性；模型用于解码未标定的原始ODMR实验数据。

Result: 物理引导模型显著降低了平均跟踪误差，相比纯统计基线实现了372倍的精度提升；在实际未标定原始ODMR数据上实现了标量磁场的高精度预测。

Conclusion: 该工作为自校准传感器铺平了道路，同时建立了一种适用于其他数据稀缺物理系统的机器学习训练方法。

Abstract: Ensemble nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond enable robust vector magnetometry in unshielded environments, yet deployment remains bottlenecked by complex calibration and a reliance on external data references. Conventional statistical machine learning requires an exorbitantly large volume of training data and suffers from severe simulation-to-reality mismatches. To address this, we introduce a physics-guided hybrid machine learning framework that embeds the Zeeman splitting directly into the learning pipeline. Our physics-guided model significantly reduces the average tracking error demonstrating a 372-fold precision improvement over purely statistical baselines. Furthermore, our hybrid architecture pairs a sparse physical measurement with scalable synthetic data generation, seamlessly incorporating real-world hardware non-idealities. When deployed to decode uncalibrated, raw experimental ODMR data, our framework delivers exceptional predictive accuracy for the scalar magnetic field. This work paves the way toward self-calibrated sensors while establishing a machine learning training method applicable to other data-scarce physical systems

</details>


### [143] [Hyperon-antihyperon system in electron-positron annihilation as quantum probes for temperature estimation with local and global dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19344)
*Anass Hminat,Abdallah Slaoui,Rachid Ahl Laamara,Hichem Eleuch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在可精确求解的纯退相框架下，利用两量子比特探针研究Ohmic型储层中的量子测温学。通过分析温度估计的方差，确定了由Ohmicity参数s、偏离角θ及衰减系数α和β控制的优化区间。结果表明QFI在有限相互作用时间出现显著峰值（尤其在亚Ohmic和Ohmic环境低温下），QSNR在高温区饱和；共同浴配置（特别是Σ⁺和Σ⁰探针）在短时间优于局域浴，而局域环境在长时间占优。同时发现超子-反超子通道中纠缠和量子失协在宽角度域内保持鲁棒，而导引和Bell非局域性仅限于较窄区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测温学是量子计量学的核心问题之一，旨在利用量子系统作为探针精确测量温度。理解不同光谱结构（Ohmic型储层）和粒子相关参数对温度估计精度的影响，对于提高量子温度计的灵敏度和设计最优测量策略具有重要理论和实际意义。

Method: 采用两量子比特探针耦合于Ohmic型储层的可精确求解纯退相模型，通过计算单次测量的方差、量子Fisher信息（QFI）和量子信噪比（QSNR）来表征温度估计精度。系统研究了Ohmicity参数s、偏离角θ、衰减系数α和β的影响，并比较了共同浴与局域浴两种估计策略，同时考察了五种超子-反超子通道（Λ、Σ⁺、Σ⁰、Ξ⁻、Ξ⁰）的量子关联特性。

Result: 亚Ohmic和Ohmic环境在低温下QFI在有限时间出现明显峰值；超Ohmic储层使QFI峰值平坦化并移向高温。QSNR在低温被抑制、随热激发增加并在高温区饱和。共同浴配置在短时间优于局域浴（归因于浴诱导关联），局域环境在长时间更有利。存在有限的最优相互作用时间t_opt和温度T_opt，低温下增加测量次数可显著降低方差。超子-反超子通道中纠缠和量子失协在宽角度域鲁棒，而导引和Bell非局域性局限于窄区域。

Conclusion: 光谱结构、粒子依赖参数与估计策略之间的相互作用为优化量子测温方案提供了有价值的指导，明确了在不同环境类型和探针配置下实现最小温度估计误差的条件。

Abstract: We investigate quantum thermometry in Ohmic-type reservoirs using two-qubit probes within an exactly solvable pure-dephasing framework. By analyzing the individual variance associated with temperature estimation, we identify optimal regimes governed by the Ohmicity parameter $s$, the deviation angle $θ$, and the decay coefficients $α$ and $β$, thereby determining the conditions that minimize estimation errors. The Quantum Fisher Information (QFI) exhibits pronounced maxima at finite interaction times, especially in sub-Ohmic and Ohmic environments at low temperatures, whereas super-Ohmic reservoirs flatten the QFI peak and shift the optimal sensitivity toward higher temperatures. Consistently, the quantum signal-to-noise ratio (QSNR) is suppressed at low temperatures, increases with thermal excitation, and saturates in the high-temperature regime, where the influence of spectral details becomes negligible. A comparative study of mutual and local estimation strategies shows that common-bath configurations, particularly for $Σ^+$ and $Σ^0$ probes, outperform local baths at short interaction times due to bath-induced correlations, while local environments become advantageous at longer times. The analysis further reveals finite optimal values of both the interaction time $t_{\rm opt}$ and the temperature $T_{\rm opt}$, as well as a strong reduction of the variance with increasing measurement number in the low-temperature regime. In addition, our study of hyperon-antihyperon channels ($Λ$, $Σ^+$, $Σ^0$, $Ξ^-$, $Ξ^0$) shows that entanglement and quantum discord remain remarkably robust over broad angular domains, whereas steering and Bell nonlocality are confined to narrower regions. Overall, the interplay between spectral structure, particle-dependent parameters, and estimation strategy provides valuable ...

</details>


### [144] [Dissipation-tunable extended and localized steady states in a non-disordered lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19694)
*Ming-Jie Tao,Yi-Ting Wang,Jing Li,Hongsheng Hou,Xiang-Ping Jiang,Lei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用精细设计的非局部键耗散（bond dissipation）在无无序的一维晶格中选择局域态或扩展态的方案。通过引入带可调相对相位的键跳变算符，仅改变耗散通道的相位而不改变哈密顿量参数，即可将长时间密度矩阵导向由局域或扩展本征态主导的扇区，并利用配对相位匹配比例和量子保真度量化这一选择过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知中耗散通常被视为导致退相干和抑制量子干涉/局域化的因素。作者希望反其道而行之，探索能否通过工程化设计的耗散机制来主动选择并操控量子态，从而为无无序晶格中的态制备和输运操控提供新途径。

Method: 在一维无无序晶格中引入空间非均匀跳跃强度，使其同时支持扩展体态和局域边界态（包括代数局域的连续谱束缚态）。定义一个非局部键跳变算符，该算符带有可调的相对相位，此相位可选择性匹配具有不同空间相位相关的本征态。通过密度矩阵演化分析长时稳态，并用距离为l的位点对中与耗散通道相位匹配的比例来量化选态的微观机制，同时以量子保真度刻画耗散猝灭过程。

Result: 结果表明，调节键耗散的相对相位可以选择性地将系统推向由局域或扩展哈密顿量本征态主导的稳态，而无需改变任何哈密顿量参数。即使在撤去耗散之后，所选稳态的特征仍可保持。相位匹配比例和量子保真度分别从微观和动力学角度揭示了选态机制。

Conclusion: 本文确立了相位选择性键耗散作为在无无序晶格中实现可控态制备和输运操控的有效途径，颠覆了耗散仅起破坏性作用的传统观点，为开放量子系统的态工程提供了新思路。

Abstract: Dissipation is usually regarded as a source of decoherence that suppresses quantum interference and localization. Here we show that suitably engineered dissipation can instead be used to select localized or extended states in a strictly non-disordered one-dimensional lattice. The underlying clean lattice has spatially inhomogeneous hopping and supports both extended bulk states and localized boundary states, including an algebraically localized bound state in the continuum. We introduce a nonlocal bond jump operator with a tunable relative phase and show that this phase selectively favors eigenstates with different spatial phase correlations. As a result, the long-time density matrix can be steered toward sectors dominated by localized or extended Hamiltonian eigenstates without changing any Hamiltonian parameter. The microscopic origin of the selection is quantified by the fraction of site pairs separated by a distance $l$ that are phase matched with the dissipative channel. We further characterize the dissipative quench through the quantum fidelity and show that the selected character of the steady state can persist after the dissipation is removed. Our results establish phase-selective bond dissipation as a route to controllable state preparation and transport manipulation in non-disordered lattices.

</details>


### [145] [Dataflows and Computational Patterns for Hybrid Quantum-Classical Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19348)
*Ryan Landfield,Jordan J. Winetrout,Michael A. Sandoval*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子执行局部性框架（QELF），这是一个用于表征混合量子-经典计算工作流的定性框架。该框架基于周期性数据流结构和量子执行局部性（计算在QPU上驻留的程度），识别了五种具有不同局部性特征的计算模式，并为交流混合工作负载提供了统一词汇，为未来定量验证和硬件-算法协同设计奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典计算已成为近期量子应用的主导范式，但现有工作通常仅通过单个算法来描述混合工作流，缺乏对其底层执行行为的系统化理解。需要一个定性框架来统一表征混合工作流的执行模式。

Method: QELF通过分析代表性应用横截面，根据周期性的数据流结构和量子执行局部性对混合量子-经典工作流进行分类，识别出五种具有不同局部性特征的重复计算模式，并讨论它们在通信开销、工作流组织和未来混合计算架构方面的含义。

Result: QELF识别出五种具有不同量子执行局部性特征的周期性计算模式，并提供了讨论混合工作负载的通用词汇。

Conclusion: QELF为混合量子-经典工作流提供了定性分类框架和共同词汇，为未来定量验证以及算法、运行时系统和混合计算架构的协同设计建立了基础。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical computing has emerged as the dominant paradigm for near-term quantum applications, yet hybrid workflows are typically described by individual algorithms rather than their underlying execution behavior. We introduce the Quantum Execution Locality Framework (QELF), a qualitative framework for characterizing hybrid quantum-classical workflows according to recurring dataflow structures and quantum execution locality, the extent to which computation remains resident on the Quantum Processing Unit (QPU) before host intervention or classical synchronization. From a representative cross-section of applications, QELF identifies five recurring computational patterns with distinct locality characteristics and discusses their implications for communication overhead, workflow organization, and future hybrid computing architectures. By providing a common vocabulary for reasoning about hybrid workloads, QELF establishes a foundation for future quantitative validation and the co-design of algorithms, runtime systems, and hybrid computing architectures.

</details>


### [146] [Rigorous existence and location of quantum phase transitions in lattice Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20209)
*Massimo Ostilli,Carlo Presilla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推广了先前工作中提出的、可解释为态空间凝聚的量子相变（QPT）分析框架，将证明方法一般化到单参数格点哈密顿量，并扩展至涵盖二阶QPT，以横向场Ising模型为例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（Ostilli & Presilla, 2021）已提出一类可解释为态空间凝聚的QPT，但原始表述存在局限，未能涵盖二阶QPT。作者旨在推广并证明该框架的普适性，说明在典型物理条件下，任何格点系统中的QPT都可视为态空间凝聚。

Method: 将文献 [Phys. Rev. Lett. 127, 040601 (2021)] 中的论证一般化，针对一般单参数格点哈密顿量，通过简单界（simple bounds）证明QPT的存在性并确定其位置。推广后的框架涵盖二阶QPT，并选取横向场Ising模型作为二阶QPT的显式示例加以验证。

Result: 成功将态空间凝聚框架推广至包含二阶QPT的一般单参数格点哈密顿量，并以横向场Ising模型提供了二阶QPT的具体例证。分析表明在物理上通常满足的条件下，格点系统中的任意QPT均可解释为态空间中的凝聚现象。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种统一视角：在满足典型物理解析性条件下，所有格点系统的量子相变均可归结为态空间中的凝聚过程，从而为理解QPT提供了更普适的几何/概率论诠释。

Abstract: We extend the analysis of the class of quantum phase transitions (QPTs) that can be interpreted as condensations in state space, first introduced in [M. Ostilli and C. Presilla, J. Phys. A 54, 055005 (2021)], by generalizing the arguments of [M. Ostilli and C. Presilla, Phys. Rev. Lett. 127, 040601 (2021)] to prove the existence and determine the location (via simple bounds) of QPTs in general one-parameter lattice Hamiltonians. Unlike our original formulation, this extension also encompasses second-order QPTs, for which we provide the explicit example of the transverse-field Ising model. Our analysis suggests that, under conditions typically satisfied in physical contexts, any QPT taking place in lattice systems can be interpreted as a condensation in state space.

</details>


### [147] [Gradient flow towards quantum states with ideal quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19770)
*T. Shiga,T. Fukui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种梯度流方法，用于优化晶格模型中的量子态，其作用量包含量子度量和Berry曲率平方两项，分别驱动谱投影子趋向Bogomolny饱和与均匀Berry曲率。由于有限维投影子的no-go定理，这两个条件在晶格模型中通常无法同时满足，因此流会接近平衡两种几何要求的非平凡固定点。在Wilson-Dirac模型中展示了近均匀Berry曲率和接近Bogomolny界的结果，并构造了短程截断的平坦化哈密顿量；该方法也适用于Hofstadter模型，验证了度量和Berry曲率项在高Chern数Chern带中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在晶格模型中构造具有宽带隙、近平坦色散和均匀Berry曲率的量子态是量子几何和拓扑物态研究中的核心问题。现有方法通常难以同时优化量子度量和Berry曲率，因此需要一种系统性的方法来实现这种几何优化。

Method: 提出由量子度量项和Berry曲率平方项构成的梯度流方程，对谱投影子进行演化。量子度量项驱动投影子向Bogomolny饱和方向流动，Berry曲率平方项驱动其向均匀Berry曲率方向流动。该流在两类约束的平衡点处取得固定点，并从流动的投影子构造短程截断的平坦化哈密顿量。

Result: 在Wilson-Dirac模型中，流动后的投影子展现出几乎均匀的Berry曲率，同时保持接近Bogomolny界。由此构造的截断哈密顿量具有近扁平带和近均匀Berry曲率。在Hofstadter模型中，该方法同样有效，并验证了度量和Berry曲率项在高Chern数Chern带中的各自作用。

Conclusion: 该梯度流方法为构造具有良好量子几何性质的晶格模型提供了一种通用且有效的工具，能够在Bogomolny饱和与均匀Berry曲率之间实现最优折中，有望在量子霍尔物理和拓扑带工程等领域得到广泛应用。

Abstract: We propose a gradient-flow method for quantum states in lattice models, generated by an action consisting of the quantum-metric and the square of the Berry curvature. These two terms drive the spectral projector toward Bogomolny saturation and uniform Berry curvature, respectively. We show that, due to a no-go theorem for finite-dimensional projectors, the two conditions cannot in general be satisfied simultaneously in lattice models. Hence the flow is expected to approach a nontrivial fixed point that balances the two geometric requirements. For the Wilson-Dirac model, we demonstrate that the flowed projector exhibits almost uniform Berry curvature while remaining close to the Bogomolny bound. We further construct a short-range truncated flattened Hamiltonian from the flowed projector and obtain a lattice model with nearly flat bands and nearly uniform Berry curvature. We also apply the method to the Hofstadter model and confirm the roles of the metric and Berry-curvature terms in a Chern band with higher Chern number.

</details>


### [148] [Analytical Solutions of the Generalized Klein-Gordon Oscillator in Som-Raychaudhuri Space-Time via the Extended Nikiforov-Uvarov Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20273)
*Hale Karayer,Tolga Celik,Dogan Demirhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用扩展的Nikiforov-Uvarov（NU）方法，在Som-Raychaudhuri时空背景下，解析求解了存在均匀磁场时的广义Klein-Gordon谐振子问题，得到了线性势和Cornell势两种势模型下的精确本征态解，并给出了能量本征值及对应的本征函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空特别是Som-Raychaudhuri时空背景下，研究Klein-Gordon方程在磁场和势场作用下的精确可解性问题，以深入理解相对论量子系统在非惯性参考系/宇宙弦时空中的量子力学行为。

Method: 采用扩展的Nikiforov-Uvarov方法，将广义Klein-Gordon谐振子在均匀磁场和Som-Raychaudhuri时空中的方程约化为超几何型方程，分别对线性势和Cornell势两种模型进行精确求解。

Result: 获得了两种势模型（线性势和Cornell势）下系统的精确能量本征值表达式和本征函数，并对应波函数进行了图形化展示与分析。

Conclusion: 扩展的NU方法在Som-Raychaudhuri时空中可有效求解广义Klein-Gordon谐振子问题，为弯曲时空相对论量子力学中精确解的研究提供了进一步的理论支持。

Abstract: In this study, we present an analytical approach based on the extended Nikiforov-Uvarov method to solve the generalized Klein-Gordon oscillator in the presence of a uniform magnetic field within the Som-Raychaudhuri space-time. Exact eigenstate solutions are obtained for two distinct potential models, namely the linear potential and the Cornell potential. The corresponding energy eigenvalues are determined, and the associated eigenfunctions are analyzed and illustrated graphically.

</details>


### [149] [Rescaled Mandelstam Tamm characterization of discrete time crystal response in a disordered Floquet Ising chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19403)
*Abrar Ahmed Naqash,Salman Sajad Wani,Saif Al-Kuwari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对二值Floquet驱动下的离散时间晶体类周期二动力学，推导了精确的分段分辨Mandelstam-Tamm（MT）下界泛函表达式（无需假设分段哈密顿量对易），并将其应用于无序Floquet Ising链。在可和性条件下，路径平均能量色散满足O(√L)上界；数值结果表明周期二MT分支的劈裂主要源自端点几何（返回角）而非路径平均能量色散，且该MT泛函的周期二分量与自旋锁定响应之间存在显著的部分Spearman秩相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯态幺正动力学中，MT下界泛函比较了端点Fubini-Study返回角与路径平均能量色散，但二者在尺寸和时间依赖上的差异掩盖了离散时间晶体类动力学中周期二MT结构的起源及其与自旋响应的关系，需要精确分解MT泛函来阐明这一机制。

Method: 推导了无需对易性假设的精确分段分辨MT表达式；基于局部能量项连接协方差的可和性，建立了路径平均能量色散的O(√L)上界；在无序Floquet Ising链上对多种系统尺寸进行端点数据数值验证，并通过部分Spearman秩相关分析（控制脉冲误差与相互作用强度）考察MT周期二分量与锁定自旋响应的关联。

Result: 四种系统尺寸的端点数据与O(√L)主导行为一致。在自旋锁定响应的代表点上，奇偶重标度MT分支在整个10²周期观测窗口内保持分离；返回角具有强交替性，而尺寸归一化的路径平均能量色散奇偶差别很小，表明端点几何是分支劈裂的主要来源。互作用参数网格上，周期二MT分量与锁定自旋响应存在强部分Spearman秩相关。

Conclusion: 重标度MT泛函能刻画有限尺寸周期二动力学的全局返回几何特征，并能量化其与锁定自旋响应的关联，为理解DTC类动力学中周期二结构与自旋响应的关系提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: For pure state unitary dynamics, the Mandelstam Tamm (MT) lower-bound functional compares the endpoint Fubini Study return angle with path averaged energy dispersion. Their distinct size and temporal dependences obscure the origin of period two MT structure in discrete time crystal (DTC) like dynamics and its relation to the spin response. For binary Floquet drives, we derive an exact segment resolved MT expression without assuming commuting segment Hamiltonians and apply it to a disordered Floquet Ising chain. Absolute and uniform summability of connected covariances of local energy terms implies an $O(\sqrt{L})$ upper bound on the path-averaged energy dispersion. Endpoint data for four system sizes are consistent with this leading behavior and support the corresponding rescaling of the MT functional. At a representative point in the finite-size region with a locked spin response, odd and even rescaled MT branches remain separated throughout the $10^2$ period observation window. The return angle alternates strongly, whereas the size-normalized path-averaged energy dispersion shows little discernible parity dependence, indicating that endpoint geometry is the main source of the branch splitting. Across the interacting parameter grid, the period-two MT component has a strong partial Spearman rank correlation with the locked spin response after controlling for pulse error and interaction strength. The rescaled MT functional characterizes the global return geometry of finite-size period-two dynamics and quantifies its association with the locked spin response.

</details>


### [150] [Resource-Efficient Bio-Molecular Docking on a NISQ-era Digital Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19868)
*Tianqi Chen,Adrian M. Mak,Jianguo Li,Jian Feng Kong,Chandra Verma,Sebastian Maurer-Stroh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合量子-经典分子对接方法，将分子对接问题映射为最大顶点加权团问题（MVWCP），并采用变分全基编码（FBE）策略将经典二元变量高效编码为Bloch球向量，通过量子电路优化求解，并在IBM量子计算机上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子对接是药物发现中的关键计算任务，但由于可能的结合构型呈组合爆炸式增长，大型柔性分子的对接在计算上极其昂贵，尤其在大规模场景下。已有的经典MVWCP形式化求解方法在高维情况下计算复杂度高，需要探索新的高效求解途径。

Method: 本文方法包括：（1）将分子对接重铸为MVWCP问题；（2）提出变分全基编码（FBE）策略，用Bloch球向量高效编码经典二元变量；（3）将对接问题映射为代价哈密顿量，在变分框架内最小化；（4）采用随机化虚时演化（ITE）启发的暖启动和基于梯度的优化技术；（5）在IBM量子计算机上执行电路验证。

Result: 作者证明了FBE目标的全局极小值总能选为纯乘积态，为使用酉变分电路优化提供了严格的理论依据；并在IBM量子计算机上成功执行了电路，验证了量子辅助优化在基于结构的药物设计中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了混合量子-经典方法在结构药物设计中的应用潜力，为量子优化中先进编码技术在计算生物学领域的更广泛应用提供了方向。

Abstract: Molecular docking is a vital computational task in drug discovery, wherein the objective is to efficiently identify optimal binding poses between a ligand and a target receptor protein. Due to the combinatorial explosion of possible binding configurations, docking of large and flexible molecules remains a computationally intensive problem, especially at scale. Early studies have revealed that the molecular docking can be re-cast as a maximum vertex-weighted clique problem (MVWCP) problem on a compatibility graph to be solved classically. In this work, we proposed a hybrid quantum-classical approach for molecular docking leveraging the MVWCP formalism with a variational full-basis encoding (FBE) strategy, which enables efficient encoding of classical binary variables with Bloch sphere vectors. We further prove that a global minimizer of the FBE objective can always be chosen to be a pure product state, thereby providing a rigorous justification for its optimization using a unitary variational circuit. The molecular docking problem is first mapped to a cost Hamiltonian that is minimized within a variational framework, optimized via a randomized imaginary time evolution (ITE)-inspired warm start, and gradient-based techniques. Finally, we also executed the circuit on an IBM quantum computer, underlying the feasibility and of quantum-assisted optimization for structure-based drug design and point towards the broader utility of advanced encoding techniques in quantum optimization for computational biology.

</details>


### [151] [Compressibility of genuine multipartite entanglement under the Hadamard map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19427)
*Klára Baksová,Lisa T. Weinbrenner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子态超激活现象中，通过Hadamard映射从多拷贝到单拷贝希尔伯特空间的局域投影在拷贝数趋于无穷时的行为。研究表明，对于固定映射存在最优拷贝数，超过该数量后获得的纠缠反而下降，揭示了纠缠蒸馏与局域投影方案之间的本质区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超激活效应描述了原本对特定任务无用的量子态在考虑多个拷贝时可能变得有用，这是量子信息中非常反直觉的现象。在多体纠缠的背景下，局域投影可以简化超激活的实际可达性，但需要深入理解在大量拷贝极限下这些投影的行为规律。

Method: 以Hadamard映射为例，研究多种量子态家族在局域投影方案下的超激活行为，分析固定映射条件下拷贝数变化对获得纠缠量的影响。

Result: 发现对于固定映射，存在一个最优拷贝数，超过该数值后获得的纠缠量会降低。这一结果揭示了局域投影方案与纠缠蒸馏方案在拷贝数依赖性上的显著差异。

Conclusion: 局域投影方案中，拷贝数并非越多越好，存在最优值；该方案与纠缠蒸馏的行为模式不同，值得进一步研究其理论基础和应用潜力。

Abstract: One of the most counterintuitive effects in the examination of quantum states is the phenomenon of superactivation, which describes the fact that a quantum state, which is useless for a specific task, may become useful when one considers multiple copies of it. This effect can be observed in the case of genuine multipartite entanglement, where local projections from multiple copies to the single-copy Hilbert space could significantly simplify the practical accessibility of its superactivation. We investigate how such projections behave in the limit of many copies, using the Hadamard map as an example, and studying different state families. We show that, in this scheme, for a fixed map, an optimal number of copies exists, beyond which the obtained entanglement decreases, highlighting the differences between entanglement distillation and local projection schemes.

</details>


### [152] [Imaginary time evolution of a quantum system through analytic continuation from real-time quantum simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19943)
*Peng Guo,Anto Shibu,Joshua Lin,Yong Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过解析延拓实测实时间关联函数来实现通用量子哈密顿量虚时演化（ITE）的算法，并在IBM量子硬件上演示了其在1D Fokker-Planck方程和1D量子力学散射问题中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然量子计算天然适合模拟实时量子系统，但实现虚时演化（ITE）相对困难。然而，量子实现ITE在经典实现存在符号问题的情况下以及在量子计算机上精确制备本征态方面都非常有用。

Method: 通过对实测的实时间关联函数进行解析延拓（analytic continuation）来获得通用量子哈密顿量的虚时演化。算法在1D Fokker-Planck方程（经典扩散过程）和1D量子力学散射中虚时演化的积分关联函数上进行了模拟和IBM量子硬件演示。

Result: 在IBM量子硬件上成功演示了该方法，分别应用于1D Fokker-Planck方程的经典扩散过程模拟和1D量子力学散射中积分关联函数的虚时演化，验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过实时间关联函数的解析延拓实现了虚时演化，为在量子计算机上精确制备本征态和规避经典实现的符号问题提供了一条可行路径，并在真实量子硬件上得到了验证。

Abstract: Though quantum computing naturally offers an advantage for simulations of real-time quantum systems, implementing Imaginary-Time Evolution (ITE) is comparatively more difficult. Nevertheless, quantum implementations of ITE are useful both in cases where classical implementations have associated sign problems, and also as an exact method of preparing eigenstates on quantum computers. In this work we present an algorithm to obtain ITE of a generic quantum Hamiltonian by performing analytic continuation of measured real-time correlation functions. We present simulations and demonstrations on IBM quantum hardware of 1D Fokker-Planck equations for classical diffusion process and imaginary-time evolution of integrated correlation functions in 1D quantum mechanical scattering as examples to demonstrate the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [153] [Dynamical one-from-many quantum metrology: Sum rule and matrix-free precision bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19988)
*Ziyu Xie,Junjie Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子单参数估计中普遍存在的"从多中取一"（one-from-many）问题，发现了一个此前被忽视的求和规则：关于所有模型参数的QFI之和与关于时间的QFI相关联，这导致必须引入时间参数构造增广QFI矩阵，而该矩阵本质上是奇异的——正是传统方法失效的场景。作者推导了一个不依赖矩阵运算的紧致量子精度界限，仅涉及标量量，广泛适用且包含现有结果为特例，在酉演化和噪声场景下均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子单参数估计几乎总是涉及冗余参数（nuisance parameters），本质上是one-from-many问题。现有方法依赖多参数计量学，通过约化矩阵量子Cramér-Rao界获得标量精度极限，但这些方法受限于QFI矩阵求逆的繁重计算、权重矩阵的选择，并且在QFI矩阵奇异时会失效。因此需要一种更通用、无需矩阵运算的量子精度界限。

Method: 作者首先揭示了一个求和规则：在动力学one-from-many估计中，关于所有模型参数的QFI之和与关于时间的QFI通过求和规则关联。这一发现要求将时间参数纳入构造增广QFI矩阵，同时导致该矩阵固有奇异。基于此，作者推导了一个紧致的、无矩阵（matrix-free）量子精度界限，仅涉及标量量，并通过理论分析证明了其包含现有结果为特例，涵盖酉演化和噪声环境等多种情形。

Result: 该方法在酉演化和噪声两种设定下均得到了验证。新的量子精度界限是紧致的，适用范围广泛，且将现有结果作为特例纳入其框架，解决了传统方法在QFI矩阵奇异时失效的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作为实际量子单参数计量学提供了更精细化、更具操作性的理论框架，克服了现有方法在奇异QFI矩阵场景下的局限性，拓展了量子精度界限的适用范围。

Abstract: Practical quantum single-parameter estimation is rarely a pristine task; it almost invariably involves nuisance parameters, casting it as a one-from-many problem. Existing approaches to this problem rely on multi-parameter metrology, reducing the matrix quantum Cramér-Rao bound to obtain scalar quantum precision limits. However, these methods are often hampered by the demanding inversion of the quantum Fisher information (QFI) matrix and the requisite choice of a weight matrix, and they break down when the QFI matrix becomes singular. Here, we show that for dynamical one-from-many estimation, a previously overlooked sum rule connecting the QFI about all model parameters to the QFI about time necessitates including the latter to consider an augmented QFI matrix while simultaneously rendering it inherently singular--precisely the scenario where conventional approaches fail. To meet this challenge, we derive a tight, matrix-free quantum precision bound that involves only scalar quantities, offers broad applicability, and subsumes existing results as special cases. Validated in both unitary and noisy settings, our findings provide a refined operational framework for practical quantum single-parameter metrology.

</details>


### [154] [Renormalization group ontology in quantum foundations: a non-interacting spin-0 toy model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19458)
*Gary Kapilevich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重整化群启发的扩展本体论（ontology）的现实主义、反psi（anti-psi）量子场论诠释。作者以经典非相互作用标量场为玩具模型，在正则系综中展示该扩展本体论中的观察者在特定极限条件下将观察到QFT。逆重整化群（IRG）是关键工具，普朗克常数被定义为随流向不动点流动、与温度相关且依赖于观察者的量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为量子场论提供一种新的现实主义诠释，回避量子力学中波函数（psi）作为根本实在的问题（anti-psi立场），同时借助重整化群思想构建扩展本体论，以期深入理解量子基础并为构造新QFT提供指导。

Method: 采用正则系综中的经典非相互作用标量场作为玩具模型，引入由重整化群启发的扩展本体论框架，利用逆重整化群（IRG）解释该本体论并生成QFT的新例子。普朗克常数被定义为随流向不动点演化、用温度表达且依赖观察者的量。

Result: 在特定极限条件下，扩展本体论中的观察者能够看到QFT；IRG提供了构造新QFT的途径；普朗克常数被重新定义为流动量，依赖于温度与所研究的观察者。

Conclusion: 该框架有望同时推动量子基础研究的发展，并为开发新的量子场论提供方法论指导。

Abstract: A realist, anti-psi interpretation of quantum field theory is introduced, based on an expanded ontology that was inspired by the renormalization group. A goal will be to work with a classical non-interacting scalar field "toy model" in the canonical ensemble and show that an observer in this expanded ontology, under certain limiting conditions, will see a QFT. The inverse renormalization group (IRG) will be key in both interpreting this expanded ontology and furnishing novel examples of QFTs. This will lead to a definition of Planck's constant that flows as we head towards a fixed point, is expressed in terms of a temperature, and depends on the observer being studied in this expanded ontology. Our hope is that this approach will lead both to insights into quantum foundations and to prescriptions for developing new QFTs.

</details>
