### [155] [Boltzmann counting in Hilbert space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20136)
*Raúl O. Vallejos,Isadora Veeren,Frederico Brito,Fernando de Melo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于量子制备的几何熵，定义为与给定约束兼容的纯态希尔伯特空间体积的对数，将玻尔兹曼计数视角推广到量子领域，并针对三类约束给出了显式标度律和闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子熵（如冯·诺依曼熵）基于密度矩阵和粗粒化描述，但在制备层面缺乏一个基于几何的体积度量来刻画量子无知（ignorance）。本文旨在将玻尔兹曼的计数思想扩展到量子态空间，为量子制备过程提供一种几何熵度量，补充现有熵概念的不足。

Method: 作者定义制备几何熵为与给定约束兼容的纯态态空间体积的对数。由于量子制备中兼容态不必正交，"态数"自然地由态空间体积替代。论文分析了三类约束：(1) 限制在子空间中；(2) 固定期望值；(3) 粗粒化子系统描述。针对代表性实例——子空间投影、自旋期望值、部分迹和不完美探测器映射——推导了显式的标度律和体积的闭式表达式。

Result: 针对四类代表性示例（子空间投影、自旋期望值、部分迹、不完美探测器映射），获得了对应的态空间体积的闭式表达式和标度律，验证了几何熵框架的有效性和可计算性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种在制备层面度量量子无知的几何方法，补充了基于密度矩阵和粗粒化的传统熵概念，为量子信息理论提供了新的熵视角。

Abstract: We introduce a geometric entropy for quantum preparations, defined as the logarithm of the Hilbert-space volume of pure states compatible with a given set of constraints. This construction extends Boltzmann's counting perspective to the quantum setting, where compatible states need not be orthogonal and the relevant notion of "number of states" is naturally replaced by a volume in state space. We analyze three classes of constraints: restriction to a subspace, fixed expectation values, and coarse-grained subsystem descriptions. For representative examples, including subspace projection, spin expectation values, partial trace, and an imperfect detector map, we obtain explicit scaling laws and closed-form expressions for the associated volumes. The resulting framework provides a geometric measure of quantum ignorance at the level of the preparation and complements entropy notions based on density matrices and coarse graining.

</details>


### [156] [Reinforcement LearningtoHarness Approximation Errors for Long-Time QuantumSimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20139)
*Yu-Bo Shi,Markus Heyl,Roderich Moessner,Marin Bukov,Hongzheng Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-Trotter，一种基于强化学习的框架，将量子模拟中不可避免的近似误差视为可编排的资源而非仅需抑制的缺陷，通过仅利用守恒律（能量和能量方差）来指导智能体自适应调整单个标量——下一个Trotter步长，从而在长时间演化中实现自我纠错的序列，显著提升动力学精度并减少测量开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字量子仿真在长时间尺度上的精度受到近似模拟固有误差累积的限制。传统方法将误差视为需要抑制的缺陷，但作者提出一个全新的视角：应当将这些不可避免的近似误差视为可资利用的资源，通过编排误差使后续误差补偿先前的累积误差，从而实现更精确的长时间量子动力学模拟。

Method: RL-Trotter框架采用强化学习智能体，其关键设计包括：(1) 从守恒律（能量及能量方差）提取低维信息作为学习信号，无需访问目标波函数；(2) 智能体仅学习调整单个标量——下一个Trotter步长；(3) 优化目标为整个长时间演化过程而非单个时间步；最终发现自我纠错的步长序列，其中后期误差补偿前期累积误差。

Result: 学习到的策略对测量噪声具有内在鲁棒性，大幅降低测量开销；策略能够泛化到未见过的物理上相似的初始态，并能从小型可经典模拟系统迁移到大约大一个数量级的系统；该框架支持经典预训练后直接在量子硬件上部署或有限微调的实际协议。

Conclusion: 研究确立了量子算法的一种更广泛视角：近似演化中的误差可以被编排为资源，用于实现精确且资源高效的量子动力学模拟，为量子计算中的误差管理提供了全新的范式。

Abstract: Accurate digital quantum simulation at long times is limited by the accumulation of errors inherent to approximate simulation. Here we introduce RL-Trotter, a reinforcement-learning framework that treats unavoidable approximation errors as resources for error correction rather than merely imperfections to suppress. We show that low-dimensional information from conservation laws, such as the energy and energy variance, provides a sufficient learning signal to guide the agent, which learns to adapt a single scalar---the next Trotter step size---without access to the target wave function. By optimizing the entire long-time evolution rather than individual steps, RL-Trotter discovers self-correcting sequences in which later errors compensate for those accumulated earlier, increasing the accuracy of the long-time dynamics. The learned policies are intrinsically robust to measurement noise, substantially reducing measurement overhead. They also generalize to previously unseen, physically similar initial states and transfer from small, classically simulable systems to systems an order of magnitude larger. This enables a practical protocol based on classical pretraining followed by direct deployment or limited fine-tuning on quantum hardware. Our results establish a broader perspective for quantum algorithms: errors in approximate evolution can be orchestrated into resources for accurate and resource-efficient quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [157] [Fast Algorithms for Stoquastic Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19489)
*Ryan L. Mann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个通用框架，用于在高温条件下开发阻挫自旋系统（stoquastic spin systems）的快速采样与计数算法。该框架基于聚合物模型的快速混合马尔可夫链以及用于采样单个聚合物的亚临界渗流过程，并将其应用于一般阻挫自旋系统、铁磁海森堡模型和二分图上的反铁磁海森堡模型，显著改进了逆温度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫自旋系统的配分函数近似计算和热分布采样是统计物理与理论计算机科学交叉领域的核心问题。现有方法在高温区域外的适用范围有限，缺乏统一且高效的算法框架来处理包括海森堡模型在内的多种阻挫自旋系统。

Method: 提出了一个系统性框架：首先为聚合物模型构造快速混合的马尔可夫链，再通过亚临界渗流过程实现对单个聚合物的高效采样。针对海森堡模型，分别利用环表示（cycle representation）和圈表示（loop representation）来改进逆温度界限。

Result: 该框架为三类阻挫自旋系统（一般阻挫系统、铁磁海森堡模型、二分图上的反铁磁海森堡模型）提供了配分函数近似与热分布采样的快速算法，并通过环/圈表示获得了更优的逆温度（高温）界限。

Conclusion: 该工作为阻挫自旋系统的高温采样与计数问题提供了一个统一且高效的算法框架，结合聚合物模型和渗流理论，并在海森堡模型中通过环/圈表示扩展了适用温度范围。

Abstract: We establish a general framework for developing fast sampling and counting algorithms for stoquastic spin systems at high temperature. Our framework is based on a rapidly mixing Markov chain for polymer models and a subcritical percolation process for sampling individual polymers. We apply our framework to obtain fast algorithms for approximating the partition function and sampling from the thermal distribution of (1) general stoquastic spin systems, (2) ferromagnetic Heisenberg models, and (3) antiferromagnetic Heisenberg models on bipartite graphs. For the Heisenberg models, we obtain an improved bound on the inverse temperature by using their respective cycle and loop representations.

</details>


### [158] [The ebbs and flows of quantum learning and sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20155)
*Matias Karjula,Teemu Ojanen,Tapio Ala-Nissila,Moein N. Ivaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过研究一类最小可调的后变分量子电路，揭示了子系统量子复杂度与计算结构涌现之间的关系，发现了一个介于可学习与量子混沌之间的"学习相"，该相的特征是谱非平坦性和读出态敏感性，并将随机量子动力学的这些特征视为可扩展非线性计算的计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索子系统量子复杂度如何与计算上有用的结构涌现相关联，理解随机量子动力学特征如何被利用为可扩展非线性计算的计算资源。

Method: 研究一类最小可调（minimally tunable）的后变分（postvariational）量子电路家族，通过分析谱非平坦性（spectral nonflatness）和计量学响应（metrological response）来直接控制系综典型的（ensemble-typical）信息处理能力。

Result: 发现存在一个介于量子混沌之前的中间"学习相"，其特征为显著的谱非平坦性和读出态敏感性；最优信息处理容量随系统尺寸增大而提升，而深度量子置乱（deep scrambling）则会抑制可观测响应。

Conclusion: 随机量子动力学中的谱非平坦性与响应敏感性等特征可被视为实现可扩展非线性计算的计算资源，为理解量子复杂度与计算能力的关系提供了新视角。

Abstract: What is the relation between subsystem quantum complexity and the emergence of computationally useful structure? We address this by studying a family of minimally tunable postvariational quantum circuits, and show how spectral nonflatness and metrological response directly control the ensemble-typical information processing power. This unveils an intermediate "learning phase" that precedes the onset of quantum chaos, characterized by pronounced nonflatness and sensitivity of readout states. The optimal information processing capacity improves with system size, while deep scrambling suppresses observable response. The results reveal how such features of random quantum dynamics can be viewed as computational resources for scalable nonlinear computation.

</details>


### [159] [Quantum algorithm for differential equations via permutation matrix representation with application to the Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19508)
*Hriday Sabharwal,Amir Kalev,Itay Hen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种求解非线性粘性Burgers方程动力学的量子算法，通过Carleman线性化、padding方案及Permutation Matrix Representation (PMR)分解，将提升后的线性微分方程与LCHS算法结合，实现了对截断提升系统解的量子态制备，并量化了稳定位移带来的指数级后选择开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Carleman的量子算法通常将提升后的线性微分方程表述为oracle模型，缺乏可直接实现的结构化分解方案。作者希望为非线性偏微分方程提供一种更实用的哈密顿模拟原语，将Carleman线性化与LCHS算法自然结合，同时避免对完整矩阵范数的依赖，提升算法在大规模对角占优生成器场景下的适用性。

Method: 首先对空间离散化的Burgers方程应用Carleman线性化，随后通过padding方案将其映射到qubit寄存器。作者使用Permutation Matrix Representation (PMR)将padding后的生成器分解为对角掩码与可逆算术置换的组合，该分解天然兼容LCHS算法。算法在LCHS所要求的正半定Hermitian部分（必要时经过稳定位移）条件下，制备与截断提升系统解成比例的归一化量子态。作者量化了稳定位移带来的指数级后选择开销，并提出rescaling方案来缓解。此外，PMR方案被扩展至包含高阶导数、非线性项、多流体变量或多空间维度的广义流体方程。

Result: 算法复杂度与Carleman生成器的非对角范数成比例而非矩阵范数，这对对角占优的生成器具有优势。PMR被证明可作为更广泛的LCU类算法的哈密顿模拟原语，并成功扩展至广义流体方程。

Conclusion: 本文展示了PMR作为哈密顿模拟原语在Carleman线性化量子算法中的有效性，为非线性流体动力学方程的量子模拟提供了结构化的实现路径，并指出了未来可推广至更广泛的LCU类算法的潜力。

Abstract: We develop a quantum algorithm for solving the dynamics of the nonlinear viscous Burgers equation. We apply the Carleman linearization procedure on the spatially discretized equation, followed by a padding scheme that allows implementation on qubit registers. Existing Carleman-based quantum algorithms commonly formulate the lifted linear differential equation in an oracle model. Here we decompose the padded generator into diagonal masks and reversible arithmetic permutations using the Permutation Matrix Representation (PMR), which we show to be naturally compatible with the Linear Combination of Hamiltonian Simulations (LCHS) algorithm. Under the assumptions required by LCHS - most importantly positive semidefiniteness of the Hermitian part of the linear generator, possibly after a stabilizing shift - the algorithm prepares a normalized quantum state proportional to the solution of the truncated lifted system; the stabilizing shift introduces an exponential postselection overhead, which we quantify and mitigate through a rescaling scheme. We show that our algorithm scales with the off-diagonal norm of the Carleman generator instead of the matrix norm, which can be advantageous for other generators that are diagonally dominant. We also extend the PMR scheme to general fluid equations that may contain higher-order derivatives or nonlinear terms, or may involve multiple fluid variables or spatial dimensions. The construction illustrates how PMR can serve as a convenient Hamiltonian-simulation primitive for a broader class of LCU-based algorithms.

</details>


### [160] [Nonthermal Dynamics of a One-dimensional Rydberg-atom Chain with Constraint Four-body Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20233)
*Tianyi Yan,Weibin Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由四体约束相互作用驱动的Rydberg原子线性链的动力学。作者通过多体基态ansatz将低能哈密顿量化为三对角形式，在热力学极限下解析获得基态能量（随链长L线性缩放），并发现该模型支持能量近似等间距的量子多体疤痕本征态。疤痕态与基态|0...0⟩有强重叠，且重叠分布被态依赖的四体相互作用倾斜，导致非遍历动力学（初始态回波）。该模型可通过具有交替键长的Peierls阵列中的Rydberg原子实现，其中短/长键分别表现为Rydberg阻塞和反阻塞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索受约束四体相互作用驱动的非遍历量子动力学，寻找可利用Rydberg阻塞和反阻塞组合实现的多体疤痕态新平台，以拓展对约束非遍历动力学的理解。

Method: 采用多体基态ansatz将低能哈密顿量简化为三对角形式；在热力学极限下解析求解基态能量并与精确对角化对比；通过分析疤痕态与基态的态重叠分布及初始态回波来表征非遍历动力学；提出利用Peierls阵列中交替键长的Rydberg原子（短键Rydberg阻塞、长键反阻塞）实现该四体约束模型。

Result: 基态能量随链长L线性缩放，解析结果与精确对角化一致；模型支持能量近似等间距的多体疤痕态，与|0...0⟩基态强重叠；态依赖的四体相互作用使重叠分布倾斜，导致非遍历动力学并观察到初始态回波；该模型可在Rydberg原子Peierls阵列中实验实现。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了四体约束相互作用可以产生量子多体疤痕和强非遍历动力学，为通过结合Rydberg阻塞与反阻塞机制探索约束非遍历物理提供了新路径。

Abstract: We investigate dynamics of a linear chain of Rydberg atoms driven by a constrained four-body interaction, where two neighboring atoms are excited simultaneously from the electronic ground state $|0\rangle$ to Rydberg state $|1\rangle$ only when their closest neighbors are in $|0\rangle$ state. By employing an ansatz for the many-body ground state, the low-energy Hamiltonian is given by a tridiagonal form. The many-body ground state energy, which scales linearly with the chain length $L$, is obtained analytically in the thermodynamic limit and agrees with the one of the exact diagonalization. Our model supports quantum many-body scar eigenstates that are nearly equally spaced energetically. The scar states overlap strongly with the basis state $|\mathbf{0}\rangle=|0\cdots 0\rangle$. We show that the overlap distribution is tilted by the state-dependent four-body interaction. This leads to non-ergodic dynamics, evidenced by the revival of the initial state. The four-body constrained model can be realized with Rydberg atoms in a Peierls array with alternating bond lengths, where atoms on the shorter and longer bonds experience Rydberg blockade and antiblockade, respectively. Our study provides a pathway to explore constrained non-ergodic dynamics with four-body interactions by combining the Rydberg blockade and antiblockade.

</details>


### [161] [Attaining Fundamental Limits of Multiparameter Incoherent Optical Imaging Using Joint-Detection Quantum Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19524)
*Nico Deshler,Aakash Warke,Michael R. Grace,Amit Ashok,Saikat Guha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究衍射极限下扩展非相干物体成像的量子极限问题，通过将物体近似为热点发射器网格，推导了亮度估计的量子Fisher信息矩阵，并证明了SPADE测量策略在可分离测量中的近最优性，同时提出了两种达到Helstrom界的联合检测接收方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 衍射极限以下分辨扩展非相干物体是一个应用丰富但具挑战性的成像问题，其解决方案可能推动新一代观测仪器和能力的出现，因此需要从量子信息论角度确定成像精度极限并寻找最优测量策略。

Method: 将任意扩展非相干物体近似为参数化亮度的热点发射器有限网格，推导量子Fisher信息矩阵；分析对称对数导数弱对易性以确定Helstrom界适用性；比较Nagaoka-Hayashi界与Helstrom界；并通过数值方法评估SPADE及其他测量策略的性能。

Result: 发现对称对数导数弱对易，Helstrom界是最终量子极限；在深亚衍射场景中，Nagaoka-Hayashi界与Helstrom界之间存在间隙，表明可分离测量无法达到量子极限；SPADE通常能饱和NH界，优于直接成像；提出了两种利用量子资源实现联合检测接收的方案，可渐近达到Helstrom界。

Conclusion: 可分离测量虽便于实验实现但在深亚衍射场景下不足以达到量子极限，需要借助作用于多拷贝量子态的联合测量；SPADE作为近最优可分离策略具有实用价值；本文提出的联合检测方案为达到理论极限提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Resolving extended incoherent objects below the diffraction limit poses an application-rich imaging challenge whose solution may enable a new generation of observational instruments and capabilities. In this work, we invoke a practical model for general imaging by approximating an arbitrary extended incoherent object as a finite grid of thermal point emitters parameterized by their brightnesses. We derive the quantum Fisher information matrix (QFIM) for simultaneous brightness estimation and show that the symmetric logarithmic derivatives weakly commute, indicating that the Helstrom bound furnishes the ultimate quantum limit on the estimation error for incoherent imaging. Furthermore, for deeply sub-diffraction scenes, we find numerical evidence of a gap between the Nagaoka-Hayashi (NH) bound and the Helstrom bound. This gap reveals that separable measurements, though more experimentally accessible, are insufficient to reach the quantum limit, and points to the prospective advantage of joint measurements acting on multiple state copies. Additionally, we show that spatial mode-demultiplexing (SPADE) often saturates the NH bound solidifying its status as a near-optimal separable measurement strategy that significantly outperforms direct imaging. Finally, we articulate two joint detection receivers implemented with bona fide quantum resources that asymptotically achieve the Helstrom bound.

</details>


### [162] [Physics-Based versus Data-Driven Classification of Single-Photon Quantum Emitters from Sparse Autocorrelation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19528)
*Nhat Minh Nguyen,Md Shakhawath Hossain,Duc Anh Ngo,Chaohao Chen,Xiaoxue Xu,Toan Trong Tran,Carlo Bradac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种单光子发射体分类方法——序贯贝叶斯推断、Levenberg-Marquardt拟合和前馈神经网络。通过合成训练和测试数据（基于六方氮化硼发射体的真实Hanbury Brown-Twiss测量校准），在已知真实发射体数量的条件下评估了三种方法的准确性与鲁棒性。结果表明：神经网络在短积分时间下最鲁棒，贝叶斯分类器收敛最快且保持物理可解释性，Levenberg-Marquardt拟合最慢但召回率最高。没有任何单一方法在所有指标上占优，物理驱动与数据驱动方法互为补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单光子发射体识别需要测量二阶自相关函数，其统计可靠性受采集时间限制。机器学习分类器可加速从稀疏数据中识别发射体，但其与基于物理推断方法的性能差异此前未被系统性研究。作者旨在为可扩展的单光子源筛选提供方法选择与组合的实践指导。

Method: 采用序贯贝叶斯推断对单光子发射体进行分类，并将其与Levenberg-Marquardt拟合和前馈神经网络进行基准比较。使用合成训练与测试数据，数据通过真实六方氮化硼发射体的Hanbury Brown-Twiss测量校准，从而在受控噪声和背景条件下实现对确切已知真实发射体数量的评估。

Result: 三种方法在足够积分时间下均能达到接近完美的准确率，但在稀疏光子统计下的收敛速度和鲁棒性差异显著：神经网络在短积分时间时最鲁棒；贝叶斯分类器收敛最快且具备完全物理可解释性；Levenberg-Marquardt拟合收敛最慢但召回率最高。

Conclusion: 没有单一方法能在所有性能指标上占据优势；仅依赖单一指标会误导对分类器性能的评估，尤其在稀疏光子统计条件下。物理驱动方法与数据驱动方法是互补而非竞争关系，应根据具体需求选择和组合分类策略。

Abstract: Identifying single photon emitters from large, inhomogeneous candidate populations is key to realizing many quantum applications. This requires measuring the emitters second order autocorrelation function, whose statistical reliability is fundamentally limited by acquisition time. Machine learning classifiers can accelerate identification from sparse data, but their performance relative to physics-based inference has not been systematically examined. Here, we introduce sequential Bayesian inference for single photon emitter classification and benchmark it against Levenberg-Marquardt fitting and a feedforward neural network. We use synthetic training and test data calibrated against real Hanbury Brown-Twiss measurements from hexagonal boron nitride emitters, enabling evaluation against an exactly known ground-truth emitter number under realistic noise and background conditions. All three approaches achieve high, near-perfect accuracy with sufficient integration time, but differ greatly in convergence rate and robustness under sparse photon statistics. The neural network is most robust at short integration times. The Bayesian classifier reaches near-perfect accuracy fastest, while retaining full physical interpretability. Levenberg-Marquardt fitting remains a valuable, fully interpretable method, achieving the highest recall despite being the slowest to converge. These results lead to several key conclusions. No single method dominates across all performance metrics. Relying on any one metric alone can give a misleading picture of classifier performance, particularly under sparse photon statistics. Physics-based and data-driven methods are complementary rather than competing approaches. Together, these findings provide practical guidance for selecting and combining classification strategies for scalable single-photon-source screening and other quantum-emitter characterization tasks.

</details>


### [163] [Programmable cavity QED with a fiber-integrated atomic array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20291)
*Stephan Roschinski,Johannes Schabbauer,Franz von Silva-Tarouca,Marvin Holten,Damien Bloch,Julian Léonard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种将十二位$^{87}$Rb光镊阵列与高协同性光纤法布里-珀罗微腔相结合的实验平台，实现了原子-腔强耦合，测量了集体增强的真空拉比劈裂，并实现了基于腔的非破坏性原子数读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高协同性腔量子电动力学系统是实现量子信息处理和量子网络的关键资源，但将其与可扩展、具有位点分辨控制能力的光镊阵列整合仍具挑战性。作者旨在解决这一集成难题，建立兼具单原子控制与强腔耦合的可扩展架构。

Method: 将十二位$^{87}$Rb光镊阵列放置在光纤法布里-珀罗微腔的腔模内，利用亚波长精度控制每个阵列位点，通过确定性位移原子穿过驻波场连续调节单原子耦合强度。在最多五个原子耦合到腔的情况下进行测量。

Result: 实现了最多五个原子与腔的集体耦合，测量到集体增强的真空拉比劈裂，并成功实现了基于腔的非破坏性耦合原子数读出。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了用于腔介导纠缠生成和具有单原子控制能力的多体腔QED的可扩展架构，为光纤集成的量子网络节点奠定了基础。

Abstract: Strong atom-photon interactions in optical cavities are a key resource for quantum information processing, quantum networking, and the exploration of quantum optical effects. Optical tweezer arrays offer scalable, site-resolved control of neutral atoms, but their integration with high-cooperativity cavity QED systems remains challenging. Here we combine a twelve-site $^{87}$Rb optical tweezer array with a high-cooperativity fiber Fabry-Pérot microcavity. The array is positioned within the cavity mode and individual sites are controlled with subwavelength precision, enabling continuous tuning of the single-atom coupling strength via deterministic displacement through the standing-wave field. For up to five atoms coupled to the cavity, we measure collectively enhanced vacuum Rabi splitting and implement cavity-based non-destructive readout of the number of coupled atoms. These results establish a scalable architecture for cavity-mediated entanglement generation and many-body cavity QED with single-atom control, and they lay the foundation for fiber-integrated quantum network nodes.

</details>


### [164] [Variational Quantum Circuit Parameterization of SchNet: A Simulator-Based Feasibility Study for Conservative Molecular Force Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19532)
*Hoang - Anh Nguyen,Nhu - Duc Dinh,Viet - Hung Tran,Tu - Uyen Le Tu,Tien - Lam Pham,Van - Duy Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子SchNet架构（Hybrid Quantum SchNet），将变分量子电路模块集成到SchNet框架中，用于机器学习力场预测，在MD17基准上通过联合能量-力训练显著提升了能量和力的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习力场是加速分子模拟的有前景途径，但准确且能量守恒的力学习仍具挑战性，尤其是在有限数据下同时捕捉全局能量趋势和局部势能梯度。现有模型难以同时兼顾能量与力的精度。

Method: 将变分量子电路（VQC）模块插入SchNet框架的滤波器生成器、原子级更新和读出变换中，使量子增强特征映射能够贡献于距离依赖的相互作用和原子能量预测，同时保持力的能量梯度公式。采用联合能量-力监督训练。

Result: 在MD17的八个分子系统上使用每分子1000个训练构型进行评估。联合能量-力训练相比仅能量训练大幅提升精度：能量MAE从2.567降至0.593 kcal/mol，力MAE从16.340降至1.540 kcal/mol·Å⁻¹。消融实验表明性能取决于量子电路宽度、深度与优化稳定性之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 变分量子电路可以被整合到神经力场架构中并端到端训练，以改进分子能量和力的预测，展示了量子-经典混合模型在分子模拟中的潜力。

Abstract: Machine-learning force fields provide a promising route for accelerating molecular simulation by replacing expensive quantum-chemical calculations with differentiable models of molecular energies and atomic forces. However, learning accurate and energy-conserving forces remains challenging, especially when the model must capture both global energy trends and local potential-energy gradients from limited data. In this work, we propose a Hybrid Quantum SchNet architecture that integrates variational quantum circuit modules into the continuous-filter SchNet framework. Quantum modules are inserted into the filter generator, atom-wise update, and readout transformations, allowing quantum-enhanced feature mappings to contribute to distance-dependent interactions and atomic energy prediction while preserving the energy-gradient formulation of forces. The model is evaluated on eight MD17 molecular systems using 1000 training configurations per molecule. Compared with energy-only training, joint energy--force supervision substantially improves both energy and force prediction accuracy. Compared with energy-only training, joint energy--force supervision substantially improves both energy and force prediction accuracy. Averaged over the benchmark, the energy MAE decreases from 2.567 to 0.593 kcal mol$^{-1}$, while the force MAE decreases from 16.340 to 1.540 kcal mol$^{-1}$ Å$^{-1}$. Ablation experiments on ethanol further show that the performance of the hybrid model depends on the balance between quantum circuit width, circuit depth, and optimization stability. These results demonstrate that variational quantum circuits can be incorporated into neural force-field architectures and trained end-to-end to improve molecular energy and force prediction.

</details>


### [165] [Quantum Energy Storage versus Heat-to-Work Conversion in an Interacting Spin System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19533)
*Omar Bachain,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在统一微观框架下研究了相互作用双量子比特系统同时作为量子电池和量子奥托热机的性能，发现偶极相互作用的增强会显著提升量子电池的最大可提取功（ergotropy）和存储容量，但会降低奥托循环的最大功提取，表明量子储能能力的增强并不必然意味着热功转换效率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池和量子热机是量子热力学领域的两个重要方向，但以往研究通常将它们分开处理。作者希望在同一个物理平台上统一研究两者，探究微观自旋相互作用对各能量转换形式的不同影响，从而揭示量子储能与热功转换之间的互补关系。

Method: 以各向异性海森堡哈密顿量（辅以偶极相互作用、对称自旋轨道相互作用和外磁场）描述工作介质，采用两种协议：(1) 相干幺正充电协议，通过ergotropy、anti-ergotropy、充电功率、存储容量和ℓ1-范数量子相干性表征储能性能；(2) 量子奥托循环，研究吸收/释放热量、净功和热力学效率随磁场调制、偶极相互作用和温度偏差的变化。

Result: 在所研究的参数区间内，增大偶极相互作用显著增强量子电池的最大ergotropy和存储容量，但奥托循环的最大提取功随之降低；奥托效率对偶极相互作用呈非单调依赖且始终低于卡诺极限。

Conclusion: 量子储能能力的增强并不必然带来热功转换效率的提升，量子电池与量子热机具有互补性质；微观自旋相互作用可在同一物理平台上灵活调控不同形式的量子能量转换。

Abstract: We investigate the energetic and thermodynamic performance of an interacting two-qubit system serving as both a quantum battery and a quantum Otto heat engine. The working medium is described by an anisotropic Heisenberg Hamiltonian supplemented by a dipolar interaction, a symmetric spin--orbit interaction, and an external magnetic field. Within a unified microscopic framework, we first analyze a coherent unitary charging protocol and characterize the resulting energy-storage performance through the ergotropy, anti-ergotropy, charging power, storage capacity, and $\ell_1$-norm of quantum coherence. We investigate the effects of the dipolar interaction, temperature, and magnetic field on these quantities. We then employ the same working medium in a quantum Otto cycle and study the absorbed and released heat, net work, and thermodynamic efficiency as functions of the magnetic-field modulation, dipolar interaction, and temperature bias. A direct comparison between the two protocols reveals a pronounced contrast in their response to the dipolar interaction. In the investigated parameter regime, increasing the dipolar interaction substantially enhances the maximum ergotropy and storage capacity of the quantum battery, whereas the maximum work extracted per Otto cycle decreases. The Otto efficiency exhibits a nonmonotonic dependence on the dipolar interaction while remaining below the Carnot bound. These results demonstrate that an enhancement of quantum energy-storage capability does not necessarily imply an enhancement of heat-to-work conversion. Our findings highlight the complementary nature of quantum batteries and quantum heat engines and show how microscopic spin interactions can
  be used to control different forms of quantum energy conversion within
  the same physical platform.

</details>


### [166] [High fidelity control of superconducting qubits with optical transmitted signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19602)
*Yu-Huai Li,Daojin Fan,Na Li,Fusheng Chen,Shaowei Li,Dong-Dong Li,Yu Xu,Jin Lin,Ming Gong,He-Liang Huang,Hui Deng,Yulin Wu,Haoran Qian,Shaojun Guo,Futian Liang,Xiaobo Zhu,Cheng-Zhi Peng,Jian-Wei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验实现了基于光辅助传输线的超导量子比特控制方案，用激光强度调制替代同轴电缆传输微波信号，在稀释制冷机中通过光电流再生信号，实现了单比特门（99.915%）和双比特门（99.676%）的高保真度控制，达到表面码容错阈值要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路在量子模拟和量子计算中展现出巨大潜力，但大规模系统中信号线数量的增加导致稀释制冷机面临空间和热负载的严重限制，传统同轴电缆方案难以扩展。

Method: 采用光学辅助传输线替代同轴电缆：在室温下将微波信号调制到激光强度上，通过光纤传输至稀释制冷机的低温平台，再利用光电效应再生微波信号，驱动频率可调的transmon超导量子比特。

Result: 实验实现了对超导量子比特的完整控制，单量子比特门保真度为99.915%±0.005%，双量子比特门保真度为99.676%±0.041%，均达到表面码容错计算的要求。

Conclusion: 光辅助传输线方案在保持高保真度的同时大幅减少了制冷机内的信号线数量，为大规模超导量子计算系统的可扩展性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Superconducting circuits exhibit remarkable potential for constructing large-scale quantum simulation and computation systems, featuring numerous qubits, extended coherence time, and precise control. Nevertheless, the growing number of signal cables poses a challenge in dilution refrigerators due to space and heat load constraints. To overcome this issue, we experimentally implemented an optically-assisted transmission line as an alternative to coaxial cables. By modulating microwave signals on laser intensities at room temperature and regenerating the signals at a cryogenic plate within the dilution refrigerator, we demonstrated full control of superconducting qubits using photocurrent. We demonstrate and benchmark both single-qubit and two-qubit gates on frequency tunable transmon qubits, achieving fidelities of 99.915% $\pm$ 0.005% and 99.676% $\pm$ 0.041%, respectively, which have reached the requirement of the surface code.

</details>


### [167] [Variational Quantum Linear Solver via Block Encoding for the Poisson Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19655)
*Viraj Dsouza,Ayush Singhal,Alex Khan,Rut Lineswala,Abhishek Chopra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于精确块编码离散拉普拉斯算子的变分量子线性求解器（VQLS），用于求解泊松方程，并在物理驱动的基准问题上验证了其性能。与传统LCU方法需要O(L²)个不同电路不同，该方法仅需单个电路即可完成代价函数评估，并实证研究了经典优化器选择对变分优化停滞点的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松方程在科学计算中应用广泛（如热传导、流体力学），量子线性求解器有望提供加速。传统基于LCU分解的VQLS每次代价函数评估需要O(L²)个不同电路，其中L是离散拉普拉斯算子的LCU分解项数，电路开销随项数平方增长，限制了实际可扩展性。因此需要更高效、更简洁的VQLS方案。

Method: 采用精确块编码（exact block encoding）构造离散拉普拉斯算子，替代LCU分解方式构建VQLS。该编码方式使得代价函数评估仅需单个量子电路即可完成，避免了LCU方案中O(L²)个电路的开销。作者系统比较了不同经典优化器对变分优化收敛行为及停滞点的影响，并在三种物理基准问题上进行了评测：1）具有正弦外力项的泊松方程（Dirichlet边界）；2）带局部高斯热源的稳态热传导问题（Dirichlet边界）；3）二维顶盖驱动空腔流的压力-泊松方程（Neumann边界，每时间步调用一次求解器）。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的基于块编码的VQLS在三个基准问题上均能有效求解泊松方程。同时，实证展示了经典优化器的选择对变分优化何时停止进步（即停滞点）具有显著影响，说明优化器选择是VQLS实际性能的关键因素之一。

Conclusion: 基于精确块编码的VQLS为求解泊松方程提供了一种比LCU方案更具电路效率的途径（单电路代价评估），并在物理相关的基准问题上展现出可行性。优化器选择对变分优化的收敛行为有重要影响，值得在实际应用中仔细调优。

Abstract: We present a variational quantum linear solver (VQLS) for the Poisson equation built on an exact block encoding of the discrete Laplacian, and demonstrate its performance on physically motivated benchmarks. Unlike LCU-based VQLS where the number of distinct circuits required per cost-function evaluation is $\mathcal{O}(L^2)$, where $L$ is the number of terms in the LCU decomposition of the discrete Laplacian operator, this approach requires only a single circuit for cost evaluation. We further empirically demonstrate that the choice of classical optimizer materially affects where the variational optimization ceases to make progress. The solver is benchmarked on three problems: a Poisson equation with sinusoidal forcing and a steady-state heat conduction problem with a localized Gaussian source, both with Dirichlet boundaries, and the pressure-Poisson equation of a two-dimensional lid-driven cavity flow, in which the solver is invoked once per time step under Neumann boundary conditions.

</details>


### [168] [Tight Entropy Contraction of Generalized Quantum Depolarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19685)
*Li Gao,Long Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了广义量子退极化信道和半群的相对熵收缩的上下界，给出了收缩率在维度常数方面的一阶渐近紧界，并扩展到完全熵收缩率和张量稳定估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对熵收缩是量子信息论中的重要概念，用于刻画量子动力学过程的信息耗散速率。对于广义量子退极化信道，此前缺乏紧致的相对熵收缩上下界，特别是在维度常数方面的一阶渐近行为尚不明确。

Method: 上界估计基于尖锐反比不等式和两个态的相对熵凸性，可通过最近引入的Hockey-Stick量子f-散度推导，也可独立地通过Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori量子Fisher信息度规得到；下界估计基于一般条件期望下存在达到指标的纯态这一事实。

Result: 获得了广义量子退极化信道的相对熵收缩率紧界，在维度常数方面提供了一阶渐近最优估计；结果扩展到完全熵收缩率以及乘积动力学的张量稳定估计；具体应用包括量子退极化、退相位和紧群对称化，并可推导相干性和不对称性衰减率的推论。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入量子f-散度和量子Fisher信息度规等现代工具，系统解决了广义量子退极化动力学中相对熵收缩的定量刻画问题，其紧界和扩展结果对量子信息处理中相干性衰减和对称性破缺的理解具有重要价值。

Abstract: We establish upper and lower bounds for relative entropy contraction of generalized quantum depolarizing channels and semigroups. Our bound provides tight first order asymptotic of the contraction rate in terms of the dimension constant. One side estimate are based on sharp reverse ratio and convexity of relative entropy of two states, which can be derived from the recently introduced Hockey-Stick quantum $f$-divergence, and also independently, Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori quantum Fisher information metric. The other side follows from the existence of index achieving pure state with respect to a general conditional expectation. Our results extend to the complete entropy contraction rate, tensor stable estimates for product dynamics. Examples include quantum depolarization, dephasing, and compact group symmetrization, with consequences for the decay rate of coherence and asymmetry.

</details>


### [169] [Multiplexing of Continuous-Variable and Discrete-Variable Quantum Key Distribution Systems over Fibered and Free-Space Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19745)
*Mattia Sabatini,Edoardo Rossi,Matías R. Bolaños,Francesco Vedovato,Thomas Liege,Eleni Diamanti,Giuseppe Vallone,Paolo Villoresi,Yoann Piétri,Marco Avesani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实验实现了CV-QKD和DV-QKD两种协议在同一光信道上的波分复用（WDM）同时运行，使用标准C波段DWDM滤波器，在光纤和620米城市白天自由空间链路上验证了混合CV-DV WDM作为异构量子通信网络的实用构建模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来量子通信基础设施需要在共享物理信道上服务异构用户：短距离高吞吐链路适合CV-QKD，而长距离高损耗链路适合DV-QKD。两种协议在同一信道上的WDM共存由于噪声敏感性差异巨大而面临挑战，且此前从未经过实验验证。

Method: 在1550.12 nm（CV）和1545.32 nm（DV）波长上，使用标准C波段DWDM滤波器将两个独立的CV-QKD和DV-QKD系统在同一光信道中同时运行，分别在光纤和620米城市白天自由空间链路上进行联合操作的实验验证。

Result: 在光纤上，两系统呈现预期的互补性，在7.56 dB信道损耗处交叉，均提供约1.43 Mbit/s密钥率；在白天自由空间中，两者在时变大气衰减下均维持Mbit/s级密钥率。所有配置下均未观察到可测量的复用引起的QBER或额外噪声惩罚。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了混合CV-DV WDM作为异构量子通信网络的实用构建模块，使城市高吞吐用户和长距离骨干链路能够在单一物理基础设施上同时获得服务。

Abstract: Future quantum communication infrastructures will need to serve heterogeneous users on shared physical channels: short-range, high-throughput links favor Continuous-Variable Quantum Key Distribution (CV-QKD), while long-reach, high-loss links remain the domain of Discrete-Variable QKD (DV-QKD). Wavelength-division multiplexing (WDM) of the two protocols on a common channel would address both regimes simultaneously, but their markedly different noise sensitivities make coexistence non-trivial and, to date, experimentally untested. Here we report the first simultaneous operation of two independent CV- and DV-QKD systems on a common optical channel, using standard C-band DWDM filters at 1550.12 nm (CV) and 1545.32 nm (DV). We demonstrate joint operation on both optical fiber and a 620 m urban daylight free-space link. On fiber, the two systems exhibit the expected complementarity, crossing over at 7.56 dB of channel loss where both deliver $\sim$1.43 Mbit/s; in daylight free-space, both sustain Mbit/s key rates under time-varying atmospheric attenuation. Across all configurations we observe no measurable multiplexing-induced penalty in QBER or excess noise. These results establish hybrid CV-DV WDM as a practical building block for heterogeneous quantum communication networks, where metropolitan high-throughput users and long-reach backbone links can be served on a single physical infrastructure.

</details>


### [170] [An Irreducible Quantum Advantage in Aligning World Models with Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19779)
*Josep Lumbreras,Hailan Ma,Jayne Thompson,Mile Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明基于经典记忆的世界模型在复杂环境中始终存在策略对齐失败，而使用单个qutrit（三维量子比特）的量子世界模型可以精确复现真实世界，确保真实与虚拟世界的最优策略完美对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型作为真实世界的数字模拟器，用于在现实部署前训练和测试智能体。在结果取决于遥远过去事件的复杂环境中，模型需要记忆。然而，经典世界模型在需要记忆的环境中是否能够始终准确地对齐真实与虚拟世界的最优策略，这一问题尚不明确，本文旨在探究经典记忆的局限性。

Method: 作者构造了特定的真实世界环境，证明了任何有限的经典世界模型在这些环境上都会失败：要么丧失区分动作的能力，要么反复将最高期望奖励分配给次优动作，且期望奖励估计存在非零平均误差。同时，作者展示了每个这样的真实世界都可以用单个qutrit的量子世界模型精确复现。

Result: 证明了对于构造的真实世界，所有有限经典记忆模型都会沿着同一可能轨迹失败，无法对齐真实与虚拟世界的最优策略；而量子世界模型（仅使用单个qutrit）的奖励估计和偏好动作始终与真实世界一致，保证最优策略完美对齐。

Conclusion: 经典世界模型即使在真实世界本身是经典的情况下，也存在固有的记忆容量局限，无法总是准确地模拟真实世界；量子世界模型则可以通过极小的资源（单个qutrit）克服这一局限，实现真实与虚拟世界最优策略的精确对齐。

Abstract: World models provide digital simulacra of the true world, allowing agents to be trained and tested before costly real-world deployment. At each time step, they receive an action and generate an observation and reward matching the statistics of the true world. In complex environments where present outcomes depend on events far in the past, this requires memory. One might expect that, by increasing memory, we can always build a model accurately enough to align the optimal agent policies of the real and virtual worlds. We show that this is false for classical world models, even when the true world itself is classical. We construct true worlds for which every finite classical model fails along the same possible trajectory: it either loses the ability to distinguish actions when the true world clearly prefers one, or repeatedly assigns the highest expected reward to suboptimal actions. Its expected-reward estimates also retain a nonvanishing average error. In contrast, each such true world admits a quantum world model using a single qutrit that reproduces it exactly: its reward estimates and preferred actions always match those of the true world, ensuring that the optimal policies of the real and virtual worlds remain perfectly aligned.

</details>


### [171] [Constant-round quantum advantage in communication complexity for total functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19787)
*Atsuya Hasegawa,François Le Gall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了存在一个全函数，其随机通信复杂性与常轮量子通信复杂性之间存在多项式差距，而此前这一分离仅对使用多项式轮数的量子协议成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信复杂性中，随机与量子协议之间的分离此前仅在协议轮数允许为多项式时成立，而常轮（即轮数受限于常数）情形下的分离是否成立一直是一个开放问题。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造一个具体的全函数，并对其随机通信复杂性和常轮量子通信复杂性进行对比分析，证明两者之间存在多项式级别的分离。

Result: 成功证明了存在一个全函数，使得随机与常轮量子通信复杂性之间存在多项式分离，扩展了此前仅对多项式轮数量子协议成立的已知结果。

Conclusion: 该结果将量子通信复杂性中随机与量子协议分离的界限从多项式轮数推广到常轮情形，深化了我们对量子通信复杂性与经典随机通信复杂性之间差距的理解。

Abstract: We show that there exists a total function for which there is a polynomial gap between the randomized and the constant-round quantum communication complexity. Previously, such a separation was known only for quantum protocols using polynomially many rounds.

</details>


### [172] [Optomechanically induced transparency in the presence of a strong Duffing nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19811)
*Rong Zhang,Jing Qiu,Stefano Chesi,Yingdan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Duffing非线性与光机械相互作用对腔密度态（DOS）的联合影响，该影响可通过光机械诱导透明（OMIT）实验直接观测。两种非线性都引入了极化激元之间的同类相互作用，在光学响应中产生独特特征。作者推导了增强极化激元间非线性散射的共振条件，详细研究了腔DOS的参数依赖性，从而刻画了在OMIT实验中观测Duffing非线性量子效应的典型强度和最优条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Duffing非线性与光机械相互作用对腔密度态（DOS）的联合效应，该效应可直接在OMIT实验中观测，旨在表征Duffing非线性量子效应的典型强度并确定最优观测条件。

Method: 推导增强极化激元间非线性散射的共振条件，并系统研究腔DOS对系统参数的依赖关系。

Result: 发现两种非线性均引入极化激元间同类相互作用，在光学响应中产生可区分的特征；确定了增强非线性散射的共振条件及观测量子效应的最优参数区间。

Conclusion: 通过腔DOS分析和共振条件推导，明确了在OMIT实验中观测Duffing非线性量子效应的可行性和最优实验条件。

Abstract: We consider the combined effect of a Duffing nonlinearity and the optomechanical interaction on the cavity density of states (DOS), which is directly observable in optomechanically induced transparency (OMIT) experiments. Both nonlinearities introduce the same type of interaction between polaritons, producing a distinct feature in the optical response. We derive the resonant condition that enhances the nonlinear scattering between polaritons and study the parameter dependence of the cavity DOS in detail. This allows us to characterize the typical strength and optimal conditions for observing the quantum effects of the Duffing nonlinearity in OMIT experiments.

</details>


### [173] [Distributed Variational Quantum Eigensolver: Embarrassingly Parallel strategies on NISQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19824)
*Marta Losada,Daniel Faílde,Andrés Gómez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了变分量子特征求解器（VQE）在分布式并行化中的策略，利用CUNQA平台在虚拟QPU上进行仿真，评估了三种可并行化策略（shot级、电路级和候选级）在异构NISQ噪声下的速度与精度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE需要大量电路执行，非常适合分布式并行化，但NISQ设备上的异构噪声会扭曲结果和效率，因此需要系统评估不同并行化策略在噪声环境下的表现。

Method: 使用CUNQA平台对虚拟QPU进行仿真，实现了三种embarrassingly并行化策略：(1) shot级并行（针对测量采样）；(2) 电路级并行（针对梯度和可观测量的计算）；(3) 候选级并行（针对群体优化器）。从加速比和精度等指标进行评估。

Result: 在CUNQA仿真平台上评估了三种并行化策略在不同异构噪声虚拟QPU上的加速比和精度表现，揭示了各策略的优劣。

Conclusion: 三种并行化策略在NISQ异构噪声环境下的VQE执行具有不同的效率和精度权衡，为分布式量子计算中的VQE并行化提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Variational Quantum Eigensolver requires many circuit executions, making it ideal for distributed parallelization. However, heterogeneous noise in NISQ devices can skew results and efficiency. Using the CUNQA platform for emulation of virtual QPUs, we evaluate three embarrassingly parallelization strategies (shot-level, circuit-level for gradients and observables and candidate level for population-based optimizers) across metrics like speedup and accuracy.

</details>


### [174] [Disassembling qLDPC codes for depth-optimal parity-check circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19917)
*Minh T. P. Nguyen,Maximilian Rimbach-Russ,Stefano Bosco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用qLDPC码Tanner图边对称性来设计症候提取电路的方法，无需从完整码出发，而是从底层组件构建。对于提升积码和平衡积码，该方法可获得解析构造且具有最优或近似最优的CNOT深度；对于量子Tanner码，在所有测试实例（包括近600个数据量子比特的码）上均达到深度最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子低密度奇偶校验码是实现可扩展容错量子计算的有前景途径，但其实际实现需要高效的症候提取电路。许多qLDPC码族通过显式构造从小组件组装而成，这些构造在Tanner图上印刻了边对称性。然而现有方法均从完整量子码出发设计电路，未能利用这一对称性结构。

Method: 本文利用qLDPC码家族中Tanner图上的边对称性，提出从底层组件而非完整量子码来设计症候提取电路的方法。针对提升积码和平衡积码，该方法可给出解析构造；针对量子Tanner码，则通过测试验证其有效性。

Result: 对于提升积码和平衡积码，该方法产生具有可证明最优或近似最优CNOT深度的电路。对于量子Tanner码，在所有测试实例上均生成了深度最优电路，测试包含接近600个数据量子比特的码。

Conclusion: 通过利用qLDPC码构造中固有的边对称性，可以从组件层面高效设计深度最优或近最优的症候提取电路，避免了从完整码出发的复杂性，为大规模qLDPC码的实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes offer a promising route to scalable fault-tolerant quantum computing, but their practical implementation requires efficient circuits for syndrome extraction. Many qLDPC families are assembled from a small set of components through explicit constructions that imprint edge symmetries on their Tanner graphs. We show that these symmetries can be exploited to design syndrome-extraction circuits from the underlying components, rather than from the full quantum code. For Lifted Product and Balanced Product codes this approach yields an analytical construction with provably optimal or near-optimal CNOT depth. For Quantum Tanner codes it produces depth-optimal circuits on every instance we test, including codes up to nearly 600 data qubits.

</details>


### [175] [Entangling two qudits of arbitrary dimension through light-atomic Faraday interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19945)
*R. Surmay,V. A. Leonov,E. A. Vashukevich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究任意维度两个量子比特（qudit）之间的纠缠操作，其中一个编码于空间多模光场的状态中，另一个编码于携带轨道角动量（OAM）的原子集体自旋相干状态中。通过包含两次法拉第相互作用以及其间原子和光正交分量的旋转协议，实现了多种纠缠操作的生成。所有生成的量子门均可表示为d维SWAP^α_d门的有理数次幂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维量子系统（qudit）中纠缠操作的生成机制，利用光场与原子系综之间的相互作用实现可扩展、可调控的量子信息处理能力。

Method: 提出一个由两次法拉第相互作用组成的协议，在两次相互作用之间对原子和光的正交分量进行旋转，从而生成一系列纠缠操作。所有生成的量子门可表示为d维SWAP门的有理数次幂（SWAP^α_d）。分析了不同逻辑空间维度下两量子比特变换的概率，以及逻辑门不同纠缠功率下的表现。

Result: 成功在任意维度的qudit系统中生成多种纠缠操作，所有量子门均对应于SWAP^α_d门的有理性幂次。计算了不同维度逻辑空间和不同纠缠功率下两qudit变换的概率。

Conclusion: 该协议为高维量子纠缠操作的生成提供了一种通用且可扩展的方案，为基于OAM的光-原子混合量子信息处理奠定了基础。

Abstract: The paper investigates entangling operations acting on two qudits of arbitrary dimension, with one encoded in the states of a spatially multimode optical field, the other in those of atomic collective spin coherence, both carrying orbital angular momentum (OAM). We demonstrate the generation of a wide range of entangling operations within a protocol consisting of two Faraday interactions and a rotation of atomic and light quadratures between them. All generated gates can be represented as rational powers of the d-dimensional gate $SWAP^α_d$. The probabilities of two-qudit transformations are calculated for various dimensions of the logical space and for different entangling powers of the logical gates.

</details>


### [176] [Wide-field mid- to long-wave infrared imaging with undetected photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20015)
*Vladimir Kornienko,Nathan Gemmell,Caiyi Liu,Asteria Chen,Chris Phillips,Rupert Oulton*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在银硫代镓（AgGaS₂）晶体中利用非共线相位匹配实现6-10 μm（1670-1000 cm⁻¹）波段的宽场量子成像（QIUP），通过仅测量可见光干涉来探测中红外物体。结合相干探测与"未探测"红外光子，成像性能比背景限制红外光电探测（BLIP）极限好约100倍，在8 μm处实现超8000个可分辨元素、297 μm分辨率及10秒采集时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中红外波段成像在化学/生物传感等领域有重要应用，但传统红外探测器受限于背景噪声、低温工作条件和较低的分辨能力。量子成像与未探测光子（QIUP）技术允许使用成熟的硅基相机技术，通过测量可见光干涉来获取红外信息，从而规避高噪声红外探测器的限制，实现快速、无背景噪声、室温工作且具有光谱选择性的中红外成像。

Method: 采用银硫代镓（AgGaS₂）晶体实现非共线相位匹配，在6-10 μm波长范围内产生宽场QIUP。利用相干探测机制结合红外光子"未被探测"的特性（即仅测量纠缠光子对中的可见光部分），绕过红外探测器的背景限制，实现中红外宽场成像。

Result: 在8 μm波长下，成像系统拥有超过8000 ± 100个可分辨元素，分辨率为297 ± 5 μm，采集时间为10秒。成像灵敏度比背景限制红外光电探测（BLIP）极限提升约100倍，覆盖6-10 μm（1670-1000 cm⁻¹）的中红外指纹区。

Conclusion: 该工作为快速、无背景噪声、室温运行的宽场光谱选择性中红外成像奠定了基础，展示了基于硅探测器技术在中红外指纹区（6-10 μm）高性能成像的可行性，有望推动中红外成像在生物、医药、环境等领域的实际应用。

Abstract: Quantum imaging with undetected photons (QIUP) allows an object to be probed at mid-infrared frequencies by only measuring interference in the visible range, thus leveraging silicon camera technology. We show that non-collinear phase-matching in a silver thiogallate (AgGaS$_2$) crystal enables wide-field QIUP in the wavelength range of 6-10 $μ$m (1670-1000 cm$^{-1}$). A combination of coherent detection and infrared photons being ``undetected'' enables imaging at ${\sim}$100 times better than the background-limited infrared photodetection (BLIP) limit. At 8 $μ$m, our images have over 8000 $\pm$ 100 resolvable elements with a 297 $\pm$ 5 $μ$m resolution, and 10 s acquisition time. Our results pave the way to fast, background-noise-free, room-temperature, spectrally-selective mid-infrared imaging.

</details>


### [177] [Lensing and enhanced single atom detection via a single-pixel nanostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20032)
*Ling-Xiao Wang,Lei Xu,Ai-Ping Liu,Guang-Jie Chen,Yuan-Hao Yang,Jia-Qi Wang,Xin-Biao Xu,Guang-Can Guo,Chang-Ling Zou,Guo-Yong Xiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了一种基于单个纳米结构散射光相位梯度的纳米尺度透镜机制，利用单原子作为量子探针通过荧光采样近场强度，实现了4倍的单原子探测效率增强，为片上量子光学平台提供了新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纳米透镜技术通常依赖复杂的微纳结构阵列或多层设计，缺乏一种简单通用的机制来实现纳米尺度的光场聚焦与局域化。同时，表征近场聚焦光场需要非破坏性的高灵敏度探测手段，而传统方法难以在纳米尺度上精确采样光场分布。

Method: 提出基于单个纳米结构散射光在近场中产生相位梯度的透镜机制。实验上采用衬底上的光波导结构，以单个原子作为量子探针，通过测量原子荧光来采样近场强度分布，从而验证纳米透镜效应并表征聚焦光场。

Result: 实验结果验证了该纳米透镜机制的有效性，展示了4倍的单原子探测效率增强，证明了该技术作为非破坏性表征聚焦光学场的方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了片上纳米结构作为多功能量子光学平台的潜力，可高效路由光子、局域光场并增强原子-光子耦合，为捕获和操控单原子以及实现混合纳米光子-原子量子体系开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We propose and demonstrate a general mechanism for nanoscale lensing based on the phase gradient imposed by a single nanostructure scattering light in its near-field. We verify this effect using an optical waveguide on a substrate, with single atoms serving as quantum probes that sample the near-field intensity through their fluorescence. This quantum probing technique provides a unique, non-destructive approach to characterizing focused optical fields and reveals a 4-fold enhancement in single atom detection efficiency. This work establishes on-chip nanostructures as a multi-functional quantum optics platform that can efficiently route photons, localize fields, and enhance atom-photon coupling, offering new opportunities for trapping and manipulating single atoms and realizing hybrid nanophotonic-atomic systems for quantum applications.

</details>


### [178] [Radial Convex Geometry of Quantum States and Its Relation to Best Separable Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20050)
*Haonan Qiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子态的凸几何角度研究两体纠缠，以最大混合态为参考点定义了几何纠缠空间量G(ρ)和相对纠缠度Q(ρ)，并将其应用于最佳可分离近似（BSA），推导出一般不等式界，并证明BSA的纠缠分量在纠缠空间中的尺寸不小于原始混合态。对两量子比特态，利用PPT判据给出了紧致界，为BSA、鲁棒性与量子态空间纠缠区域之间的关系提供了简明几何描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从几何角度理解量子纠缠的度量与结构，特别是通过凸几何中可分离态与量子态集合的边界相对位置来刻画纠缠程度，建立BSA、纠缠鲁棒性等概念之间的统一几何框架。

Method: 以最大混合态为参考点，定义几何量G(ρ)（可分离边界与量子态边界的相对位置）和相对纠缠度Q(ρ)（与鲁棒性比较）；将其应用于BSA最优分解，推导不等式界；对两量子比特态利用PPT判据显式评估几何参数。

Result: 推导出一般界(1-p)L_B/[p(1-L_B)]≤p_0≤Q(ρ)，证明BSA纠缠分量的纠缠空间尺寸不小于原始混合态；对两量子比特态得到紧致界(1-p)/(2p)≤p_0≤Q(ρ)。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了BSA、纠缠鲁棒性与量子态空间纠缠区域之间简洁的几何关系，为两体纠缠的几何描述和量化提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study bipartite entanglement from the convex geometry of quantum states. Taking the maximally mixed state as a reference point, we define a geometric entangled-space quantity $G(ρ)=[1-L(ρ)]/L(ρ)$ from the relative positions of the separable and quantum-state boundaries, and introduce a relative entanglement degree $Q(ρ)=[(1-p)/p]/G(ρ)$ by comparing it with the robustness relative to the maximally mixed state.
  We apply this geometric construction to the Best Separable Approximation (BSA). For the optimal decomposition, we derive the general bound $(1-p)L_B/[p(1-L_B)]\leq p_0\leq Q(ρ)$ and show that the entangled component of the BSA has an entangled-space size no smaller than that of the original mixed state, namely $[1-L_R]/L_R\leq[1-L_B]/L_B$.
  For two-qubit states, the BSA entangled component is a pure entangled state. Using the PPT criterion, its geometric parameter can be evaluated explicitly, giving the bound $(1-p)/(2p)\leq p_0\leq Q(ρ)$. These results provide a simple geometric description of the relation between BSA, robustness, and the entangled region of the quantum-state space.

</details>


### [179] [PPT Entanglement with Correlated Catalysis: Monotones and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20063)
*Jingsong Ao,Aby Philip,Alexander Streltsov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子催化剂（特别是关联催化剂）在PPT（正部分转置）资源理论中对态操纵的影响，证明了正则化PPT相对熵是强超可加的，并表明即使允许任意关联催化剂，混合纠缠态的渐近可逆性仍无法恢复，纠缠蒸馏率仍严格小于纠缠代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子催化剂可以在不被消耗的情况下帮助完成原本不可能的量子态变换，而允许催化剂与输出关联会使其能力大幅增强。这引发了一个关于纠缠理论的基本问题：当此类关联催化剂自由可用时，态操纵的哪些限制仍然存在？回答这一问题有助于理解混合态纠缠操纵的根本边界。

Method: 该论文在PPT资源理论框架下进行分析（该理论允许比LOCC更广泛的操作类别），识别了正则化相对熵度量变为强超可加性的一般条件，并利用这些条件构造了在不需要知道催化剂任何信息的情况下约束关联催化PPT变换的单调量。特别地，证明了正则化PPT相对熵的完全可加性和强超可加性，解决了纠缠理论中的一个开放问题。

Result: 证明正则化PPT相对熵具有完全可加性和强超可加性；这些约束表明即使使用任意关联催化剂，渐近可逆性仍无法恢复——对于显式态，最优纠缠蒸馏率仍严格小于纠缠代价。

Conclusion: 即使提供强大的催化辅助（包括关联催化剂），混合态纠缠操纵的某些根本限制依然存在，催化帮助无法消除这些基本局限性。

Abstract: Quantum catalysts can overcome otherwise impossible quantum state transformations without being consumed, and allowing them to become correlated with the output makes this assistance substantially more powerful. This raises a fundamental question for entanglement theory: which limitations on state manipulation remain when such correlated catalysts are freely available? We answer this question in the positive-partial-transpose (PPT) resource theory, which allows a substantially broader class of operations than local operations and classical communication (LOCC). We identify general conditions under which regularized relative-entropy measures become strongly superadditive, and use them to construct monotones that constrain correlated catalytic PPT transformations without any knowledge of the catalyst. In particular, we prove that the regularized PPT relative entropy is fully additive and strongly superadditive, resolving an open problem in entanglement theory. Most importantly, these constraints show that even arbitrary correlated catalysts cannot restore asymptotic reversibility: for an explicit state, the optimal entanglement distillation rate remains strictly smaller than the entanglement cost. Thus, substantial catalytic assistance does not remove some of the fundamental limitations of mixed-state entanglement manipulation.

</details>


### [180] [Shadow models of a quantum model for cloud cover and the influence of finite sampling noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20076)
*Hedwig Keller,Mierk Schwabe,Veronika Eyring*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将量子机器学习（QML）模型的"影子"（即经典模型）用于混合量子-经典计算的方法，通过离散傅里叶变换和截断部分傅里叶级数来经典重构QML模型，并在真实量子系统Euro-Q-Exa上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算硬件受限，独立量子应用稀少，混合量子-经典计算（尤其是QML）是必要路径。然而将训练好的QML模型集成到经典应用中（如气候模型中的云量预测）面临挑战，因此需要构造QML模型的经典"影子"来模拟其输入输出关系，使量子资源仅在训练阶段需要。

Method: 采用无显式训练或回归阶段的构造性影子方法：将QML模型（量子傅里叶模型/电路）表示为部分傅里叶级数，利用编码策略允许的离散傅里叶变换高效地经典重构电路；通过截断部分傅里叶级数进一步减小影子模型规模。将经典插值方法与量子傅里叶模型的近似方法进行了比较，并在基于IQM Radiance超导量子比特的Euro-Q-Exa量子系统上计算影子模型。

Result: 两种影子方法在特定条件下都具有缓解有限采样噪声的效果，表明影子模型在超越硬件受限时代之后仍有应用动机。在Euro-Q-Exa量子系统上观察到误差缓解效应，但难以将其与系统标定相关的误差区分开来。

Conclusion: 构造性影子方法能够有效实现QML模型与经典应用的耦合，且具有噪声缓解的额外优势，即使在量子硬件成熟后仍具有价值；但在当前硬件上噪声与标定误差的区分仍是挑战。

Abstract: Quantum computing is a quickly growing field that is promising various advantages compared to conventional computing. However, currently stand-alone quantum applications are scarce and hybrid (quantum-classical) computing is needed, especially in quantum machine learning (QML). Due to current limitations of quantum computing hardware and the coupling between HPC and quantum devices, integrating a trained (QML) model in classical applications is challenging. In this case, it is helpful to couple so-called shadows of the QML model instead, i.e., classical models that imitate the input-output relations of QML models such that quantum resources are only needed during the training stage. Here we consider constructive shadowing processes without an explicit training or regression stage to avoid rendering the QML model redundant, and apply them to a previously developed QML model for cloud cover [1] to allow for an efficient coupling to a climate model. We compare classical interpolation methods to an approximation of the quantum Fourier model, the representation of the circuit as a partial Fourier series. The encoding strategy in [1] allows the use of the discrete Fourier transform to efficiently reconstruct the circuits classically. Truncating the partial Fourier series further reduces the size of the shadow models. Both methods have the effect of mitigating finite sampling noise under certain conditions, which yields a motivation to use shadow models also beyond the era of limited hardware availability. Further, we compute the shadow models on the quantum system Euro-Q-Exa, based on the IQM Radiance system with superconducting qubits, where error mitigating effects can also be observed, albeit it is still difficult to distinguish them from errors connected to the calibration of the system.

</details>


### [181] [Squeezed- and coherent-state quantum key distribution over a deployed hybrid fibre-free-space channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20088)
*Dnan A. E. Hajomer,Huy Q. Nguyen,Ivan Derkach,Andreas B. Kidmose,Edoardo Rossi,Mattia Sabatini,Yoann Pietri,Marco Avesani,Francesco Vedovato,Michael Hentschel,Radim Filip,Giuseppe Vallone,Vladyslav Usenko,Tobias Gehring,Soren Forchhammer,Paolo Villoresi,Ulrik L. Andersen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在部署的混合信道（620米自由空间链路+2公里光纤，总损耗高达20 dB）上，演示了本地生成本振的连续变量量子密钥分发（CV-QKD），同时支持高斯调制相干态和压缩态两种协议。通过将信道适配从光学层迁移到后处理层，采用统一的适应性后处理框架（基于透射率聚类的信号分组、协方差矩阵平均/去衰落处理、速率自适应盲协调），在相干态和压缩态协议下分别实现了0.42 Mbit/s和0.93 Mbit/s的渐近密钥率，并首次在部署的大气信道上建立了压缩态CV-QKD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要结合光纤和自由空间链路，但CV-QKD此前主要针对单一介质开发，跨光纤-自由空间级联信道的操作基本未被探索。光纤传输稳定允许长处理间隔，而大气传输引入透射率波动（在短时间内尺度上退化安全性），两种介质要求截然相反，迫切需要一种统一的解决方案来支撑异构量子网络。

Method: 提出统一的自适应后处理框架，将信道适配从光学层转移到数据后处理层：(1) 透射率聚类（transmittance-based clustering）对视场分组；(2) 通过协方差矩阵平均（residual-fading mitigation）或去衰落（de-fading）处理残余衰落；(3) 采用速率自适应盲协调（rate-adaptive blind reconciliation），该协调单独可恢复多达19%的额外密钥。该框架同时应用于相干态和高斯调制压缩态两种协议，并分别考虑其不同的安全分析和统计要求。

Result: 在620米自由空间+2公里部署光纤的混合信道（总损耗高达20 dB）上，相干态协议达到0.42 Mbit/s渐近密钥率，压缩态协议达到0.93 Mbit/s渐近密钥率，并在部署的大气信道上首次实现了压缩态CV-QKD。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对传输介质的适配可以很大程度上转移到数据处理层来完成，为跨越光纤、地面自由空间和卫星链路的异构量子网络提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum networks will combine optical fibre with free-space links, yet continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) has been developed predominantly for one medium or the other, while operation across concatenated fibre-free-space channels remains largely unexplored. The two media impose contrasting requirements: fibre transmission is stable and permits long processing intervals, whereas atmospheric propagation imposes transmittance fluctuations that degrade security and must be resolved on short timescales. Here we demonstrate a locally generated local oscillator CV-QKD with both Gaussian-modulated coherent and squeezed states over a deployed hybrid channel comprising a 620-m free-space link and 2 km of deployed fibre, with a total loss up to 20 dB. Rather than adapting the optics to each medium, we move channel adaptation to the post-processing, through a unified adaptive post-processing framework coupling transmittance-based clustering, residual-fading mitigation by covariance-matrix averaging or de-fading, and rate-adaptive blind reconciliation, which alone recovers up to 19% additional key. The same adaptive-processing principle is applied to both protocols, while accounting for their different security analyses and statistical requirements, yielding asymptotic secret-key rates of 0.42 Mbit per sec for the coherent-state protocol and 0.93 Mbit per sec for the squeezed-state protocol under the respective channel conditions, and establishing squeezed-state CV-QKD over a deployed atmospheric channel. These results show that adaptation to the transmission medium can largely be transferred to the data-processing layer, providing a route towards heterogeneous quantum networks spanning fibre, terrestrial free-space and satellite links.

</details>


### [182] [Counterexamples to the fractional coloring conjecture for triply efficient shadow tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20113)
*Jędrzej Stempin,Santiago Llorens,Felix Huber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造一族状态和可观测量，证明了King等人提出的关于分数染色数上界（χ_f·ε² ≤ C）的猜想14是错的——不存在满足该界的有限常数C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数图染色在Pauli可观测量的影子断层扫描中具有重要应用。如果King等人提出的猜想（关于反对易图的分数色数χ_f与期望阈值ε之间的关系χ_f·ε²≤C）成立，将意味着存在三重高效的Pauli影子断层扫描算法。因此验证或推翻该猜想对于量子态层析的实际可行性至关重要。

Method: 作者构造了一个状态族和可观测量族，证明不存在有限常数C满足χ_f·ε²≤C。更一般地，他们利用图的交换指标（commutation index）或β数给出了推广构造。证明的关键成分是放大技巧（amplification trick），通过字典序图乘积（lexicographic graph products）同时放大分数色数、β数和期望值。

Result: 成功构造出反例，证明该猜想为假：存在一族状态和可观测量，使得分数色数与ε²的乘积无界增长，不存在有限常数C。

Conclusion: 该猜想被推翻，意味着基于该猜想的"任意Pauli可观测量子集具有三重高效影子断层扫描算法"的推论不再成立，需要寻找新的途径或修正猜想条件。

Abstract: Fractional graph colorings are useful for the Shadow tomography of Pauli observables. In practice, it is desirable that any experimentally interesting set of Pauli operators has a small fractional chromatic number $χ_{f}$ for its anticommutation graph. Conjecture 13 in King, Gosset, Kothari, and Babbush [PRX Quantum 6, 010336 (2025)] states that if $B_ε(\varrho)$ is the set of Pauli observables having expectation value magnitude at least $ε$ in some given quantum state $\varrho$, then the fractional chromatic number of the anticommutation graph $G$ induced by $B_ε(\varrho)$ is $O(ε^{-2})$. In other words, it asserts that there exists a constant $C$ such that $χ_{f} \cdot ε^2 \leq C$ on all states and graphs. If the conjecture were true, it would imply that there exists a triply efficient Pauli shadow tomography algorithm for {\it any} subset $S$ of Pauli observables, provided that there is also an efficient fractional coloring algorithm for the set $B_ε$. Here we show that the conjecture is false by constructing a family of states and observables for which no finite $C$ satisfying the bound exists. We also give a more general construction relying on the commutation index or $β$ number of a graph. The key ingredient in the proofs can be seen as an instance of the amplification trick, where fractional chromatic numbers, $β$ numbers, and expectation values are amplified through lexicographic graph products.

</details>


### [183] [SAKE: Spectral Autodiff Kernel Expansion for Geometric Liouvillian Transport. A Differential-Geometric Framework for Response Transport in Quantum Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20132)
*Eric R. Bittner,Carlos Silva-Acuna,Hao Li,Simon Paiva-Ortega*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了光谱自微分核展开（SAKE）框架，这是一个可微分的计算框架，用于在相邻量子动力学模型之间传输非线性光谱响应。通过结合前向模式自动微分和Duhamel传输理论，SAKE构建了围绕参考模型的局部传输展开，避免了为每个哈密顿量或Liouville算符重新计算多维光谱的需要，并在四能级激子二聚体上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的非线性光谱计算方法需要对每个不同的哈密顿量或Liouville算符独立地重新计算多维光谱，这在参数空间探索、灵敏度分析和逆向设计等应用中计算成本高昂。亟需一种可微分的计算框架，能够高效地在相邻量子动力学模型之间传输光谱响应，并揭示光谱背后的机制信息。

Method: SAKE框架结合了两种核心技术：(1) 前向模式自动微分，用于生成参数依赖的Liouville算符的一阶、二阶和三阶导数；(2) Duhamel传输理论，用于构建路径传输算子（pathway transport operator），该算子将参考模型的非线性响应映射到相邻系统上。这些导数被组装成传输算子，实现对双面Feynman路径的映射和传输。

Result: 该框架在一个具有su(2)×su(2)对称性的四能级激子二聚体上进行了验证。通过将二阶和三阶传输路径算子与直接计算得到的精确投影传输矩阵进行比较，发现三阶展开能够精确复现投影传输算子及其相关的路径混合行为。此外，传输算子还揭示了相干和耗散扰动如何在双面Feynman路径之间重新分配振幅，展示了从非线性光谱中无法直接观察到的机制信息。

Conclusion: SAKE建立了一个可微分的非线性光谱计算框架，支持高效的局部参数探索、灵敏度分析以及未来的逆向设计应用。它不仅提供了一种高效的计算策略，还通过传输算子揭示了扰动对Feynman路径振幅重分配的机制性洞察，为非线性光谱的计算和解释开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce the Spectral Autodiff Kernel Expansion (SAKE), a differentiable computational framework for transporting nonlinear spectroscopic response between neighboring quantum dynamical models. Rather than recomputing multidimensional spectra independently for each Hamiltonian or Liouvillian, SAKE constructs local transport expansions about a reference model by combining forward-mode automatic differentiation with Duhamel transport theory. Automatic differentiation generates first-, second-, and third-order derivatives of the parameter-dependent Liouvillian, which are assembled into a pathway transport operator that maps the nonlinear response of a reference model onto neighboring systems. The framework is validated for a four-level excitonic dimer possessing an $su(2)\times su(2)$ symmetry by comparing second- and third-order transported pathway operators with exact projected transport matrices obtained from direct calculations. The third-order expansion accurately reproduces the projected transport operator and its associated pathway mixing. Beyond providing an efficient computational strategy, the transport operator reveals how coherent and dissipative perturbations redistribute amplitude among double-sided Feynman pathways, exposing mechanistic information that is not directly apparent from the nonlinear spectrum. SAKE thereby establishes a differentiable computational framework for nonlinear spectroscopy that supports efficient local parameter exploration, sensitivity analysis, and future inverse-design applications.

</details>


### [184] [Preparation of Large Fock States in Resonators with High Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20133)
*Lucas R. S. Santos,Ciro M. Diniz,Daniel Z. Rossatto,Celso J. Villas-Boas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合量子非破坏性(QND)光子数编码与量子振幅放大(QAA)的协议，可在高概率下宣告式制备大Fock态。该方案基于相干态腔模，通过量子相位估计将光子数信息编码到多量子比特寄存器，再用量子振幅放大提升目标结果的测量概率，在circuit-QED中可直接实现，可推广至其他具有QND光子数读取能力的玻色子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大Fock态是玻色量子信息与量子增强计量学的重要资源，但高激发数下以高概率制备它们仍具挑战性：确定性方法需要越来越复杂的控制，而测量基方法通常宣告概率较低。因此需要一种能够实现高概率宣告式生成大Fock态的新协议。

Method: 协议从腔模的相干态出发，利用量子相位估计(QPE)将光子数信息编码到多量子比特寄存器中，随后应用量子振幅放大(QAA)提升目标测量结果的概率，最后通过测量获得大Fock态。方案可直接在色散circuit-QED中实现，并可类比推广到具有QND光子数读出的其他玻色子平台（如cavity-QED）。此外，该协议可通过条件分束器操作作为生成双模NOON态的第一阶段。

Result: 使用最多八比特的寄存器，可实现数百个激发的Fock态的高概率（近确定性）制备。

Conclusion: 该协议结合QND光子数编码与量子振幅放大，克服了传统测量基方法宣告概率低的缺陷，为大Fock态的高概率制备提供了可行方案，并可作为生成双模NOON态的扩展基础。

Abstract: Large Fock states are important resources for bosonic quantum information and quantum-enhanced metrology, but preparing them with high probability at large excitation numbers remains challenging, as deterministic methods become increasingly control-intensive, while measurement-based approaches typically suffer from low heralding probabilities. Here we propose a protocol that combines quantum nondemolition photon-number encoding with quantum amplitude amplification to enable high-probability heralded generation of large Fock states. Starting from a cavity mode prepared in a coherent state, Quantum Phase Estimation encodes photon-number information into a multi-qubit register, while Quantum Amplitude Amplification boosts the probability of a desired target outcome before measurement. The scheme has an immediate implementation in dispersive circuit-QED, but can be analogously adapted to other bosonic platforms with QND photon-number readout, such as cavity-QED. With a register of up to eight qubits, near-deterministic preparation of Fock states with hundreds of excitations is possible. We also show that the protocol can serve as the first stage of an extension toward generating a two-mode NOON state via a conditional beam-splitter operation.

</details>


### [185] [Vacuum viscosity and relativistic inertia: Motion of a massive object with charged internal degrees of freedom interacting with a classical field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20140)
*Jen-Tsung Hsiang,Bei-Lok Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一个基本问题：具有带电内部自由度的运动大质量物体与自由无界经典标量场在零温下相互作用时是否会受到粘性力（摩擦力）。非相对论计算给出"是"的答案，但相对论协变计算给出"否"的答案。作者定位了非相对论框架的缺陷所在，指出这一潜在的冲突在物理上富有启发性，并在加入狭义相对论原则后丰富了牛顿第一、第二定律的内容，同时给出了获得正确非相对论答案的规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者受两类自70年代以来被研究的问题驱动：宇宙学粒子产生及其更易处理的类比——动力学卡西米尔效应，以及中性原子沿介质表面运动所经历的量子摩擦。被产生粒子的反作用效应（如使宇宙膨胀各向同性化或使运动镜减速）可通过量子场涨落产生的真空粘性来理解。作者想追溯这一效应的起源：运动物体是否普遍承受粘性力，即是否来源于这类真空摩擦效应。

Method: 采用光机力学（optomechanics）微观物理模型，可处理不等三体的 χ-φ-M 相互作用（内部自由度χ、自由无界经典场φ、大质量物体M）。首先进行非相对论计算（对原子物理而言似乎完全合理），然后进行相对论协变计算，对比两者结果并定位非相对论框架的缺陷。

Result: 非相对论计算给出的答案为"是"（存在粘性力），但相对论协变计算给出的答案为"否"（不存在粘性力）。两者结论冲突，且该冲突的解决在技术上不平凡。

Conclusion: 这一潜在但真实的冲突的解决在物理上极具启发性：当狭义相对论原则被强制实施时，牛顿第一和第二定律获得了更丰富的内容；同时给出了如何获得正确非相对论答案的规则。这说明在讨论运动物体受真空涨落反馈时，必须谨慎处理非相对论近似的适用范围。

Abstract: Our present investigation into a rather rudimentary problem is motivated by two classes of problems studied since the 70's, cosmological particle creation and its more accessible analog, the dynamical Casimir effect on the one hand, and quantum friction a neutral atom moving along a dielectric surface would experience, on the other. The backreaction effects of produced particles being able to isotropize the expansion of the universe, or to slow down the moving mirror can be understood via the concept of vacuum viscosity arising from fluctuations of the quantum field. We want to track down the origin of this effect by asking the question whether a moving massive $M$ object with a charged internal degrees of freedom $χ$ interacting with a free unbounded classical field $φ$ at zero temperature would experience a viscous force, similar to the said precedents. Adopting a microphysics model for optomechanics which can treat the unequal tripartite $χ$-$φ$-$M$ interactions, we first perform a nonrelativistic calculation, which seems perfectly legitimate considering the needs of atomic physics, and found the answer to be yes, but a relativistic covariant calculation says no. We identify where the nonrelativistic framework is defective. The resolution of this latent yet real conflict is technically nontrivial but physically quite inspirational. It results in added enriched contents to Newton's first and second laws when the principles of special relativity are enforced, and rules to follow to get the correct nonrelativistic answer

</details>


### [186] [Proper Learning of Shallow All-to-All Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20162)
*Steven Kordonowy,Jacob Watkins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究浅层量子电路的学习问题，提出了一种基于迭代局部门反转的元算法框架，可学习广泛的电路类别。将该框架应用于随机全连通两局域电路，通过光锥增长分析发现该系综在深度 d* ~ log₂ n + log₂log₂ n 处存在尖锐的可学习性转变，并对依赖电路学习难度的量子密码方案具有启示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在给定电路查询访问和门布局知识的前提下，学习特定的量子门并生成操作等价的电路是量子计算中的基础问题。近期关于学习Haar随机砖墙电路的工作激发了更广泛电路类别的学习算法研究，同时其与量子密码学的关联使得理解电路可学习性的边界具有重要意义。

Method: 提出一个元算法框架，核心策略是在电路前端和后端进行迭代的局部门反转（iterative local gate inversions），逐步消除未知门的效应，从而重建整个电路。基于此框架分析随机全连通（all-to-all）两局域（two-local）电路的学习复杂度，并通过光锥增长（lightcone growth）分析推导可学习性转变的深度阈值。

Result: 通过解析与数值分析表明，随机全连通两局域电路在深度 d* ~ log₂ n + log₂log₂ n 处经历尖锐（sharp）的可学习性转变，其中 n 为量子比特数。该阈值基于光锥增长的分析得出，为电路可学习性的临界深度提供了明确刻画。

Conclusion: 该元算法框架为学习更广泛的量子电路类别提供了统一方法，所发现的可学习性转变对近期基于电路学习难度的量子密码学方案（如量子加密原语）具有重要含义，同时本文与密码设置之间存在关键差异，为未来研究指出了方向。

Abstract: This work considers a variation on the problem of learning shallow quantum circuits. Given query access to the circuit, as well as knowledge of its gate layout, we consider the task of learning the specific gates used in the circuit, producing an operationally-equivalent circuit matching this structure. Building on recent work for learning Haar random brickwork circuits, we identify a meta-algorithmic framework for learning broad classes of circuits based on iterative local gate inversions at the front and back of the circuit. We apply these techniques to study random, all-to-all, two-local circuits, and provide analytical and numerical evidence that this ensemble undergoes a sharp learnability transition at depth $d^* \sim \log_2 n + \log_2\log_2 n$ in the large size limit, based on an analysis of lightcone growth. These results have implications for recently proposed quantum cryptographic schemes based on the difficulty of circuit learning, though there are important distinctions with respect to our setting that suggest avenues for future study.

</details>


### [187] [Architecture and Compilation Co-Design for High-Rate Quantum Product Codes on Neutral Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20164)
*Adrian Liu,Wan-Hsuan Lin,Daniel Bochen Tan,Qian Xu,Jason Cong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: ONEX是一个针对量子低密度奇偶校验码（qLDPC）在中性原子阵列上执行的编译框架，通过将复杂的2D物理执行规划分解为独立的1D子问题，并利用SMT编码生成深度最优的执行方案，实现3.7x-6.1x和29.8x-42.1x的时钟速率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现实用规模的容错量子计算需要高编码率的量子纠错码。qLDPC码是极具潜力的候选方案，尤其在动态长程连接的中性原子阵列上。然而，为QEC合成有效且高效的物理执行计划被证明是一个极难的组合优化问题，构成关键编译瓶颈，且随码规模增大而恶化。

Method: 1) 将复杂2D执行规划分解为独立1D子问题；2) 使用可满足性模理论（SMT）编码形式化1D执行计划，产生可证明深度最优的解；3) 构建多阶段编译流水线，包含随时优化（anytime optimization）、移动压缩和迭代反馈；4) 在超图乘积码上评估，并扩展到分区（zoned）布局和提升乘积码族。

Result: 在超图乘积（HGP）码映射到中性原子阵列的应用中，ONEX相比构造性1D算法和通用2D编译器，分别实现3.7x-6.1x和29.8x-42.1x的时钟速率提升，并有效扩展到2500数据量子比特的码规模。

Conclusion: ONEX通过维度约简策略有效克服了qLDPC码物理执行规划的编译复杂性，实现了深度最优的1D解决方案和高效的编译流水线。该框架不仅适用于HGP码，还通过分区布局和LP码族的例子展示了广泛的适用性，为大规模容错量子计算编译提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Achieving fault-tolerant quantum computing at a practical scale demands quantum error correction (QEC) codes with high encoding rates. Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes emerge as a promising candidate, especially given the rise of neutral atom arrays that provide dynamic long-range connectivity via atom movements. In general, synthesizing valid and efficient physical execution plans for QEC is a provably hard combinatorial problem, forming a critical compilation bottleneck that worsens as code sizes grow. To overcome this complexity, we focus on an important product family of qLDPC codes with dimension-reduction properties, and propose ONEX. This framework decomposes complex 2D physical execution planning into independent 1D subproblems, each solved to optimal execution depth within practical compilation time. First, we formulate the 1D execution plan with an explicit satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) encoding. This protocol produces provably depth-optimal solutions with substantial duration reduction. Second, we develop a multi-stage compilation pipeline featuring anytime optimization, movement compaction, and iterative feedback. This pipeline maintains practical wall-clock times while providing progressive refinement and on-demand retrieval of quality solutions. Third, we evaluate ONEX in the application of hypergraph product (HGP) code memory mapped onto neutral atom arrays, achieving 3.7x to 6.1x and 29.8x to 42.1x higher clock rates than the constructive 1D algorithm and the general 2D compiler, respectively, while scaling efficiently to codes with 2,500 data qubits. Finally, we extend ONEX to zoned layouts, revealing architectural insights into the associated trade-offs, and demonstrate its applicability to the broader lifted-product (LP) code family through a representative example.

</details>


### [188] [Large Scale Entanglement Structure Detection in 100-Qubit Systems via Local Joint Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20170)
*Rui Li,Yuhang Wang,Chunxiao Du,Shikun Zhang,Zheng Qin,Wenxiu Li,Hao Zhang,Zhisong Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的量子多体纠缠结构识别框架，通过选取满足边界匹配条件的局域Pauli基，在单一测量配置下即可读取整个量子链，测量开销与系统尺寸无关。在含有噪声的模拟中，该协议对多达100量子比特的系统，在30个候选分区中分类GHZ、W和簇聚类纠缠结构，平均准确率超过95%；并在超导量子处理器上对多达13量子比特的系统进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别多体量子态的纠缠结构（即体系如何划分为非纠缠块）是量子信息科学的核心任务，但传统量子层析方法随系统尺寸呈指数级增长，难以扩展到大规模系统。

Method: 提出一种可扩展框架，从局域关联指纹直接识别大规模纠缠结构。通过选择满足边界匹配条件p_1=p_R的代表性局域Pauli基，整个链可在单一测量配置下完成读出，使测量开销不依赖系统规模。该方法结合了噪声模拟与超导量子处理器上的实验验证。

Result: 含有噪声的模拟中，单基协议在30个候选分区中对GHZ、W和簇聚结构分类，高达100量子比特系统的平均准确率超95%。硬件实验中，在超导量子处理器上可靠分类多达13量子比特的块结构，更大规模时因噪声和电路深度导致的退化现象出现。

Conclusion: 通过明确映射这些失效模式，研究划定了硬件级可扩展性的边界，并为在近期量子设备上表征纠缠结构提供了具体策略。

Abstract: Identifying the entanglement structure of a many-body quantum state, namely how its constituents partition into unentangled blocks, is a central task in quantum information science, yet conventional tomography scales exponentially with system size. Here we introduce a scalable framework that recognizes large-scale entanglement structures directly from local correlation fingerprints. By choosing a representative local Pauli basis that satisfies a boundary-matching condition p_1 = p_R, the entire chain is read out in a single measurement configuration, keeping the measurement effort independent of system size. In noisy simulations, this single-basis protocol classifies GHZ-, W-, and cluster-type structures among 30 candidate partitions with a mean accuracy exceeding 95% for systems of up to 100 qubits. We further validate the protocol on a superconducting quantum processor, where it reliably classifies block structures for systems of up to 13 qubits before noise- and depth-induced degradation sets in at larger sizes. By mapping these failure modes explicitly, our results delineate the boundary of hardware-level scalability and point to a concrete strategy for characterizing entanglement structure on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [189] [Characterization of a damping channel as a mixture of amplitude damping and anti-damping channels of different parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20178)
*Vijay Pathak,R. Srikanth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构造更广泛相位协变动力学的框架，通过以不等衰减参数和时变混合概率混合振幅阻尼与反阻尼通道，实现对收缩和位移的独立控制，可在非幺正与幺正动力学之间连续调节，并产生GAD通道所不具备的有效退相干贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统中的非幺正噪声是能量交换的基本特征，但标准模型（如广义振幅阻尼通道）对这些特性的控制能力有限。需要一种更灵活的框架来工程化相位协变动力学，以实现对收缩、平移和退相干等特征的独立调控。

Method: 通过混合振幅阻尼和反振幅阻尼通道，采用不等衰减参数和时变混合概率，构建新的相位协变动力学框架。利用P-可分性的一般定理，在适当选择的希尔伯特空间基下进行评价，简化了幺正和非幺正情形的条件。

Result: 该框架能够捕捉CP可分、P可分但非CP可分、以及非P可分等多种动力学行为。适当的混合可以减小与理想无噪声演化的偏差，且这种改进在将动力学调至幺正区域后仍然保持。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了超越标准热模型的开放系统动力学工程化方法，对噪声控制和量子信息处理具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Non-unital noise is a fundamental feature of open quantum systems, governing energy exchange and inducing state translations on the Bloch sphere. While such translations can be useful for tasks such as state preparation and channel capacity, unital dynamics where no translation occurs are often preferred in quantum information processing, particularly for error correction. Phase-covariant dynamics provide a general framework encompassing dissipation, excitation, and dephasing processes in qubit systems; however, commonly used models such as the generalized amplitude damping (GAD) channel offer only limited control over these features. In this work, we present a constructive framework for generating a broader class of phase-covariant dynamics by mixing amplitude-damping and anti-damping channels with unequal decay parameters and time-dependent mixing probabilities. This approach enables independent control over contraction and translation, allows continuous tuning between non-unital and unital regimes, and yields an effective dephasing contribution absent in GAD. To characterize these dynamics, we employ the general theorem for P-divisibility and show that it can be used constructively by evaluating it in suitably chosen Hilbert space bases, leading to simplified conditions for both unital and non-unital cases. The framework captures a wide range of behaviors, including CP-divisible, P-divisible but not CP-divisible, and non-P-divisible dynamics. We further show that appropriate mixing can reduce the deviation from the ideal noiseless evolution and that such improvement persists even after tuning the dynamics to the unital regime. Our results provide a flexible approach to engineering open system dynamics beyond standard thermal models, with potential applications in noise control and quantum information processing.

</details>


### [190] [Hybrid Qubit-Rotor Quantum Systems: Clifford Structure, Universal Control, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20227)
*Dengyao Luo,Arvin Kushwaha,Mastawal Tirfe,Bojko N. Bakalov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统地发展了由n个量子比特与r个U(1)量子转子组成的混合寄存器上的Clifford理论，给出了混合相空间费米子/玻色子型Weyl关系的所有自同构分类与显式有限Clifford电路实现，并证明了该结构在加入转子余弦势和一个固定条件相位后可在强算子拓扑下实现全局普适量子控制。该理论进一步应用于规范协变物质跳跃、转子相位估计和转子动量码上的有限傅里叶变换三个场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子转子（U(1) rotor）由周期角度与整数共轭动量构成，广泛存在于分子转动、超导相位-电荷电路和紧凑规范场中。将转子与量子比特耦合形成的混合寄存器，其控制结构既不同于振子-量子比特系统，也不同于qudit系统，缺乏系统的Clifford理论和普适控制结果，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者定义了n量子比特-r转子的混合相空间F2^{2n}×Z^r×T^r的Weyl交换关系，分类了所有保持该关系的自同构（即Clifford群），并为每个自同构给出显式有限Clifford电路。分类具有方向性特征：转子动量奇偶性可控制量子比特Pauli操作（属于Clifford群），而任何非零的量子比特控制的转子动量平移均为非Clifford操作。作者还推导了混合量子比特-转子耦合的正规形式和实现它们所需的基本混合门的精确最小数目。在本地Clifford操作基础上加入转子余弦势与一个固定的量子比特-转子条件相位后，可在强算子拓扑下实现满希尔伯特空间的普适控制。

Result: 三个应用结果：(1) 实现了规范协变物质跳跃的精确controlled-shift方案，该方案必然是非Clifford的；(2) 转子相位估计采用直接角度读取和探测优化，受动量支撑与能量约束；(3) 转子动量码上的有限傅里叶变换，其中d=2^s的单转子变换可编译为O(s)个动量选择与controlled-shift指令，每个跨寄存器傅里叶因子可由一个二次转子Clifford门实现。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套完整且实用的混合量子比特-转子系统的Clifford框架和普适控制定理，揭示了该类系统独特的"非继承性"控制结构，并展示了其在量子模拟（规范场理论）、量子计量（相位估计）和量子算法（动量码傅里叶变换）中的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: A $U(1)$ quantum rotor pairs a periodic angle with an integer-valued conjugate momentum, and occurs in molecular rotation, superconducting phase-charge circuits, and compact gauge fields. Coupling such a rotor coherently to qubits gives a hybrid register whose control structure is not inherited from either the oscillator-qubit or the qudit case. We develop a Clifford theory, together with a universal-control result, for registers of $n$ qubits and $r$ rotors. We classify all automorphisms of the hybrid phase space $\mathbb{F}_2^{2n}\times\mathbb{Z}^r\times\mathbb{T}^r$ that preserve the Weyl commutation relations, and give an explicit finite Clifford circuit for each one. The classification is directional: rotor momentum parity may control qubit Pauli operations within the Clifford group, while every nonzero qubit-controlled rotor momentum shift is non-Clifford. It also yields normal forms for the mixed qubit-rotor couplings and the exact minimum number of elementary mixed gates needed to synthesize them. Adding a rotor cosine potential and one fixed qubit-rotor conditional phase to the local Clifford operations gives universal control on the full Hilbert space in the strong operator topology. We then apply this structure in three settings: an exact controlled-shift realization of gauge-covariant matter hopping, which is necessarily non-Clifford; rotor phase estimation with direct angle readout and probe optimization under momentum-support and energy constraints; and finite Fourier transforms on rotor momentum codes, where the one-rotor transform for $d=2^s$ compiles into $O(s)$ momentum-selective and controlled-shift instructions and each cross-register Fourier factor is implemented by one quadratic rotor Clifford gate.

</details>


### [191] [Jamming Extensions of Quantum Correlations Lead to Hidden Superluminal Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20247)
*Ravishankar Ramanathan,Xie Sicheng,Michał Eckstein,Paweł Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文全面刻画了与相对论因果性(RC)兼容的关联集合，证明在量子态约束下，任何非平凡的jamming（无论是态无关还是态相关）必然导致隐藏的超光速信号传递。作为应用，本文构造了能够突破设备无关(DI)比特承诺和秘密共享协议的RC攻击，表明在相对论性敌手面前，仅凭输入输出统计量不足以保证安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论因果性允许jamming现象——超光速因果影响但不产生超光速信号传递。已有协议通常假设敌手只受无信号(no-signaling)约束，但更弱的相对论因果性约束可能导致更强的敌手能力。理解RC关联的完全特征及其对量子协议安全性的影响至关重要。

Method: 通过扩展PRL 104, 140404中的算子框架，对与相对论因果性兼容的关联集合进行完整刻画。在量子态约束下，分别分析态无关和态相关的jamming情形，证明任何非平凡jamming必然导致隐藏的超光速信号传递。并基于该表征构建显式的RC攻击方案来检验DI密码原语的安全性。

Result: 证明在量子态约束下，任何非平凡jamming（无论是态无关还是态相关）必然导致隐藏的超光速信号传递，因此唯一一致的可能性是标准量子关联（无jamming）或完全RC关联集合，中间态jamming扩展会导致信号传递。同时构造了能打破DI比特承诺和DI秘密共享的显式RC攻击，即使这些协议对无信号敌手是安全的。

Conclusion: 安全性的保证需要考虑行为与测量事件时空位置的联合规范，仅凭输入输出统计量不足以在相对论性敌手面前确保协议安全性。

Abstract: Relativistic causality in certain spacetime configurations permits jamming - superluminal causal influences that nevertheless do not enable superluminal signaling. We fully characterize the correlations compatible with relativistic causality (RC) by extending the operator framework of PRL 104, 140404. When the underlying state is required to be quantum, we prove that any nontrivial jamming - whether state-independent or state-dependent - necessarily leads to hidden superluminal signaling. Thus, the only consistent possibilities are standard quantum correlations without jamming or the full relativistically causal correlation set, with intermediate jamming extensions of quantum correlations leading to signaling. As an application of our characterization, we investigate the security of device-independent (DI) cryptographic primitives against adversaries constrained only by relativistic causality. We construct explicit RC attacks that break DI bit commitment and DI secret sharing in jamming geometries, even when these protocols remain secure against no-signaling adversaries. Our results show that security against relativistic adversaries requires behaviors to be specified together with the spacetime locations of their measurement events; input-output statistics alone are insufficient.

</details>


### [192] [Logarithmic depth compression of Heisenberg Hamiltonian simulation by fan-out parallelization, with built-in error detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20250)
*Artemiy Burov,Clément Javerzac*

Main category: quant-ph
