TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于fan-out的gadget编译器，以电路宽度换取深度，用于Heisenberg型NMR哈密顿量的自旋系统量子模拟。通过将每个逻辑自旋编码为按交互度缩放的重复码寄存器，所有同类型Pauli对交互可并行执行，并利用冗余寄存器提供后选择的错误检测。在heavy-hex超导和全连接离子阱目标平台上进行了固定协议资源对比，在高交互度中心节点系统中体积最优调度可将双比特深度减半、体积降低1.7倍（13自旋演示），深度缩减在最高度分子上可达2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ量子计算机受限于电路深度，而自旋系统的乘积公式模拟导致窄而深的电路。作者旨在通过fan-out架构的编译策略，将深度换取宽度，以在深度受限的硬件上获得更可行的量子模拟方案。

Method: 将每个逻辑自旋编码为小规模重复码寄存器（大小由交互度决定），使所有同类型Pauli交互在对数深度CNOT fan-out后并行执行；冗余寄存器提供无需额外算法开销的后选择错误检测。作者对两种编译方案（gadget并行与顺序电路）在heavy-hex超导和全连接离子阱两个硬件目标上进行了固定协议的资源对比，并以四甲基硅烷（13自旋星形系统）验证零场NMR光谱。

Result: 高交互度中心节点图上体积最优调度将双比特深度减半、体积降低1.7倍，heavy-hex上双比特门数也降低；全连接平台上深度缩减最高2.5倍但双比特门数在所有系统中均上升。增益随交互图度不均匀性增加，对稠密均匀图则退化为顺序电路最优。噪声模型下，gadget电路需在错误率改进1~1.5个数量级后配合后选择才能达到或超越顺序编译的性能。

Conclusion: fan-out-based gadget编译在深度受限硬件上对高交互度不均匀的自旋系统有利，但增益依赖于图结构，对均匀稠密图无优势。当前噪声水平下需配合错误检测后选择并等待错误率进一步降低，gadget方法才能展现实际优势。作者通过独立经典计算验证了光谱结果的正确性。

Abstract: Noisy intermediate-scale quantum computers are constrained by circuit depth, while product-formula simulation of spin systems leads to narrow and deep circuits. Here we introduce a fan-out-based gadget compiler that trades circuit depth for width in simulations of Heisenberg-type nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) Hamiltonians. Each logical spin is encoded into a small repetition-code register sized by its interaction degree, so that all pairwise interactions of a given Pauli type execute in parallel after a logarithmic-depth CNOT fan-out, and the redundant registers provide error detection for post-selection at no additional algorithmic overhead. The central result is a fixed-protocol resource comparison of the two compilations, transpiled to heavy-hex superconducting and all-to-all trapped-ion targets across a set of NMR spin systems. For interaction graphs with a high-degree hub the volume-optimal schedule halves the two-qubit depth and reduces the volume 1.7-fold for the 13-spin demonstration, which on heavy-hex also lowers the two-qubit gate count, and the depth reduction rises to 2.5-fold on all-to-all for the highest-degree molecule studied. On all-to-all the two-qubit gate count rises for every system, so the volume reduction is a benefit on depth-limited hardware. The gain grows with the degree inhomogeneity of the interaction graph and vanishes for dense uniform graphs, where the optimum is the sequential circuit. We simulate the zero-field NMR spectrum of tetramethylsilane, a 13-spin star system. Under a noise model scaled from a published present-day processor calibration, the shallower gadget circuits match or surpass the sequential compilation only after post-selection on their built-in error detection, once error rates improve by one to one and a half orders of magnitude. We verify the spectra against an independent classical computation.

</details>


### [193] [Parallel Quantum Advantage with Limited Adaptivity Requires Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20297)
*Qipeng Liu,Saachi Mutreja*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Aaronson和Ambainis提出的量子查询算法几乎处处经典模拟猜想在大规模并行量子查询情形下成立，并通过耦合定理将均匀预言机分布与稠密分布联系起来，进一步推广到有限前缀并行查询、混合算法以及常数轮自适应等更广泛的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson和Ambainis于2014年猜想量子查询算法可以在几乎所有的输入上被经典算法高效模拟，这一猜想若成立则暗示指数级量子加速仅在结构化的输入上才可能出现。此前Yamakawa和Zhandry展示了并行查询量子算法在采样问题上仍可实现指数级加速，因此验证并推进该猜想的适用范围是重要的理论问题。

Method: 作者首先证明了并行量子查询算法无法区分均匀预言机分布与"稠密分布"上的预言机，这是一个更强的论断。其核心技术创新是一个耦合定理，将均匀预言机分布与稠密分布上的预言机关联起来。基于此，进一步将方法推广到更广泛的场景：有界量子查询前缀加大规模并行查询的算法，以及先进行自适应经典查询再进行大规模并行量子查询的混合算法。最后以并行查询模拟定理为基础，推广到常数轮自适应量子算法。

Result: 证明了并行查询量子算法的高效几乎处处经典模拟定理；建立了核心耦合定理；并将模拟定理推广到了有界量子前缀+并行查询、混合算法（任意多项式次自适应经典查询+并行量子查询）以及常数轮自适应的量子算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为Aaronson-Ambainis猜想提供了新的实质性进展，在并行和常数轮自适应等重要情形下确认了量子算法的经典可模拟性，同时与Yamakawa-Zhandry采样问题的指数分离结果互补，深化了对经典量子查询复杂度边界的理解。

Abstract: Aaronson and Ambainis (Theory of Computing, 2014) conjectured that quantum query algorithms admit efficient almost-everywhere classical simulation: for any $T$-query quantum algorithm, its acceptance probability can be approximated on a $(1-δ)$ fraction of inputs, up to $ε$ additive error, using $\mathrm{poly}(T, 1/ε, 1/δ)$ classical queries. At a high level, the conjecture suggests that exponential quantum speedups are possible only on sufficiently structured inputs.
  In this work, we make progress on this conjecture by proving it for quantum algorithms that make massively parallel quantum queries. In contrast, Yamakawa and Zhandry (Journal of the ACM, 2024) showed that quantum algorithms restricted to parallel queries can still achieve exponential speedups over classical algorithms for sampling problems. We establish our simulation theorem by proving the stronger statement that parallel-query quantum algorithms cannot distinguish the uniform distribution over oracles from oracles drawn from so-called "dense distributions". Our main technical contribution is a coupling theorem that relates the uniform distribution over oracles to oracles drawn from dense distributions.
  We further extend this approach beyond the purely parallel setting, obtaining simulation theorems both for algorithms with a bounded quantum-query prefix followed by a massively parallel quantum-query stage, and for hybrid algorithms that make an arbitrary polynomial number of adaptive classical queries before the massively parallel quantum-query stage. Finally, using the parallel-query simulation theorem as a base case, we obtain simulation theorems for quantum algorithms with constant rounds of adaptivity.

</details>


### [194] [Group-theoretic treatment of strong light-matter coupling with an arbitrary number of excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20340)
*Antti Peltola,Olli Siltanen,Kimmo Luoma,Konstantinos S. Daskalakis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过利用量子发射体的组合结构，研究了具有任意激发数目的Tavis-Cummings模型，推导了其本征系统的结构特性，识别了实际尺寸系统中允许的辐射跃迁，并揭示了仅靠少数激发不能访问的新行为，同时为计算复杂度呈指数增长的类似系统提供了降低复杂度的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学微腔中的强光-物质相互作用产生极化激元态，但现有理论描述通常局限于单激发情形，无法捕捉多激发现象，限制了技术进一步发展的可能性。作者旨在突破这一局限。

Method: 利用量子发射体的组合结构，研究具有任意数目激发的Tavis-Cummings模型，推导其本征系统的结构与性质，并识别实际尺寸系统中允许的辐射跃迁。

Result: 揭示了少数激发体系中无法观察到的新行为，并提供了一种降低具有指数增长希尔伯特空间的类似系统计算复杂度的框架。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了极化激元系统的理论描述范围，从单激发扩展到任意激发数目，为多激发物理研究和技术应用提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Strong light-matter interactions in optical microcavities give rise to hybrid light-matter states known as polaritons. While actively used in modern technologies, theoretical descriptions of such systems are often restricted to the single-excitation case, limiting their ability to capture many-excitation physics and hindering further technological advancements. Here, by exploiting the combinatorial structure of quantum emitters, we investigate the Tavis-Cummings model with arbitrary number of excitations. We derive the structure and properties of its eigensystem and identify allowed radiative transitions in systems of realistic size scales. Our work reveals new behavior inaccessible to the few-excitation regime, while also providing a framework to reduce the computational complexity of similar systems with exponentially growing Hilbert spaces.

</details>
